Our goal is to synthesize these threads into a coherent framework that clarifies what each approximation explains, when it is expected to hold, and how apparently competing narratives relate or diverge. Together, these perspectives form a unified physical framework that views representation learning as a collective phenomenon in high-dimensional systems, bridging deterministic and stochastic regimes of gradient-based optimization.

</details>


### [106] [Learning Generative Dynamics for 3D Molecule Generation via Sequential Neuro-Symbolic Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09142)
*Zhuoran Li,Xu Sun,Chang Wen Chen,Wanyu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种将符号化化学规则融入三维分子扩散生成的框架，通过按阶段施加约束，提升生成分子的化学有效性与规则符合度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有扩散模型主要从数据中隐式学习原子分布，未将物理化学规则明确纳入生成过程，因此生成结果虽可能在统计上合理，却可能违反化学结构约束。本文旨在把科学先验融入模型训练与生成动态，减少无效分子。

Method: 框架依据分子生成扩散轨迹由粗到细的特点，先缓解整体几何冲突，再逐步细化局部结构以达到化学一致性。训练过程中按阶段施加符号化约束，并自适应调整约束权重，逐渐引导模型生成符合规则的分子几何结构；同时将领域知识转化为可解释的生成控制。

Result: 在分子基准测试和现实约束生成任务中，该方法持续提升了生成分子的化学有效性，并能遵循物理化学规则及用户指定的目标。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将可解释的符号规则与扩散生成过程按序结合，有望使三维分子生成更符合科学先验，并提高受约束生成的可靠性。

Abstract: Diffusion models have recently emerged as a powerful paradigm for generating 3D molecules. Despite showing remarkable promise, they learn atomic distributions implicitly from data, without explicitly encoding physicochemical rules into their generative dynamics. Consequently, generated samples can be statistically plausible yet chemically invalid. To address this problem, we propose Sequential Neuro-Symbolic Constrained Diffusion (SensDiff), a framework that integrates symbolic rules into generative modeling, enabling the model to internalize scientific priors during training. Central to SensDiff is the observation that the diffusion trajectory of 3D molecule generation exhibits a coarse-to-fine evolution: valid molecules arise by first mitigating macroscopic geometric conflicts and then refining structures toward microscopic chemical consistency through iterative denoising. Accordingly, SensDiff mirrors this dynamic by sequentially enforcing constraints with adaptive weighting during training, progressively steering generation toward valid molecular geometries. Moreover, SensDiff translates domain knowledge into interpretable generative controls, grounding opaque neural denoising in scientific principles. Experiments on molecular benchmarks and real-world constrained generation tasks confirm that SensDiff consistently improves chemical validity while adhering to physicochemical rules and user-specified targets. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/WanyuGroup/NeurIPS2026_SensDiff.

</details>


### [107] [Augmentron: Scalable Multi-View Visual Augmentation for Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09145)
*Tanay Tandon,Aseem Doriwala,Jade Choghari,Catherine Weaver,Pragna Mannam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该方法利用几何感知的场景重建与相机位姿渲染，生成跨时间、跨视角一致的训练数据，提升机器人策略在真实环境变化下的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仅依赖视觉变化有限的示范数据训练，机器人策略容易过度依赖与任务无关的环境特征。现有生成式增强方法虽能增加视觉多样性，但逐帧、逐视角处理成本高，也可能造成不同相机视角或时间点之间的不一致。

Method: 提出一种几何感知的数据增强流程：构建可复用的场景表示，并依据记录的相机位姿进行渲染，从而生成环境与工作空间的变化。增强过程中保留机器人、任务物体、动作和标注不变，以确保生成数据在时间和多视角上保持一致。

Result: 在三项实体操作任务中，以增强数据替换一半真实训练数据后，模型在三项任务的分布外干扰物体评测和分布外工作空间变化评测中均取得成功率提升。与其他增强方法比较时，该方法在三个评测条件下的汇总成功率均最高。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将几何一致的合成增强数据纳入训练流程，有助于提升机器人策略应对真实环境动态变化的泛化能力。

Abstract: Robot policies trained on visually narrow demonstrations can become sensitive to environmental features that are unrelated to the task. Existing generative augmentation methods can introduce visual diversity, but processing every frame and camera view independently is computationally expensive and may produce inconsistent observations across cameras and timestamps. We introduce Augmentron, a geometry-aware pipeline that generates reusable scene representations and renders them through recorded camera poses. This produces temporally and multi-view consistent environ- ment and workspace variations while preserving the robot, task objects, actions, and annotations. Across three physical manipulation tasks, replacing half of the real world training dataset with Augmentron data improves model success across three tasks for both in out-of-distribution distractor objects, and out-of-distribution workspace variation evaluations. In a separate comparison against alternate augmentation techniques, Augmentron achieves the highest pooled success in all three conditions. The results show that introducing augmented data to a data training recipe can help policies generalize to dynamic real-world settings.

</details>


### [108] [Beyond the Grid: Continuous Dictionary Pursuit for Interpretable Signal Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09163)
*Nick Janssen,Melanie Schaller,Bodo Rosenhahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出连续字典追踪方法，在连续参数空间中从多类可解释函数中逐步提取信号成分，降低离散字典造成的频谱泄漏与网格失配，并兼顾重建精度、稀疏性和物理可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度学习模型虽能取得较高精度，但内部机制难以解释；传统匹配追踪和稀疏编码较透明，却依赖离散字典。当信号频率不与字典原子对齐时，会产生频谱泄漏和网格失配。既有连续追踪方法通常也局限于单一解析函数族，难以处理结构多样的信号。

Method: 该方法建立由周期、趋势、瞬态和不连续结构组成的异质函数库，并在可微原子的连续参数流形上直接优化函数参数。算法采用贪心迭代策略，结合数据驱动先验与梯度优化逐个分离信号成分；对不连续波形则通过逐步调整频谱尖锐度，使其能够参与梯度优化。为控制原子数量，算法根据预设原子预算和残差误差收敛情况停止。

Result: 在合成与真实数据实验中，对于函数库包含的原子类型，该方法消除了字典网格离散误差；对可由函数库表达的平稳合成信号，生成参数恢复误差可控制在百分之一以内。在稀疏场景下，其稀疏性与准确率的综合权衡优于传统方法、已有连续追踪方法和神经网络基线。此外，将提取出的原子用作序列模型的结构化课程学习目标，在多数评测数据集上提升了预测准确率。

Conclusion: 连续字典追踪为信号分解提供了一种透明且可解释的替代方案：它在保证较高重建精度的同时，直接给出具有物理含义的信号成分及其参数，并可进一步辅助序列模型训练。其效果依赖于函数库对目标信号结构的覆盖程度。

Abstract: Deep learning models achieve high accuracy in signal processing but remain difficult to interpret. Classical matching pursuit and sparse coding offer better transparency but suffer from spectral leakage and grid mismatch when signal frequencies do not align with discrete dictionary atoms. We introduce Continuous Dictionary Pursuit (CDP), a framework for interpretable signal decomposition that learns the parameters of closed-form functions directly from data. CDP optimizes over a continuous manifold of differentiable atoms and, unlike prior continuous pursuit methods that operate on a single analytic family, searches a heterogeneous library spanning periodic, trend, transient, and discontinuous structures. The algorithm employs a greedy iterative strategy using data-driven priors and gradient-based optimization to isolate individual signal components, with discontinuous waveforms made differentiable through spectral sharpness annealing. To ensure a sparse atom set for reconstruction, the framework utilizes a stopping criterion based on a predefined atom budget and residual error convergence. Evaluations on synthetic and real-world datasets show that CDP removes the grid discretization error for atom families contained in its library, recovers generative parameters to within $1\%$ on stationary synthetic signals expressible in that library, and achieves the best sparsity-accuracy trade-off among classical, continuous-pursuit, and neural baselines in the sparse regime. Beyond decomposition, we demonstrate that the extracted atoms can serve as structured curriculum targets for training sequence models, improving forecasting accuracy over standard end-to-end training on most evaluated datasets. The method offers a glass-box alternative for signal decomposition by providing high reconstruction fidelity alongside direct physical interpretability.

</details>


### [109] [TOFS: Feature Selection with Swap Supervision for Semantic Communication over Fading Channels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09173)
*Zhiyi Zhu,Eiji Takimoto,Patrick Finnerty,Chikara Ohta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向任务的特征选择方法，根据接收端分类损失评估特征优先级，在相同传输特征数和平均发射功率下提升图像分类准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在资源受限且信道状况不佳时，语义通信需要优先传输对接收端任务有用的特征。单个特征的价值会受到其他已传输特征及信道失真的影响，因此仅依据特征自身的重要性进行筛选，可能无法充分优化最终分类性能。

Method: 该方法通过替换特征并比较接收端分类损失，评估特征选择对分类效果的影响。研究使用独立传输样本验证损失差异，并将其转化为特征两两排序监督信号，以训练特征选择网络。训练完成后，网络只需一次前向计算即可预测特征优先级。

Result: 在十类图像分类数据集上的仿真结果显示，在传输特征数量和平均发射功率相同的条件下，该方法优于基于重要性的特征选择基线。在瑞利衰落和高斯噪声条件下，其分类准确率为百分之八十四，对比先进方法的百分之六十八。

Conclusion: 该研究将接收端任务损失直接用于特征优先级学习，使选择过程能够考虑特征之间的相互作用及无线信道失真。仿真结果表明，这种面向任务的特征选择有助于提升受限通信条件下的分类性能。

Abstract: Task-oriented semantic communication transmits task-relevant features to support receiver inference under limited communication resources and adverse channel conditions. For image classification, the usefulness of a feature depends on the other transmitted features and the distortion introduced by the wireless channel. Effective feature prioritization should therefore account for classification performance after transmission of the selected features. This paper proposes a Task-Oriented Feature Selection (\textit{TOFS}) framework and method, which evaluates feature replacements using receiver classification loss. Loss differences confirmed under independent transmission samples provide pairwise ranking supervision for a selector network. The trained selector predicts priorities in a single forward pass. CIFAR-10 simulations demonstrate improved classification accuracy over an importance-based feature selection baseline at the same transmitted feature count and average transmit power. The proposed \textit{TOFS} framework shows 84\% accuracy performance under Rayleigh fading and Gaussian noise, compared with the 68\% performance of a SOTA method.

</details>


### [110] [SIFR: Stable Signed Flow Reinforcement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09175)
*Runlong Liao,Xingqian Xu,Lizhang Chen,Baiyu Su,Yufan Huang,Zhonghao Wang,Akash Gokul,Ming-Yu Liu,Qiang Liu,Yogesh Balaji*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出稳定有符号流强化方法，通过有符号优势信号和自适应漂移约束，提升流模型奖励微调的稳定性与长期效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 流模型的奖励微调需要同时提高高奖励样本概率、降低低奖励样本概率，并在多轮迭代中保持稳定。现有迭代式奖励微调主要采用正权重，缺少明确的负向调整信号；反复重新加权还会使生成分布逐渐集中，最终导致模型崩溃。

Method: 该方法以有符号优势替代单纯的奖励权重，为低于基准的样本提供明确的抑制信号；同时限制模型相对参考模型的速度场漂移，以稳定更新。它通过拉格朗日乘子自适应调节正则化强度，使漂移控制符合预设预算。论文还将既有流模型强化学习方法纳入统一框架，指出它们隐式最小化有符号损失，主要差异在于正则化方式。

Result: 该方法在中型与大型图像生成模型上得到验证，并扩展至六百四十亿参数模型。在涵盖偏好型和规则型奖励的十二组模型—奖励组合中，其表现均达到或超过已有方法，且更快达到对照方法的峰值奖励；在长期训练中仍能持续改进，而对照方法往往停滞或发生崩溃。

Conclusion: 研究表明，将明确的正负向优化信号与受控的参考模型漂移相结合，有助于缓解奖励微调中的分布集中和训练不稳定问题，并支持更快、更持久地提升生成质量。

Abstract: Reward-based fine-tuning of flow models requires effectively promoting high-reward and suppressing low-reward samples, while maintaining training stability over many rounds of iterative updates. Iterative reward fine-tuning provides a simple starting point, but the positive reward weights give no explicit unfitting signal, and repeated reweighting concentrates the distribution and causes collapse. We propose Stable Signed Flow Reinforcement (SIFR), which replaces the reward weights by signed advantages to supply the missing unfitting signal, and constrains the velocity drift from a reference model to stabilize the updates, with a Lagrange multiplier that adapts the regularization strength to a prescribed drift budget. We further show that existing flow RL methods fall within this framework: they implicitly minimize the signed loss, differing in their regularization choices. We validate SIFR on SD3.5 Medium (2.5B) and FLUX.2 Klein (9B) and scale it to Cosmos 3 Super Text2Image (64B): across all 12 backbone--reward pairs with preference and rule-based rewards, it matches or exceeds existing methods, reaches their peak rewards much faster, and keeps improving over long training where they plateau or collapse.

</details>


### [111] [RoleWalk: training-free structural embeddings for directed graphs that transfer across graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09189)
*Maixent Chenebaux*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需训练的结构角色嵌入方法：通过固定半径内的短程惰性随机游走，为节点生成跨图可比较的表示，并在多项基准任务中兼具较高效果与较低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 结构角色关注节点在图中的功能位置，例如枢纽、桥梁或稠密核心成员，而非其邻居的具体身份。既有方法往往依赖单个图的训练，或使用整张图的尺度与归一化信息，导致相同局部结构在不同图中得到不同表示；此外，对有向边的利用也较有限。因此，需要一种局部、跨图一致且能处理方向信息的角色表示方法。

Method: 方法从每个节点出发进行短程惰性随机游走，并在每个步数记录两类信息：转移概率分布在一组固定频率上的特征函数，以及游走者到起点的距离分布。对于有向图，还加入先顺向、再逆向的游走，以刻画共享目标节点或共享来源节点的关系。表示仅依赖固定半径内的邻域，不需要训练或整图归一化。论文还说明，在不考虑曲率时，该表示可视为游走足迹的核均值嵌入；对较小概率进行剪枝时，则可提供逐节点误差界。

Result: 实验显示，该方法在五个分类基准上达到与领先角色嵌入方法相当的表现，但计算开销显著更低；在维基百科管理员身份数据中的真实有向角色任务上取得最高分。在其采用的安然网络对齐基准中，各噪声水平下的准确率均超过已有的有向图波方法。用于中心性预测跨图迁移时，它优于现有归纳式嵌入、人工特征及训练式图神经网络基线；单核处理器可在约二十秒至三分钟内嵌入百万节点图。

Conclusion: 该方法以局部随机游走统计构造跨图语义一致的节点角色表示，兼顾有向结构表达、理论可分析性与计算效率。摘要报告的实验结果表明，它在多类角色识别、网络对齐及跨图迁移任务中具有较强竞争力，适合大规模图场景。

Abstract: Structural role embeddings describe a node by its position in a graph - hub, bridge, periphery, member of a dense core - rather than by who its neighbours are. Existing methods either learn coordinates specific to one graph (struc2vec, Role2Vec, RESD) or read scales and normalisations from the whole graph (GraphWave, Digraphwave), so the same neighbourhood can receive different coordinates in two graphs; few read edge directions. We present RoleWalk, a training-free embedding that follows a short lazy random walk from each node and records, at each step, two distributions: that of the transition probabilities, through its characteristic function at a fixed grid of frequencies, and that of the walker's distance to its starting node. On directed graphs, walks that go forward then backward add who shares a node's targets or sources. The embedding depends only on a node's neighbourhood within a fixed radius, so its coordinates mean the same thing in every graph. We show that, without curvature, it is the kernel mean embedding of the walk's footprint, that it separates graphs that the Weisfeiler-Leman test cannot, and that pruning small probabilities comes with a per-node error certificate. Empirically, RoleWalk is competitive with the best role embeddings on five classification benchmarks at a fraction of their cost, has the highest scores on real directed roles in Wikipedia adminship, exceeds the published accuracy of Digraphwave at every noise level of its own Enron network-alignment benchmark, transfers centrality predictors to graphs four times larger better than existing inductive embeddings, hand-made features and a trained GIN, and embeds a million nodes in 20 s to 3 min on one CPU core. The code is open source (BSD license): https://github.com/kerighan/rolewalk

</details>


### [112] [Provable State Estimation with Recurrent Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09217)
*Vincent Andrieu,Pauline Bernard,Lucas Brivadis,Laurent Praly,Christian Wolf*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文从非线性观测器理论出发，为循环神经网络的状态估计提供收敛与鲁棒性保证，并在移动机器人任务中验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循环模型虽在状态估计中表现良好，但通常缺少关于误差收敛和鲁棒性的形式化保证。论文旨在弥合深度序列模型的经验成功与控制理论保证之间的差距。

Method: 将循环神经网络视为连续潜在动力系统的离散化，并对潜在流施加收缩条件。论文证明，该条件可保证真实物理状态能以平滑且单射的方式映射到潜在空间；通过增大一个缩放潜在向量场的标量增益，可使估计误差以指数速度收敛至任意小的邻域。此外，论文给出解码潜在状态所需的多层感知机复杂度定量界，并将保证扩展到离散时间残差循环网络。

Result: 理论分析表明，该架构可作为可调节的观测器，实现快速且可控的状态估计。在真实移动机器人任务中，具备收缩保证的模型优于普通门控循环单元，且训练稳定性显著更高。实验还发现，未施加约束的门控循环单元在该任务中也会收敛到收缩动力学。

Conclusion: 通过将收缩性纳入循环模型，可为状态估计建立明确的收敛保证，同时保留神经网络的表达能力。实验结果支持该理论，并提示收缩动力学可能是训练良好的序列模型自然形成的一种性质。

Abstract: Estimating the state of a dynamical system from observations is a crucial task in robotics and control. Despite their empirical success, recurrent models often lack formal guarantees on convergence and robustness. We bridge this gap by analyzing these architectures from the perspective of nonlinear observer theory. By framing RNNs as a time-discretization of continuous latent dynamical systems (Neural ODEs), we demonstrate that enforcing a contraction condition on the latent flow guarantees the existence of a smooth, injective map of the true physical state into the latent space. We prove that this architecture acts as a tunable observer: by increasing a single scalar gain that scales the latent vector field, the estimation error can be driven to an arbitrarily small neighborhood of zero at an exponentially fast rate. We further establish quantitative bounds on the MLP complexity required to decode this latent state, and extend our formal guarantees to the discrete-time residual RNN implementation. We validate the theory on a real mobile robotic task, where a provably contracting model outperforms a vanilla GRU with substantially more stable training. We also report that unconstrained GRUs converge to contracting dynamics on this task, suggesting that contraction is an emergent property of well-trained sequence models.

</details>


### [113] [Cross-Layer TopK: Dynamically Allocating Sparsity Across Layers in Transcoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09233)
*Anyuan Zhuo,Shaohua Zhang,Tianle Li,Yuan Lin,Diandian Gu,Jun Wang,Pinyan Lu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出跨层前Ｋ稀疏选择，为整个模型设定统一的激活特征预算，并在训练中动态分配到各层；实验显示，该方法以更少激活特征实现更好的重构与电路追踪效果，并可加速训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 跨层转码器能够将大语言模型的激活分解为可解释特征，并追踪特征如何跨层构成电路，但训练时需要同时处理所有层。各层的重构难度差异很大，尤其是中间层更难重构。既有方法要么通过惩罚项间接控制稀疏度，要么给每层分配相同数量的特征，因而难以兼顾稀疏性与重构质量，也无法合理利用各层的特征预算。

Method: 提出跨层前Ｋ稀疏选择，为整个模型设置统一的零范数预算，并在训练过程中根据需要动态分配各层的激活特征数量，而非固定每层预算。由于每个词元的总预算固定，该方法还可与稀疏解码结合，以减少计算和通信开销。论文同时考察了特征规模与稀疏度变化下的缩放规律。

Result: 在千问三八十亿参数基础模型上的比较中，该方法相较于跳跃修正线性单元方法，使用的激活特征减少百分之二十，同时取得更低的方差未解释比例，表明重构效果更好。更好的重构也改善了电路追踪，使所得电路更完整，并提升替换分数与完整性分数。结合稀疏解码后，每个训练步骤的速度提升至原来的二点九倍。

Conclusion: 跨层前Ｋ稀疏选择通过跨层动态分配统一稀疏预算，缓解了各层重构难度不均带来的资源分配问题，在特征效率、重构质量和电路追踪方面均优于所比较的方法，并支持高效训练与规模扩展。

Abstract: Cross-Layer Transcoders (CLTs) decompose the activations of large language models into human-interpretable features and trace how these features interact across layers to form circuits, making them a key tool for mechanistic interpretability. However, training CLTs effectively and efficiently is hard. Beyond the usual trade-off between sparsity and reconstruction quality, a CLT has to model all layers at once, which adds a further challenge. The common JumpReLU recipe controls sparsity only indirectly through a penalty, while the direct extension of TopK selects a fixed number of latents in every layer. Yet reconstruction difficulty differs widely across layers, and intermediate layers are much harder than others, so giving every layer the same budget is suboptimal. We propose Cross-Layer TopK, which sets a single L0 budget for the whole model and dynamically allocates it across layers during training. In a comparison with JumpReLU and other TopK variants on Qwen3-8B-Base, this gives lower FVU while using 20% fewer active latents than JumpReLU. The better reconstruction carries over to circuit tracing, yielding more complete circuits with higher replacement and completeness scores. The fixed per-token budget of Cross-Layer TopK also makes it easy to pair with sparse decoding, which cuts both computation and communication and gives a 2.9x speedup per training step. We further show that Cross-Layer TopK follows scaling laws in both latent size and sparsity. We have open-sourced the weights of our CLT at https://huggingface.co/ByteDance-Seed/Cross-Layer-TopK-CLT.

</details>


### [114] [Controllable Molecule Generation via Sparse Representation Editing: An Interpretability-Driven Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09235)
*Zhuoran Li,Xu Sun,Chang Wen Chen,Wanyu Lin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向大语言模型分子生成的可解释控制框架，通过操纵与化学概念对应的稀疏特征，实现对分子局部结构和整体性质的精细控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型有望用于分子生成，但其内部表征稠密且相互纠缠，难以准确控制指定位置的原子或官能团，也难以按目标范围调节分子的整体结构与理化性质。论文旨在将模型可解释性从事后分析推进为生成过程中的可操作控制。

Method: 提出稀疏表征编辑框架：学习一个过完备的稀疏特征空间，将大语言模型的表征解耦为一组紧凑、具有化学含义的潜在特征；随后直接编辑与目标概念对应的特征。该方法支持局部控制，即在指定位置生成目标原子或官能团；也支持全局控制，即将整体结构和理化性质调整到预设范围。

Result: 摘要称，实验表明该框架能够在现实场景的复杂约束下生成符合化学需求的分子，并为定量结构—性质分析提供机制层面的见解。摘要未提供具体数据或基线比较，因此无法进一步判断其性能提升幅度。

Conclusion: 该研究将可解释的稀疏特征用于分子生成控制，为精细约束大语言模型的分子生成提供了新思路。其主要价值在于兼顾局部结构指定、整体性质调节与机制分析；实际效果和适用范围仍需结合论文中的实验细节进一步评估。

Abstract: Controllable molecule generation is crucial for diverse scientific applications, such as drug discovery and materials design. While large language models (LLMs) show great promise, their dense and entangled representations impede precise control over the generation of molecules with bespoke substructures or properties. To address this, we propose Sparse Representation Editing (SpaRE), an interpretability-driven framework for fine-grained and precise control in LLM-based molecule generation. The crux of SpaRE is to learn an overcomplete sparse feature space that disentangles LLM representations into a compact set of latent features corresponding to chemically meaningful concepts. Within this space, we can directly manipulate these concept-aligned latent features to achieve (1) local control, by generating target atoms and functional groups at specified positions; and (2) global control, by customizing the overall structural and physicochemical properties within defined ranges. In this way, our framework advances interpretability from post-hoc analysis to actionable generative control. Experiments show that SpaRE can generate chemically desirable molecules under complex constraints in real-world scenarios, while offering mechanistic insights for quantitative structure-property analysis. The code and demo are available at https://github.com/WanyuGroup/ICML2026_SpaRE.

</details>


### [115] [Practical Non-Stationary Graph Gaussian Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09247)
*K. S. Sesh Kumar,Viacheslav Borovitskiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出方差耦合图高斯过程，用节点变异程度调节图上的信息传播，在保持节点方差差异的同时建模网络相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图高斯过程能够进行预测并量化不确定性，但异质网络中的节点变异程度含义不一：它可能是应沿网络传播的有效信号，也可能是应被抑制的不可靠测量，或两者兼有。现有建模需要在表达灵活性与参数复杂度之间权衡。

Method: 提出方差耦合高斯过程，以一个正值辅助信号表示节点变异程度，并通过单一可学习的耦合指数调节高变异节点对信息传播的增强或抑制；另以按簇扩展的形式支持同一图中存在不同耦合机制。论文还在特定结构假设下，刻画了基础模型族中唯一的平稳重缩放点：此时相关结构与平稳图核一致，但节点方差仍可不同。

Result: 在交通、空气质量和天气数据上的实验表明，该方法的预测与不确定性估计整体上具有竞争力；当网络需要多种耦合机制时，收益最明显。不过，部分数据上对方差进行重缩放后的平稳相关结构已足够，效果也取决于辅助信号和模型选择。

Conclusion: 方差耦合为异质图建模提供了一种兼顾灵活性与参数效率的方案，可依据节点变异程度自适应调节信息传播。其优势并非对所有数据都成立，实际表现需要结合辅助信号质量、网络特征及模型选择判断。

Abstract: Gaussian processes on graphs provide a principled framework for prediction and uncertainty estimation, but modelling heterogeneous networks often requires balancing flexibility against parameter complexity. Variability at a node may reflect useful signal that should propagate through the network, unreliable measurements whose influence should be suppressed, or a mixture of both. We introduce Variance-Coupled Gaussian Processes (VCGPs), which use a positive auxiliary signal to modulate graph interactions through a single learned coupling exponent. The exponent controls how strongly high-variability nodes propagate or attenuate information, while a cluster-wise extension accommodates different regimes within the same graph. Under stated structural assumptions, we characterize the unique stationary-rescaling point of the basic family, where correlations match those of a stationary graph kernel while node-dependent variances remain. Experiments on traffic, air-quality and weather datasets show competitive prediction and uncertainty estimation relative to stationary and per-node non-stationary baselines, with the largest gains on networks requiring multiple coupling regimes. Stationary correlations after variance rescaling remain sufficient on some data, and performance depends on the auxiliary signal and model selection.

</details>


### [116] [Topology as a Design Variable: Joint Architecture-Sizing Optimization for SRAM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09248)
*Can Wang,Junhao Ma,Yikai Wang,Shan Shen,Wei W. Xing*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一套开源静态随机存取存储器设计工具，可联合优化位单元拓扑与晶体管尺寸；实验显示，联合优化相较最强的单一拓扑基线，超体积最多提升百分之十七点三。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 静态随机存取存储器的功耗、性能、面积与稳定性取舍，既受位单元拓扑这一离散架构选择影响，也受晶体管尺寸这一连续变量影响，且两者相互作用。既有开源工具要么不提供优化，要么只针对固定的六管单元调整尺寸，无法联合探索拓扑与尺寸。

Method: 该工具包含三项核心设计：一是建立参数化编译器，可生成包含外围电路和复制位线自定时机制的六管、十管存储器宏，确保不同拓扑在相同仿真条件下比较；二是利用少量完整宏电路级仿真样本，训练能够预测不同拓扑下功耗、性能和面积表现的少样本替代模型；三是采用由粗到精的多目标优化流程，先全局探索离散拓扑选择，再局部优化连续晶体管尺寸。

Result: 在不同设计条件下的实验发现，拓扑选择的决策边界会随运行约束变化，并不存在固定不变的拓扑选择规则。跨越该边界进行联合优化，相较表现最强的单一拓扑基线，超体积最多提升百分之十七点三。

Conclusion: 研究表明，应将位单元拓扑与晶体管尺寸纳入统一的优化流程，而非预先固定拓扑后仅调整尺寸。该开源工具提供了可复现的存储器设计研究平台，并支持在不同运行约束下开展拓扑比较与联合优化。

Abstract: SRAM's power-performance-area-stability trade-off depends on bitcell topology, a discrete architectural choice that interacts non-separably with continuous transistor sizing. However, no open-source SRAM tool treats topology as an optimizable variable. OpenRAM generates SRAM layouts but performs no optimization, while OpenYield supports high-fidelity evaluation but restricts optimization to scalar sizing of a fixed 6T cell. OpenYieldV2 closes this gap through three integrated contributions. First, a fully parameterized compiler generates complete 6T and 10T SRAM macros with peripheral circuits and replica-bitline self-timing, enabling topology comparison under identical simulation conditions. Second, a few-shot TabPFN surrogate learns topology-dependent PPA responses with limited full-macro SPICE samples. Third, a coarse-to-refined multi-objective optimizer combines global exploration of discrete architectural choices with local refinement of continuous transistor sizes. Experiments across varying design conditions reveal a topology decision boundary that shifts with operating constraints rather than following a fixed design rule. Jointly optimizing across this boundary improves hypervolume by up to 17.3\% over the strongest single-topology baseline. OpenYieldV2 is open-source to support reproducible SRAM design research.

</details>


### [117] [Rethinking MoE Load Balancing as Feedback Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09252)
*Yikun Hou,Alp Yurtsever*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将专家混合模型的负载均衡视为反馈控制问题，并提出纯积分控制方法；在训练数据分布变化时，该方法比现有方法更快、更稳定地恢复专家负载平衡，且困惑度相近。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练数据混合比例会因课程阶段切换、持续预训练转向新领域或语料更新而改变，使路由器原先学到的专家负载分配失效。现有均衡方法主要在数据分布固定的情况下评估，难以说明其面对分布变化时的表现。

Method: 论文将各专家负载偏离公平份额的程度定义为控制误差，将路由偏置定义为控制输入，并据此构建比例、积分、微分控制器家族。辅助均衡损失可视为比例控制，无损失偏置更新则相当于对误差符号进行积分控制。分析表明，在带有一步延迟的路由器中，积分项既能消除持续偏差，也能平均负载测量的逐步波动；比例项和微分项则会把这种波动传递或放大到路由偏置中。因此，论文采用只有一个增益参数的纯积分控制方法。

Result: 实验包括从头训练每层含十六至六十四个专家的模型，并在训练中途切换数据混合比例；以及对已发布的专家混合模型进行新语料持续预训练。纯积分方法在分布变化前维持负载平衡，并在变化后比辅助均衡损失和无损失偏置更新更紧密地恢复平衡，同时保持相近的困惑度。

Conclusion: 将专家负载均衡建模为反馈控制，有助于解释既有方法的行为及其局限。纯积分控制能够兼顾消除长期负载偏差与抑制测量噪声，在训练数据分布非平稳的场景中表现出更稳健的均衡能力。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) training is often not stationary: the data mixture changes at curriculum boundaries, when a model is continually pretrained into a new domain, or when the corpus is refreshed. Routing is learned against the current mixture, so each such change disrupts the per-expert load the router had converged to, and existing balancing methods have only been evaluated on stationary data. We rethink load balancing as a feedback control problem, in which the deviation of each expert's load from its fair share is the control error and the per-expert routing bias is the control input, and define a family of controllers, the PID-Balancer, from the proportional, integral, and derivative terms of classical PID control. Two widely used methods belong to this family: the auxiliary balancing loss behaves like a proportional controller, and the loss-free bias update is an integral controller acting on the sign of the error. Reading the router as a static map with a one-step delay, we show that the integral term is the only one that both removes persistent imbalance and averages out the step-to-step fluctuation of the measured load, whereas the proportional and derivative terms pass or amplify that fluctuation into the routing bias. We therefore adopt PID (I), the pure-integral member of the family with a single gain. Two settings are evaluated: (i) training an OLMoE backbone from scratch with 16 to 64 experts per layer, through a mid-run mixture switch, and (ii) continual pretraining of the released OLMoE-1B-7B on a new corpus. PID (I) holds balance before the shift and restores it more tightly afterwards than either the auxiliary loss or loss-free balancing, at comparable perplexity.

</details>


### [118] [Diversity-Aware Client Selection for Communication-Efficient Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09278)
*Max Halleryd,Sima Sinaei,Viktor Valadi,Mina Alibeigi,Henrik Abrahamsson,Salman Toor*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文系统比较联邦学习中的三种客户端选择策略，发现每轮仅选取百分之十五的客户端即可显著降低通信开销；若选择过程兼顾参与多样性与均衡性，还能改善模型泛化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习需要在异质、非独立同分布的数据环境下减少通信开销，同时保持模型性能。客户端选择有望兼顾这两个目标，但其对数据多样性和代表性的影响尚未得到充分研究。

Method: 研究比较择优客户端选择、基于费舍尔信息的选择和基于中心化核对齐的选择策略。除准确率与收敛表现外，还使用基尼系数和库尔贝克—莱布勒散度衡量客户端参与的多样性与均衡性，并在非独立同分布条件下进行评估。

Result: 每轮仅让百分之十五的客户端参与训练，便能显著节省通信开销。与参与分布有偏的策略相比，能够促进多样、均衡贡献的选择策略可提升模型泛化能力。

Conclusion: 客户端选择不应只关注训练效率或单轮贡献，还应重视参与多样性与代表性。实验分析表明，面向多样性的客户端选择能够使通信效率与模型质量相辅相成，而非彼此牺牲。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training across distributed clients without sharing raw data, making it attractive for privacy-sensitive and bandwidth-constrained applications. A practical challenge in deploying FL is reducing communication overhead while maintaining model performance under heterogeneous, non-IID data distributions. Selecting which subset of clients participates in each training round offers a principled way to address both challenges simultaneously, yet its impact on data diversity and representativeness remains underexplored. In this paper, we investigate three client selection strategies, Power-of-Choice, Fisher information, and Centered Kernel Alignment, evaluating them not only by accuracy and convergence, but through the lens of participation diversity using the Gini coefficient and KL divergence. Under non-IID conditions, restricting participation to 15% of clients per round achieves significant communication savings, and when selection promotes diverse and balanced contributions, it also improves generalization over biased alternatives. This work provides a systematic and reproducible analysis demonstrating that diversity-focused client selection renders communication efficiency and model quality complementary rather than competing objectives.

</details>


### [119] [Safety-Focused Reinforcement Learning Using Risk-Sensitive Coupled Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09290)
*Jiabin Lin,Shana Moothedath,Vivek S. Borkar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向安全强化学习的双时间尺度耦合控制方法：先通过风险敏感控制保障安全，再优化平均成本，并给出算法收敛性保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在复杂动态环境中，控制系统既要获得良好性能，又必须避免进入危险状态。单纯优化平均成本可能忽视安全风险，因此论文研究如何将安全约束与性能优化有效结合。

Method: 论文将原问题转化为结合平均收益准则的风险敏感问题，并提出双时间尺度的Ｑ学习算法。较快时间尺度处理风险敏感控制，以降低进入不安全状态的风险；较慢时间尺度优化平均成本。论文还为算法提供了收敛性理论保证。

Result: 作者在网格世界和安全健身房平台的汽车目标任务中验证了该方法。摘要称实验验证了方法的性能，但未提供具体数值、基线比较或安全性指标，因此无法据此判断提升幅度。

Conclusion: 该研究通过风险敏感控制与平均成本优化的分层协同，为安全与性能并重的控制提供了一种方法，并以理论分析和任务验证支持其可行性。

Abstract: This paper investigates the coupled control problem within safety-focused reinforcement learning, addressing the essential challenge of achieving optimal performance while ensuring safety in systems operating within complex environments. We define safety as the requirement to prevent the system from reaching undesirable or hazardous states. We propose a coupled control approach that prioritizes safety by employing effective methods to prevent the system from entering unsafe states while optimizing the performance metric, defined as the average cost. We propose a novel problem transformation that reformulates it into a risk-sensitive problem combined with an average reward criterion. We present a two-timescale Q-learning algorithm where in the faster timescale, the algorithm solves a risk-sensitive control problem to ensure safety, while on a slower timescale, the algorithm focuses on average cost control. We provide theoretical guarantees for the convergence of our algorithms, establishing their significance in ensuring safe, optimal control in dynamic environments. We validated the performance of our approach using GridWorld and CarGoal task in Safety Gymnasium.

</details>


### [120] [A Stochastic Latent Variable Generalization of Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09302)
*Nikos Giakoumoglou,Paschalis Giakoumoglou,Tania Stathaki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出随机化联合嵌入预测架构，使掩码图像预测能够表示多种合理补全及其不确定性，同时保持或提升表征质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有联合嵌入预测方法虽设有用于条件化预测的辅助变量，但实际均以固定确定性标记承载该变量，因而把多种可能的补全压缩为单一预测，无法表达预测的不确定性与多样性。

Method: 提出随机化联合嵌入预测方法：训练时由上下文与目标共同推断随机潜变量的后验分布；推理时由上下文条件化的先验分布生成潜变量。该方法不使用解码器，也不进行像素重建。其确定性极限下的目标函数形式退化为既有确定性方法的损失，但属于对该目标的推广，而非对原模型的严格包含。

Result: 在架构与训练计划匹配的比较中，该方法优于确定性基线，并在匹配架构时超过另一种基线。随机性带来与误差相关的预测不确定性和多样化补全，而未牺牲表征质量；此外还能产生具有空间结构的不确定性图和语义多样的补全结果。

Conclusion: 将辅助变量建模为真正的随机潜变量，可以在维持甚至提升表征性能的同时，增强模型对不确定性和多种合理预测的表达能力。

Abstract: Joint-Embedding Predictive Architectures (JEPAs) predict the latent embedding of a target signal from a context signal, with an auxiliary variable z conditioning the predictor. Despite its prominent role in the original JEPA blueprint, the content of z has been carried by a fixed deterministic token in every prior instantiation, collapsing the genuinely multi-modal distribution over plausible completions to a point estimate. We present S-JEPA, which realises z as a proper stochastic latent variable for masked image prediction: a learned posterior infers z from context and target during training, while a learned context-conditioned prior approximates the distribution of plausible completions at inference, with no decoders and no reconstruction. In its deterministic limit the objective reduces to the I-JEPA loss in functional form, generalising that objective rather than strictly containing the I-JEPA model. Our central finding is that stochasticity adds capabilities, error-correlated predictive uncertainty and diverse completions, at no cost to representation quality: under matched architectures and schedules S-JEPA improves over the deterministic I-JEPA baseline and exceeds LeJEPA at matched architecture, while additionally producing spatially structured uncertainty maps and semantically diverse completions, neither of which the deterministic predictor produces.

</details>


### [121] [Stable probabilistic emulation of partially observed dynamical systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09317)
*Mario Massy,Sara Shamekh,Fabrizio Falasca,Matthieu Blanke*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种具有稳定性保证的随机数据驱动气候模拟器，可长期稳定地模拟多尺度系统，并准确预测外部强迫下均值和方差的变化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 气候系统具有多尺度、部分可观测等特征。粗粒化会遗漏快速变量，因此模拟器既要表示未解析过程的不确定性、再现长期统计特征，又要在长时间积分和外部扰动下保持稳定。现有生成模型虽有表达复杂随机动力学的能力，却缺乏相应的稳定性保证。

Method: 提出强迫耗散型随机插值框架，用于学习随机约化动力学。模型架构融入受湍流动力学启发的结构约束，以增强稳定性，同时保留对复杂概率动力学的表达能力。理论上，作者基于马尔可夫链稳定性理论建立福斯特—李雅普诺夫漂移条件，并证明模型具有几何遍历性，且在恒定外部强迫下保持稳定。

Result: 在用于代表部分观测多尺度湍流的两时间尺度洛伦兹-96系统上进行评估。模拟器在长时间积分及有限幅度外部强迫下均保持稳定，并能准确预测强迫响应中的均值与方差变化。

Conclusion: 该研究将结构化随机建模与稳定性理论结合，为部分观测多尺度系统提供了一种兼具长期稳定性和概率表达能力的数据驱动模拟方法。实验结果支持其用于预测气候类系统长期行为及外部强迫响应的潜力。

Abstract: Predicting the long-term behavior of partially observed dynamical systems such as the coupled atmosphere-ocean system is central to societal decision-making. Motivated by the success of deep learning emulators in weather forecasting, data-driven climate models represent a promising approach. However, climate emulation poses distinct challenges: models must account for unresolved fast variables introduced by unavoidable coarse-graining, reproduce long-term statistics, and remain stable over arbitrarily long rollouts and under external perturbations. These requirements motivate stochastic data-driven emulators, but modern generative models lack stability guarantees. We introduce forced-dissipative stochastic interpolants, a generative framework for learning stochastic reduced dynamics. Its architecture uses structural constraints inspired by turbulent dynamics, which provide stability by design while retaining the flexibility of stochastic interpolants to represent complex probabilistic dynamics. Building on the theory of Markov-chain stability, we establish a Foster--Lyapunov drift condition and prove geometric ergodicity for our emulator, together with stability under steady external forcing. We evaluate the framework on the two-timescale Lorenz-96 system, a prototype of partially observed multiscale turbulence. The resulting emulator remains stable over long integrations and under finite-amplitude forcing, with accurate prediction of forced responses of both the mean and variance.

</details>


### [122] [DiffusionRef-IMU: Guide-Anchored Residual Diffusion for Cross-Location Inertial Measurement Unit Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09323)
*M. M. Golam Hafiz,Safia Akter Simi,Md. Rakibul Islam,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种先预测目标位置惯性信号、再以残差扩散模型修正误差的两阶段方法，在受控跨位置重建中优于多个基线；但外部零样本迁移效果不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 穿戴式惯性测量设备在身体不同位置采集的信号存在差异。当目标位置的训练测量不可用时，这种跨位置变化会增加模型学习和信号重建的难度。研究旨在提升目标位置信号的重建质量，同时考察方法的计算开销、条件信息依赖及跨数据集迁移能力。

Method: 提出两阶段跨位置信号转换框架。第一阶段由确定性直接网络估计目标信号；第二阶段以该估计作为引导，仅对剩余的目标信号差异进行残差扩散精修，并采用确定性采样。研究在五个按受试者划分、且留出活动与位置组合的折上评估，并与容量匹配的确定性残差方法及流模型基线比较；还测试了仅使用源信号活动预测结果进行条件控制、不同采样步数的计算效率，以及在两个独立数据集上的零样本迁移表现。

Result: 采用十六步确定性采样时，平均归一化均方根误差为0.087538，优于直接网络的0.090262、确定性残差精修的0.088847和八步流模型的0.088417，并在五个折上均胜过各基线。四步采样的误差为0.087820，每个窗口耗时47.62毫秒，体现出较好的精度与计算效率折中。将真实活动标签替换为仅依据源信号得到的活动预测后，误差仅升至0.088190；该活动预测器的宏平均Ｆ一值为0.8473。零样本评估结果不一致：在一个外部数据集上略有优势，在另一个数据集上则逊于直接网络。

Conclusion: 以直接估计结果为锚点、针对残差进行扩散精修，能够稳定改善受控实验中的跨位置信号重建，且少量采样步数即可取得有竞争力的效率与精度。该方法对活动条件误差较为稳健，但外部零样本迁移尚不可靠，说明其泛化能力仍受数据集差异限制。

Abstract: Cross-location variation in wearable inertial measurement unit (IMU) signals complicates learning when measurements from a desired target body location are unavailable. This study proposes DiffusionRef-IMU, a two-stage cross-location translation framework in which a deterministic Direct network first estimates the target signal and a guide-anchored residual diffusion model refines only the remaining target discrepancy. The evaluation comprises five subject-disjoint activity-location composition-held-out folds, capacity-matched deterministic and flow-based residual baselines, source-only activity conditioning, computational ablation, and true zero-shot evaluation on two independent datasets. DiffusionRef-IMU with 16-step deterministic DDIM achieved a mean nRMSE of 0.087538 +/- 0.004922, compared with 0.090262 for Direct, 0.088847 for deterministic residual refinement, and 0.088417 for eight-step CompFlow, and outperformed each baseline in all five folds. Four-step DDIM retained a mean nRMSE of 0.087820 at 47.62 ms per window, providing a favourable quality-computation trade-off. Replacing ground-truth activity conditioning with a source-only activity predictor (macro-F1 0.8473) increased 16-step DiffusionRef nRMSE only to 0.088190. True zero-shot evaluation produced mixed results: DiffusionRef was marginally favourable on Grouvel2025 but remained worse than Direct on Saldaña2025. Overall, guide-anchored residual diffusion improves controlled cross-location reconstruction while revealing clear limits in zero-shot external transfer.

</details>


### [123] [Robust Deep Prototype Classifiers via Lipschitz Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09327)
*Adia Khalid Khan,Lukas Gehring,Benjamin Paaßen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出通过限制嵌入网络的局部变化幅度，将原型分类器在嵌入空间中的鲁棒性转化为输入空间中的鲁棒性；实验显示，此方法与原型分类头结合时表现出最强的经验鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒性与可解释性相互关联：模型若会因人眼难以察觉的扰动而改变预测，就难以符合人类对特征的理解。原型分类器通过比较输入与代表性样本作出决策，具有较强可解释性，并能依据分类间隔给出鲁棒性证书。然而，高维数据通常需要深层嵌入网络提升准确率，而不受约束的网络可能将极小的输入扰动放大为嵌入空间中的巨大变化，使分类器的鲁棒性证书无法适用于原始输入。

Method: 论文证明：若嵌入网络在某点附近满足局部利普希茨约束，且分类器在该点的嵌入表示附近具有一定鲁棒性，那么完整模型在输入空间中的鲁棒范围可由这两项性质共同确定。基于这一结果，作者提出一种通过采样训练来约束嵌入网络变化幅度的方法，并比较线性分类器、线性支持向量机和广义学习向量量化分类器，分别搭配普通或受利普希茨约束的嵌入网络。

Result: 在手写数字、自然图像和小型图像分类数据集上，作者使用快速梯度符号法、投影梯度下降法及卡尔利尼—瓦格纳攻击进行评估。结果表明，原型分类头与受利普希茨约束的嵌入网络结合时，整体鲁棒性最强；嵌入网络的约束在原型分类头本身优势之外，还带来了额外的鲁棒性收益。

Conclusion: 限制嵌入网络对输入扰动的放大效应，可以弥合原型分类器在嵌入空间中的鲁棒性与完整模型在输入空间中的鲁棒性之间的差距。该方法兼顾模型的可解释性与抗攻击能力。不过，利普希茨约束通过采样训练实现，而非由网络结构直接保证，因此论文报告的是经验鲁棒性，并非经过严格证明的鲁棒性证书。

Abstract: Robustness and interpretability are interrelated and desirable properties of machine learning models. A model that changes its prediction under imperceptible perturbations behaves inconsistently with human-recognizable features and, hence, is not interpretable. Prototype-based classifiers such as Generalized Learning Vector Quantization address both at once. They are interpretable by construction because they make the decision by comparing the input to the prototypical example; and their hypothesis margin yields a closed form robustness certificate. However, on high-dimensional data, these shallow models need deep embedding networks to achieve high accuracy, and this voids the robustness certificate. The margin is a statement about distances in the embedding space, and an unconstrained network can map an arbitrarily small input perturbation to arbitrarily large attacks in the embedding space. We close this gap with a theorem: if the embedding network is locally L-Lipschitz around a point, and the classifier is ε-robust around its embedding, then the full model is ε/L-robust in the input space. Based on this, we propose a Lipschitz training scheme to achieve L-Lipschitz properties for embedding networks. We evaluate three classification heads (linear, linear Support Vector Machine, and GLVQ) crossed with two embedding networks (standard and Lipschitz-constrained) on MNIST, CIFAR-10, and Imagenette-160, under FGS, PGD and C\&W attacks. The evaluation results show that combining a prototype-based head with a Lipschitz-constrained embedding provides the strongest robustness. This suggests that the Lipschitz constraint provides an additional robustness benefit in addition to the advantage provided by the prototype-based head. Since the Lipschitz constraint is enforced by sampling rather than by construction, the reported robustness is empirical rather than certified.

</details>


### [124] [Probably Safe Sim-to-Real Transfer with Lifetime Validity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09344)
*Rohan Tangri,Jan-Peter Calliess*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该方法利用生产环境中安全基线策略收集的数据，逐次检验仿真训练的强化学习策略，并为无限次策略更新提供持续有效的安全保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习策略从仿真迁移到真实环境时，部署新策略可能带来安全风险；而策略会不断更新，因此仅对单次部署提供安全保证并不足够。该研究旨在为受约束决策问题中的连续策略更新建立长期有效的安全认证机制。

Method: 在每次部署前，方法使用生产环境安全基线策略收集的轨迹，通过逐决策重要性采样和投注式置信序列构造随时有效的检验，以认证在仿真中训练的候选策略。各次检验分配可求和的失败概率，从而将安全保证扩展到无限序列的策略更新。仿真质量只影响生产数据的使用效率，不影响安全保证本身。

Result: 理论分析表明，该方法可在无限次策略更新过程中维持概率意义上的安全保证，并具有全生命周期有效性。三个仿真到真实环境的基准实验支持了理论结果。

Conclusion: 该方法为受约束强化学习策略的仿真到真实环境迁移提供了可重复、长期有效的部署前安全认证方案。其核心优势是安全保证不依赖仿真质量，而是依靠真实生产环境中安全基线策略收集的数据。

Abstract: We present Dejavu, a method that makes sim-to-real transfer of reinforcement learning policies in constrained Markov decision processes probably safe over an unbounded sequence of policy updates. An anytime valid test using per-decision importance sampling and a betting confidence sequence certifies each candidate trained in simulation before deployment using only trajectories collected by a safe baseline policy operating in production. Simulation quality does not affect the safety guarantee, only the production sample efficiency of the method. Finally, a summable per-test failure probability yields the lifetime valid property. Empirical results across three sim-to-real benchmarks support the theoretical work.

</details>


### [125] [Reliable Prerequisite-Aware Learning Path Recommendation under Uncertain Knowledge Structures: Long-Horizon Evaluation and Cross-Dataset Validation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09345)
*M. M. Golam Hafiz,A. K. M. Shahriar Emtiaz,Safia Akter Simi,Md. Rakibul Islam,M. F. Mridha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，下一学习资源预测准确、先修结构可靠性与长期学习路径质量是相互关联但不能互相替代的评价维度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习路径推荐依赖知识结构和先修关系，但这些结构可能存在不确定性。单步预测表现良好，也不代表模型能生成可靠的长期路径，因此需要同时评估即时排序、结构可靠性与路径行为。

Method: 研究提出一种面向可靠性的先修关系感知框架，考察即时资源排序、先修结构不确定性、不同约束语义、图结构鲁棒性及自回归路径生成。模型先在慕课立方X的课程数据上开发，再于冻结的课程留出集上确认评估，并使用君益数据集进行独立外部验证。主要任务以课程及其已有前缀为条件，不针对个体学习者；外部验证依赖课程层级信息，而非显式先修图，也不属于零样本迁移。

Result: 在慕课立方X确认评估中，极端梯度提升成对排序模型的即时排序表现最佳，平均倒数排名为0.9048；提出的先修图模型为0.7707。开发阶段的受控消融实验中，不使用图结构的条件即时排序最强，且预测高度依赖允许使用的标题与路径相似度信息。自回归评估揭示了单步排序无法体现的路径特性。在君益外部验证中，马尔可夫模型的平均倒数排名最高，为0.4453，先修图模型为0.3954；不同排序指标上的相对表现并不完全一致。

Conclusion: 不能仅凭下一资源预测准确率判断学习路径推荐系统是否可靠。评估应分别考察即时预测、先修结构可信度和长期路径质量，并结合结构扰动与跨数据集验证，以获得更完整的系统表现判断。

Abstract: Learning-path recommendation often depends on knowledge and prerequisite structures whose reliability is uncertain, while high accuracy in predicting the next resource does not necessarily establish reliable long-horizon path behaviour. This study develops a reliability-oriented prerequisite-aware framework that jointly examines immediate next-resource ranking, uncertain prerequisite structures, hard, soft and hybrid constraint semantics, graph robustness, and autoregressive path generation under course-level confirmatory evaluation and independent cross-dataset validation. Development used 3,187 MOOCCubeX courses, followed by a frozen confirmatory evaluation on 541 held-out courses and 37,747 transitions. The primary MOOCCubeX task is structural and course-prefix-conditioned rather than learner-personalised. Junyi provides independent structure-/hierarchy-aware external validation, uses hierarchy metadata rather than an explicit prerequisite graph and is not a zero-shot transfer setting. On the frozen MOOCCubeX evaluation, XGBoost pairwise was the strongest observed immediate ranker, with mean reciprocal rank (MRR) of 0.9048, whereas the proposed prerequisite-aware graph model achieved 0.7707. In the controlled graph-variant development ablation, the no-graph condition produced the strongest immediate ranking result, and immediate prediction showed strong dependence on permitted title/path-similarity information. Autoregressive evaluation exposed path properties not captured by one-step ranking. On Junyi, Markov achieved the strongest observed MRR of 0.4453, compared with 0.3954 for the proposed graph model, with metric-specific differences across ranking measures. Overall, immediate predictive accuracy, prerequisite-structure reliability, and long-horizon path quality should be treated as complementary rather than interchangeable evaluation dimensions in educational recommendation.

</details>


### [126] [Open-world Reconstruction-based Classifier with Concept Drift Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09350)
*Joanna Komorniczak*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向数据流的开放世界分类方法，利用自编码器重构误差及其核密度估计识别未知类别，并通过增量微调适应已知类别的概念漂移；实验显示，该方法在稳定环境中具有竞争力，在发生概念漂移时表现最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据流中的类别分布会随时间变化，且可能出现训练阶段未见的新类别。现有识别方法需要同时应对已知类别的概念漂移、未知类别检测，以及对未知样本群体的区分与学习，才能适用于开放世界场景。

Method: 使用自编码器重构样本，并依据重构误差及其核密度估计刻画已知类别的分布。当样本偏离已知类别分布时，方法可检测并学习新类别的分布；当已知类别分布发生变化时，则通过增量微调自编码器权重，使模型持续适应不断演化的概念。

Result: 实验涵盖多种合成数据流、由静态图像数据集嵌入构造并模拟概念漂移与未知类别出现的数据流，以及包含新颖类别的真实数据集。与先进方法比较时，该方法在稳定环境下表现具有竞争力；发生概念漂移时，其整体表现最佳。研究还评估了三项识别质量指标及资源消耗。

Conclusion: 该方法将未知类别检测与分布学习、已知类别概念漂移适应结合起来，在开放世界数据流分类中展现出较强效果，尤其适合类别分布随时间变化的场景。

Abstract: Data stream processing has become central to modern machine learning applications, with concept drifts and novel class appearances posing the primary challenges faced by recognition methods. Adapting to concept drifts while also being able to detect novel classes and distinguish groups of unknown observations can be seen as a step towards effective method employment in an open-world setting. This work proposes a classification method that can detect and learn the distributions of new, unknown classes, as well as adapt to concept drifts once the distribution of known classes shifts. The proposed method uses the autoencoders' reconstruction errors and the KDE that models their distribution. The autoencoders incrementally adapt to the changing distributions of known classes via weight fine-tuning, resulting in continuous adjustment to evolving concepts. Conducted experiments used a diverse set of synthetic data streams, embeddings of a real-world static CIFAR dataset arranged into a stream to simulate concept drifts and the emergence of unknowns, as well as real-world datasets where novelty is observed. The methodology was compared with state-of-the-art approaches with three-criterion recognition quality assessment and resource consumption evaluation. The results indicate that the proposed method is competitive with reference approaches in stationary environments and performs best when concept drifts occur.

</details>


### [127] [Mitigating Covariate Shift in Behavioral Cloning via Robust Stationary Distribution Correction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09371)
*Seokin Seo,Byung-Jun Lee,Jongmin Lee,HyeongJoo Hwang,Hongseok Yang,Kee-Eung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向离线模仿学习的分布鲁棒行为克隆方法，通过估计平稳分布校正比率，增强策略对任意状态分布数据所引发分布偏移的抵抗力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线模仿学习仅凭专家示范数据训练，无法与环境在线交互。行为克隆虽然简单有效，却容易受到协变量偏移影响：学习策略访问的状态分布可能不同于专家策略访问的分布。当示范数据来自有偏采集或不完整轨迹等任意状态分布时，这种偏移会加剧，导致现有方法性能显著下降。本文聚焦于由状态数据分布任意性造成的偏移，而非环境动力学变化或专家动作噪声。

Method: 提出分布鲁棒行为克隆方法“DrilDICE”。该方法利用平稳分布校正比率估计技术，构造可行的校正方案，并将其用于分布鲁棒的行为克隆目标，以减轻状态分布偏移对策略学习的影响。

Result: 作者在多种协变量偏移情形下进行了大量实验。结果表明，该方法能提升策略对状态分布偏移的鲁棒性，并在相关场景中优于现有离线模仿学习方法。

Conclusion: 对于专家数据来自有偏或不完整状态分布的离线模仿学习问题，利用平稳分布校正比率构建分布鲁棒目标是一种有效方案。实验支持该方法在此类偏移场景中的优势；摘要未提供具体性能数值或适用边界。

Abstract: We consider offline imitation learning (IL), which aims to train an agent to imitate from the dataset of expert demonstrations without online interaction with the environment. Behavioral Cloning (BC) has been a simple yet effective approach to offline IL, but it is also well-known to be vulnerable to the covariate shift resulting from the mismatch between the state distributions induced by the learned policy and the expert policy. Moreover, as often occurs in practice, when expert datasets are collected from an arbitrary state distribution instead of a stationary one, these shifts become more pronounced, potentially leading to substantial failures in existing IL methods. Specifically, we focus on covariate shift resulting from arbitrary state data distributions, such as biased data collection or incomplete trajectories, rather than shifts induced by changes in dynamics or noisy expert actions. In this paper, to mitigate the effect of the covariate shifts in BC, we propose DrilDICE, which utilizes a distributionally robust BC objective by employing a stationary distribution correction ratio estimation (DICE) to derive a feasible solution. We evaluate the effectiveness of our method through an extensive set of experiments covering diverse covariate shift scenarios. The results demonstrate the efficacy of the proposed approach in improving the robustness against the shifts, outperforming existing offline IL methods in such scenarios.

</details>


### [128] [Calibrated Uncertainty Quantification on TDC ADMET: What Can Be Resolved Across 22 Datasets?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09373)
*Laurits Fredsgaard,Michael Riis Andersen,Mikkel N. Schmidt*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究比较多种不确定性量化流程在二十二个吸收、分布、代谢、排泄与毒性数据集上的表现。集成蒙特卡洛丢弃法总体排名靠前，但没有在所有指标上显著胜出；光梯度提升模型则在部分数据集表现突出、在另一些端点较弱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相关数据集通常规模小且来源异质，但模型预测会影响哪些化合物进入实验测试。因此，评估不能只看预测表现，还应考察概率与区间是否可靠。研究旨在比较常见不确定性量化方法，并检验基准评估能够支持哪些结论。

Method: 研究在二十二个数据集上比较十一种经过校准的建模流程，采用嵌套五折交叉验证，并跨十种化学结构划分和五个模型随机种子进行评估。评价指标包括基准预测表现、布里尔分数和百分之九十预测区间分数；同时考察不同评估规模及统计分析对方法比较结论的影响。

Result: 集成蒙特卡洛丢弃法在总体平均排名上最佳，但就基准预测表现而言，无法与其他集成流程区分；就布里尔分数和区间分数而言，也不能排除光梯度提升模型为最佳。光梯度提升模型在单个数据集上的获胜次数最多，但在若干端点表现较差。较小规模的评估可能选出不同的领先方法，且往往无法复现整体比较结果。

Conclusion: 结果支持将光梯度提升模型与集成神经网络模型作为互补基线，而非认定某一种方法普遍占优。比较不同方法时，应覆盖多个化学结构划分，并根据具体研究问题选择相匹配的统计分析。

Abstract: Absorption, distribution, metabolism, excretion, and toxicity (ADMET) models often rely on small, heterogeneous datasets, yet their predictions may determine which compounds proceed to experimental testing. Useful evaluations must therefore assess both predictive performance and the quality of reported probabilities or intervals. We ask which common uncertainty-quantification methods perform best on the 22 Therapeutics Data Commons (TDC) ADMET datasets and what this benchmark can resolve. We compare eleven calibrated pipelines using nested five-fold cross-validation across ten chemical partitions and five model seeds. Ensemble Monte Carlo (MC) dropout has the best average rank for TDC performance, Brier score, and 90% interval score. For TDC performance, however, it is not separated from the other ensemble pipelines; for Brier and interval score, LightGBM is also not ruled out as best. LightGBM wins more individual datasets than any other method for TDC performance and Brier score, but performs poorly on several endpoints. Smaller evaluations can identify a different leader and often fail to recover the broader comparison. These results make LightGBM and an ensemble neural model complementary baselines. Comparative claims should be checked across multiple chemical partitions using statistical analyses matched to the question being asked.

</details>


### [129] [k-Volume: a Unified Active Learning Strategy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09388)
*Eliot Tron,Alain Celisse,Christophe Denis,Alice Le Brigant*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种名为“近邻体积”的主动学习策略：依据样本近邻所覆盖的局部体积决定标注抽样概率，兼具密度、多样性及特定条件下的不确定性优势，并在多类数据集上表现稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在分类任务中，获取样本标签往往成本高昂，因此需要主动学习策略有效挑选待标注样本。现有策略可能对分类器或实验设置敏感，在某些场景下表现不佳。论文旨在提出一种适用范围广、计算成本低且表现稳健的选择方法。

Method: 对每个样本，计算包含其近邻的最小球体积，并依据该体积随机决定样本被选中标注的概率。该策略既可在输入空间使用，也可在输出空间使用；理论上，随着样本规模增大，其行为渐近等价于在数据分布的支撑集上进行均匀抽样。

Result: 在合成数据、真实表格数据和图像数据上的实验表明，该策略对分类器及实验设置具有较强稳定性，整体上常处于表现最佳的方法之列。它在若干真实数据集上优于核心集、典型性聚类和不确定性采样等先进方法，同时计算成本较低。

Conclusion: 近邻体积策略以局部几何信息实现了简洁、灵活且稳健的主动学习样本选择，并具有明确的渐近理论性质。实验结果显示，它可作为多种任务和设置下具有竞争力的低成本方案。

Abstract: In the classification context where revealing the label of a sample is costly, we propose a new Active Learning (AL) strategy named k-Volume (kVol). It randomly selects a sample to be labeled according to the volume of the smallest ball containing its k nearest neighbors. kVol is highly versatile: it can be applied either in the input space or in the output space, and it enjoys the desirable properties of the three main families of AL strategies: density, diversity, and (when performed in the output space) uncertainty. From a theoretical perspective, we establish that kVol is asymptotically equivalent to uniform sampling over the support of the (input or output) data distribution. Based on experiments on various synthetic and real-life tabular datasets and images, we observe that kVol is surprisingly stable: whatever the classifier or the experimental setup, it still lies among the best AL strategies unlike other competitors that fail dramatically. In particular, kVol outperforms state-of-the-art competitors such as CoreSet, TypiClust or Uncertainty on several real-life datasets, at a low computational cost.

</details>


### [130] [Causal graph fusion across incomplete cohorts and causal steering of tabular foundation models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09400)
*Yanke Li,Dhiyaa Al Jorf,Ayoube Ighissou,Anke Scheel-Sailer,Robert Riener,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出两项互补方法：融合多个不完整队列中的因果图，并在推理阶段将因果结构注入冻结的表格基础模型。实验显示，图融合优于所比较的基线；因果引导的注意力在分布变化下提升了预测区分能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床数据常分散在变量仅部分重叠、且存在缺失的多个队列中。现有研究通常将缺失数据下的因果发现与多来源数据整合分开处理；此外，表格基础模型通常无法直接利用因果图，除非重新训练。论文旨在统一处理这些问题，并让现成模型利用发现的因果结构。

Method: 方法包含两部分。第一，因果图融合：在队列共有变量上学习部分祖先图，再结合时间背景知识，将其融合进各队列的因果图；同时利用马尔可夫毯指导重新插补，使缺失数据处理嵌入因果发现过程。第二，因果注意力引导：将学得的因果图编译为注意力约束，在推理阶段引导一个参数冻结的表格基础模型，无需重新训练。

Result: 在具有已知真实因果结构的实验中，图融合在所有测试的缺失程度和队列异质性条件下均优于合并数据及逐队列分析基线，差异具有统计显著性。因果注意力引导在分布变化下提升了区分能力，而随机图未能带来同样收益。在两个脊髓损伤队列中，融合方法将年龄、白蛋白和血红蛋白识别为住院时长的祖先变量；没有任何单一队列能完整确定这一组关系。该方法还得到简洁且符合时间顺序的预测变量集合，在五个结局中的四个上达到或超过自动特征选择。

Conclusion: 将多队列因果图融合、缺失数据处理与基础模型推理阶段的结构引导结合起来，有望提升临床因果发现和预测的稳健性。真实队列结果显示，跨队列整合能够揭示单队列难以完整识别的变量关系，并产生具有时间合理性的预测变量；但摘要未提供外部验证等更多证据，实际推广效果仍需进一步评估。

Abstract: Clinical data are split across incomplete cohorts with partially overlapping variables. Causal discovery under missing data and multi-source integration have developed separately, and tabular foundation models cannot take a causal graph without retraining. We make two contributions. First, causal graph fusion learns a partial ancestral graph on the shared variables, fuses it into each cohort's graph under temporal background knowledge and lets Markov blankets guide re-imputation, so missing data are handled inside discovery. Second, causal attention steering compiles the learned graph into the attention of a frozen tabular foundation model at inference. Against known ground truth, fusion dominates pooled and per-cohort baselines in every missingness and heterogeneity condition tested (p<0.001), and steering improves discrimination under distribution shift (p<10^{-5}), a gain that a random graph does not produce. In two spinal cord injury cohorts, fusion orients age, albumin and hemoglobin as ancestors of length of stay, a set no single cohort orients in full, and yields compact, temporally ordered predictor sets that match or exceed automatic feature selection on four of five outcomes.

</details>


### [131] [Future-Informed Actor-Critic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09417)
*Samy Mokeddem,Gaspard Lambrechts,Adrien Bolland,Damien Ernst*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出未来信息价值函数及其对应的策略梯度，并据此构建未来信息演员—评论家算法；实验显示，利用依赖动作的未来信息可提升噪声奖励和稀疏奖励环境中的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法虽通过未来状态、动作或奖励特征改善样本效率和回报，但通常只使用不受动作影响的外生特征，忽略了依赖动作的内生特征，可能因此无法充分进行奖励归因。

Method: 提出未来信息价值函数，使其能够以未来状态、动作和奖励中的外生与内生特征为条件；基于该价值函数推导未来信息策略梯度，并据此设计未来信息演员—评论家算法。

Result: 实验结果表明，在奖励带噪声或稀疏的环境中，利用未来内生特征的所提算法优于标准演员—评论家算法。

Conclusion: 将依赖动作的未来信息纳入价值估计与策略梯度，有望改善奖励归因，并提升演员—评论家方法在困难奖励环境中的效果。

Abstract: Actor-critic methods optimize a parametrized policy using a critic to estimate their policy gradient. Recent work has shown that conditioning the critic on features of future states, actions, or rewards can improve sample efficiency and even final return. However, existing methods are restricted to exogenous features (independent of actions), overlooking endogenous features (dependent on actions) that may be crucial for credit assignment. We introduce the future-informed value function, which can be conditioned on both exogenous and endogenous features of future states, actions, and rewards. Then, we express the policy gradient using this value function, yielding the future-informed policy gradient. Building on that, we propose the future-informed actor-critic algorithm. Empirical results show that this algorithm leveraging endogenous features of the future outperforms standard actor-critic algorithms in environments with noisy and sparse rewards.

</details>


### [132] [MINE: Entropy-coded Memory-efficient Inference for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09424)
*Emil Schätzle,Markus Püschel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向大语言模型单流推理的无损熵编码方法，在不改变模型权重的前提下压缩权重数据，并以更快的数据读取实现推理加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单流解码受内存带宽限制：每生成一个词元，都需从内存读取全部权重；在本地或低延迟场景中，请求又难以通过批处理摊薄开销。既有无损压缩方法往往只适用于特定数据类型，或依赖额外假设，因此需要一种更通用且兼顾解码速度的压缩方案。

Method: 该方法采用针对大语言模型推理改造的图形处理器熵编码技术，利用量化后仍存在的权重非均匀分布进行压缩。它提供可调参数，以权衡压缩率和解码开销，并针对每个张量及图形处理器自动调优，形成模型体积与解码延迟之间的帕累托前沿；随后根据内存预算选择合适的内核配置，并集成到推理框架中。

Result: 在一款约八十亿参数的指令模型及二点一比特量化检查点上，该方法在两款显卡上分别达到二点五二倍和一点四八倍于最快基线的加速；对应的有效码率分别为每权重二点二四比特和三点四三比特。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，利用量化权重中的非均匀分布进行无损熵编码，可以在压缩模型存储的同时加速受内存带宽限制的单流推理。通过按硬件和张量调优，方法能够在模型大小与解码速度之间灵活取舍。

Abstract: We present MINE (Memory-efficient INference Engine), a lossless entropy coding approach that accelerates single-stream inference of Large Language Models (LLMs), where existing lossless compression methods yield speedups only for specific data types such as \texttt{BF16} and under additional assumptions. Single-stream decoding is strongly memory-bound, since every weight must be streamed from memory once per generated token and requests cannot be batched in local or low-latency deployments. At the core of MINE is dtANS-LLM, a GPU entropy coding scheme adapted from the prior dtANS to the needs of LLM inference. It exploits the non-uniform distribution of weights, which persists even after quantization, and exposes hyper-parameters that trade compression ratio against decoding cost. We auto-tune these parameters for each tensor and GPU to obtain the Pareto front between model size and decoding latency. MINE integrates into vLLM as a plugin and selects the best kernel configuration for a given memory budget. On Llama~3.1~8B~Instruct with a 2.1-bit EntQuant checkpoint, MINE achieves speedups of 2.52$\times$ and 1.48$\times$ over the fastest baseline on an RTX~4060~Ti and an RTX~5090, at effective bitrates of 2.24 and 3.43~bits per weight. MINE is available as open source.

</details>


### [133] [DexDICE: Reconciling Safety and Performance via Dual-Expert Offline Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09433)
*Seokin Seo,Sung Kuk Shyn,Minji Seo,Yisoo Lee,Jongmin Lee,Hongseok Yang,Kee-Eung Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种利用安全示范与高回报示范的离线模仿学习方法，在追求高性能的同时限制策略进入不安全区域，并在基准测试和真实移动机器人导航任务中取得较好效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 离线模仿学习需要兼顾高回报与安全，但现有方法往往依赖难以设计的显式安全代价函数，或要求收集既安全又高性能的专家示范，后者成本较高。论文因此探索如何结合两类各有所长、但单独使用均有局限的示范数据。

Method: 论文提出双专家离线模仿学习方法：结合遵循安全规范但可能表现欠佳的示范，以及回报较高但可能违反安全规范的示范。该方法将离线分布校正学习与安全支持约束结合，使策略能够利用高回报行为，同时将行为限制在安全示范所覆盖的区域内。作者还证明，理想情况下若精确满足该约束，策略以高概率不会访问不安全区域。

Result: 在重新整理的安全强化学习基准上，该方法优于所比较的基线；在直接使用人类远程操控示范训练的真实移动机器人导航任务中，也表现出明显优势，并能较好应对数据稀缺和数据质量下降。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，分别利用安全性与性能导向的示范，并通过安全支持约束协调二者，是降低高性能离线模仿学习安全风险的一种可行路径。理论分析与真实机器人实验共同支持了该方法的有效性；不过，摘要未提供具体提升幅度或适用范围的详细数据。

Abstract: Ensuring high returns while providing strong safety guarantees in offline imitation learning (IL) is a fundamental challenge: existing approaches either rely on explicitly specified cost functions, which are difficult to design for complex real-world safety constraints, or require expert demonstrations that are both safe and high-performing, which are often too costly to collect. We introduce dual-expert imitation learning, which leverages two complementary data sources: (i) safety-compliant demonstrations that faithfully follow safety guidelines but may be suboptimal, and (ii) performance-oriented demonstrations that achieve high returns but may violate safety guidelines. To address this problem, we propose DexDICE (Dual-expert offline imitation learning via stationary DIstribution Correction Estimation). Instead of conventional distribution constraints, DexDICE combines DICE-style offline learning with safe support constraints, enabling agents to exploit high-return behaviors while remaining within safety-compliant regions. We prove that the ideal solution satisfying this constraint exactly has zero unsafe-region visitation with high probability. We evaluate DexDICE on a re-curated DSRL benchmark and validate it on a real-world mobile-robot navigation task trained directly from human teleoperation demonstrations, where it substantially outperforms baselines and remains robust to data scarcity and quality degradation.

</details>


### [134] [Not All Entropy Is Equal: Reward-Conditioned Entropy Allocation in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09448)
*Song Yu,Li Li,Zhenming Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出按奖励结果、过程有效性及正确解策略多样性分解熵的诊断框架，指出单一全局熵无法可靠反映推理能力；应关注训练究竟消除了哪类不确定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在可验证奖励强化学习中，策略熵常被用来判断探索程度或策略是否坍缩，但熵的变化并不总与推理准确率一致。原因在于全局词元熵混合了成功与失败轨迹的不确定性、正确解法策略之间的差异，以及同一策略不同表达形式之间的差异，因而难以解释。

Method: 提出奖励条件熵分配框架，将完整轨迹熵与平均词元熵区分开来，并按验证器结果和过程有效性对不确定性分层；同时衡量正确解法模式的保留情况，并以局部协方差驱动的更新分析连接群体层面的熵变化。研究在两种模型规模、六种强化学习优化目标下进行对照实验。

Result: 不同优化目标下的熵变化轨迹无法稳定排列推理准确率。无论采用分别汇总的估计，还是针对相同问题的配对分析，验证失败轨迹的词元熵都高于成功轨迹；配对分析展示的十二个端点中，有十个置信区间排除了两者相等。局部协方差近似在非失败的相邻检查点变化中，有百分之九十五点五正确预测熵变化方向。按奖励分层的端点代理指标还显示，失败前缀中的正向被选词元协方差更集中于分布尾部。所有可报告的训练终点都出现了经独立审计确认的正确解法模式广度收缩；其与大规模抽样覆盖率的正相关仍属探索性发现。

Conclusion: 熵下降本身不能判定推理能力提升或探索坍缩。更有效的诊断方式是辨明训练移除了哪种不确定性，并分别考察成功与失败轨迹、过程有效性及正确解法策略的多样性；正确解法模式收缩值得关注，但其与覆盖率的关联尚需进一步验证。

Abstract: Policy entropy is widely used to diagnose exploration and collapse in reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), yet its magnitude does not consistently track reasoning performance across optimization objectives. We argue that this ambiguity arises because global token entropy conflates uncertainty in successful and failed trajectories, diversity across correct solution strategies, and variation among realizations of the same strategy. We introduce \textbf{Reward-Conditioned Entropy Allocation (RCEA)}, a hierarchical diagnostic framework that separates complete-trajectory entropy from average token entropy, conditions uncertainty on verifier outcome and process validity, measures correct-solution-mode retention, and connects these population-level quantities to local covariance-driven updates. In controlled sweeps over six RLVR objectives at two model scales, entropy trajectories do not induce a stable ordering of reasoning accuracy. Token entropy is higher for verifier-failed than successful trajectories under both broad separately aggregated estimates and a common-question paired analysis, whose confidence intervals exclude equality at 10 of 12 displayed endpoints. A local covariance approximation predicts the direction of adjacent-checkpoint entropy change in 95.5\% of nonfailed transitions; a reward-stratified endpoint proxy further shows that positive chosen-token covariance is more tail-concentrated within failed prefixes. Every reportable trained endpoint exhibits some contraction in independently audited correct-solution-mode breadth, whose positive association with large-\(k\) coverage remains exploratory. These results show that entropy collapse should be diagnosed by which uncertainty is removed, rather than by global entropy alone.

</details>


### [135] [Consensus-Constrained Dual-Federated Optimization with Client-Side Dynamic Weighting for Distributed Android Device Threat Defense](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09470)
*Mohammad Mashreghi,Mohammad Hossein Badiei,Md. Jalil Piran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种结合动态客户端加权与原始—对偶协调的联邦优化方法，用于安卓恶意软件检测；实验显示其整体表现略优于对照方法，并在部分数据集和较长通信预算下优势更明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安卓设备面临日益复杂的恶意软件威胁，而集中式检测需要汇集原始数据，带来隐私与扩展性问题。联邦学习虽可在不集中数据的情况下协同训练，但客户端参与不完整、各客户端本地模型质量不同，可能使全局优化不稳定。

Method: 提出动态加权联邦交替方向乘子法。该方法依据客户端性能动态调整其本地状态对全局更新的影响，并通过对偶变量约束本地模型与全局模型之间的差异，以协调客户端更新。研究在三种安卓恶意软件基准数据集上测试不同客户端数量、部分参与情况及通信轮数，每种配置使用十个随机种子重复实验。

Result: 该方法在第一种数据集上取得最高的种子平均准确率，为百分之九十七点六四；在十八种数据集、客户端数量和通信轮数组合中，有九种取得最高平均准确率，其中十轮通信的九种组合中占七种。汇总所有配置后，准确率、F一分数、精确率和曲线下面积分别为百分之九十五点四二、九十四点六二、九十六点零六和九十九点四五，略高于联邦交替方向乘子法基线；同时平均误报率最低，客户端更新范数最小。两种原始—对偶方法在另外两种数据集上明显优于基于平均或近端优化的基线，后者在客户端数据分散时常停留于多数类预测。动态加权的收益在第一种数据集和较长通信预算下最突出，并降低了不同随机种子间的结果波动。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，依据客户端表现动态调节本地更新，并结合原始—对偶协调，有助于提升联邦恶意软件检测的准确性、稳定性和更新效率。不过，该方法并非在所有配置中都取得最佳准确率；其优势会随数据集和通信预算变化。

Abstract: Smartphones, smart-home hubs, and wearables are increasingly exposed to sophisticated Android malware, while centralized threat-detection pipelines raise privacy and scalability concerns. Federated learning (FL) enables collaborative model training without collecting raw client data, but partial participation and variation in local model quality can destabilize global optimization. In this article, we propose DW-FedADMM, a federated optimization framework that combines primal-dual coordination with performance-dependent client-side weighting. The dynamic coefficient modulates each participating client's local state before the augmented-Lagrangian update, while the dual variables regulate disagreement between local and global model states. We evaluate DW-FedADMM on three Android malware benchmarks, namely Drebin, Malgenome, and TUANDROMD, with 5, 10, and 15 clients holding IID data shards, partial client participation, and communication budgets of 5 and 10 rounds. Each configuration is repeated with ten random seeds. DW-FedADMM obtains the highest seed-averaged accuracy on Drebin (97.64% +/- 0.20%) and the best mean accuracy in 9 of the 18 dataset-client-round configurations, including 7 of the 9 configurations with a 10-round budget. Pooled over all configurations, it reaches 95.42% accuracy, 94.62% F1-score, 96.06% precision, and 99.45% AUC, compared with 95.32%, 94.51%, 95.56%, and 99.41% for FedADMM, while also giving the lowest mean false-positive rate and the smallest client update norm among the six compared methods. Both primal-dual methods are well ahead of the averaging- and proximal-based baselines on Malgenome and TUANDROMD, where the latter often remain at the majority-class solution when clients are fragmented. The gain of dynamic weighting over FedADMM is most pronounced on Drebin and with the longer communication budget, where it also reduces the variation across seeds.

</details>


### [136] [Mosaic:montage-agnostic self-distilled graph learning for subject-independent ieeg seizure detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09496)
*Arya Jalali,Mahsa Shoaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究提出一种不依赖固定电极布局的轻量级图学习框架，用于跨患者脑内脑电发作检测；在四个公开数据集上，其检测表现优于现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 脑内脑电具有较高的信噪比和空间分辨率，但不同患者的电极数量与排列方式差异很大，导致依赖固定通道结构的检测器难以推广到未见过的患者。研究旨在提升发作检测在不同植入方案之间的泛化能力。

Method: 该方法结合共享卷积通道编码器与患者无关的通道注意力模块，根据编码特征自适应推断加权通道连接矩阵，无需预先设定固定电极图结构。此外，采用指数移动平均自蒸馏，让当前预测向缓慢更新的患者级教师模型靠拢，以增强训练稳定性。

Result: 在四个公开脑内脑电数据集上，该方法的Ｆ二分数和科恩卡帕系数均优于先进基线，并学得更稳定的通道连接结构。结果表明，自蒸馏连接学习能够迁移到未见过的电极植入方案。

Conclusion: 自适应学习通道间连接，并结合自蒸馏正则化，是应对患者间电极布局差异的有效途径，可提升脑内脑电发作检测的跨患者泛化能力。

Abstract: Intracranial EEG provides high signal-to-noise ratio and spatial resolution for detecting epileptic activity, but patient-specific electrode placement leads to substantial variation in channel count and montage across subjects. This heterogeneity limits the generalization of existing detectors under subject-independent evaluation. We propose MOSAIC (MOntage-agnostic Self-distilled Adaptive Inter-channel Connectivity learning), a lightweight graph-learning framework for subject-independent iEEG seizure detection. MOSAIC pairs a shared convolutional channel encoder with a patient-agnostic channel-attention module that infers a weighted inter-channel adjacency matrix from the encoded features, requiring no fixed montage graph. An exponential moving average self-distillation objective regularizes each prediction toward a slowly updated per-patient teacher. Across four public iEEG corpora (SWEC-ETHZ long- and short-term, Mayo, FNUSA), MOSAIC outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in F2 and Cohen's $κ$ while learning more stable inter-channel adjacencies, demonstrating that self-distilled connectivity learning transfers across unseen implantations.

</details>


### [137] [e-means: outperforming k-means (in one-tenth the epochs)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09501)
*Swann Bessa,Pierre-Emmanuel Mazaré,Gergely Szilvasy,Hugo Touvron,David Picard,Matthijs Douze,Hervé Jégou*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出基于指数移动平均和小批量更新的聚类算法，在更少训练轮数下达到更低损失，并显著缩短图形处理器运行时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的劳埃德算法虽广泛用于聚类，但在大规模嵌入数据上计算耗时较长。小批量更新有望提升效率，但衰减率和批量大小等参数如何随问题规模变化，缺乏可直接采用的设定方法，往往需要反复调参。

Method: 提出指数移动平均聚类算法，通过小批量更新优化聚类中心，并研究衰减率、批量大小与最终聚类性能之间的缩放规律。据此给出适用于不同问题规模和嵌入数据集的参数设定规则，无需超参数搜索。

Result: 在多种嵌入数据集上，该算法用约1.4至2.6轮训练达到劳埃德算法运行25轮时的损失，带来约10至45倍的图形处理器运行时间加速；其损失通常更低，且经常优于加号加号初始化的聚类方法。作为大规模实例，该算法仅用2.5轮便将一百亿个嵌入向量划分为一千六百万个簇，损失低于劳埃德算法运行25轮的结果。

Conclusion: 研究表明，结合指数移动平均、小批量优化和基于缩放规律的参数设定，可以显著提升大规模聚类的速度与损失表现，并减少人工调参需求。

Abstract: Lloyd's k-means algorithm is a long-running standard for clustering, with applications in data analysis, retrieval, quantization and data curation. In this paper, we introduce an exponential moving average optimization procedure for k-means with mini-batch updates, powered by modern deep learning machinery and new insights. We also establish scaling laws on how decay rate and batch size impact final performance. This gives us a formulaic rule to fix them for multiple problem sizes and embedding datasets, without the need for any hyperparameter sweep. The resulting clustering algorithm, coined e-means, is much faster than Lloyd k-means and converges to lower losses, frequently even beating k-means++. On a wide array of embeddings datasets, it beats the loss of Lloyd k-means at 25 epochs in 1.4 to 2.6 epochs, which translates to 10-45x GPU wall-clock speedups compared to state-of-the-art k-means implementations. As an example, e-means partitions DINO10B, a dataset of 10 billion DINO embeddings, into 16 million clusters in only 2.5 epochs, reaching a loss below Lloyd's at 25 epochs.

</details>


### [138] [TimeOmni-Live: Continuous & Live Time Series Understanding with Long-Term Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09502)
*Tong Guan,Alexander Chemeris,Yiqing Guo,Warren Jin,Zhao Li,Shirui Pan,Ming Jin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出面向连续时间序列流的理解范式，并构建配套基准与模型。所提模型在准确性、响应延迟和边缘实时运行方面表现突出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有时间序列语言模型通常采用离线、一次性处理方式：每次只分析固定长度的输入，且不保留历史状态。这与真实流式应用持续到达数据的特点不匹配，也使模型难以结合不同时间窗口的信息进行推理。因此，论文旨在推动模型从处理独立片段转向对开放数据流进行持续、有状态的理解。

Method: 论文提出“连续与实时的时间序列理解”范式，并构建首个针对该范式的综合数据套件，支持数据生成、模型训练和系统评测，覆盖六项核心能力、十八项任务。数据套件包含五千余条数据流，共二十二亿个时间步，并标注一万五千个特定响应事件。评测协议综合考察响应时效性、内容准确性和计算效率。在此基础上，论文提出实时模型：采用混合时间序列编码处理原生时序输入，以持久化混合记忆保留精确历史证据与长期任务状态，并通过弹性的数据流与推理同步策略降低处理延迟。

Result: 实验表明，该模型为连续时间序列理解建立了有竞争力的基线：以四十亿参数取得九十点零的得分，高于前沿大语言模型奥普斯五的七十九点三；其第九十五百分位延迟比所用骨干模型低二百零六倍。该模型还在杰特森边缘设备上部署，展示了本地实时连续理解的能力。

Conclusion: 论文指出，时间序列模型需要具备跨窗口记忆、持续推理和及时响应能力，才能适应真实流式场景。其提出的任务范式、数据与评测体系以及模型共同构成了研究这一方向的基础；边缘部署结果也显示，该方法具有实际应用潜力。

Abstract: Existing time series language models predominantly follow an offline, one-shot paradigm: they process fixed-length inputs independently and do not retain past states. This static approach fundamentally misaligns with the continuous nature of real-world streaming applications and fails to maintain the historical context necessary for cross-window reasoning. To bridge this gap, we introduce Continuous & Live Time Series Understanding (CL-TSU), a novel paradigm that shifts the focus toward maintaining a persistent, stateful understanding over open-ended data streams. To systematically evaluate this paradigm, we present LiveTS-Suite, the first comprehensive data suite tailored for CL-TSU, supporting data generation, model training, and systematic evaluation across 18 tasks spanning six core capabilities. It comprises over 5K streams totaling 2.2B timesteps, annotated with 15K specific response events. We further design an evaluation protocol that jointly assesses response timeliness, content accuracy, and computational efficiency. Building on this foundation, we propose TimeOmni-Live, the first live time series understanding model. It integrates a hybrid time series encoding for natively temporal input, a stream-persistent hybrid memory mechanism to effectively preserve exact historical evidence and long-term task states, and an elastic stream-inference synchronization strategy to reduce processing lag. Evaluations show that TimeOmni-Live establishes a strong baseline for CL-TSU, outperforming frontier LLMs (90.0 vs. 79.3 for Opus 5) with only 4B parameters and achieving 206$\times$ lower p95 latency than its backbone. Deployment on a Jetson edge device further shows its ability to support real-time continuous temporal understanding locally at the edge.

</details>


### [139] [Active Learning of Robust Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09520)
*Mizu Nishikawa-Toomey,Andreas Kirsch,Laurent Charlin,Dhanya Sridhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究部署环境与标注数据存在子群体分布偏移时的主动学习，利用数据集中的元数据作为子群体的噪声代理，改善模型在不同子群体上的分类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实任务中，获取标注数据成本高，且训练与部署数据可能因地区、群体等因素而分布不同。传统主动学习通常未考虑这两项挑战同时出现的情况，可能因此无法有效选择样本，或导致模型在部分子群体上表现不佳。

Method: 针对子群体分布偏移，提出一种主动学习策略，目标是在不同子群体上都获得较好的分类性能。该方法利用数据收集元数据作为样本所属子群体的噪声代理，并将这些信息纳入主动学习的样本选择过程。

Result: 实验结果表明，与常规方法相比，该策略提升了分类器在不同子群体上的准确率。摘要未提供具体数据集、基线方法或提升幅度。

Conclusion: 将子群体信息纳入主动学习，有助于同时应对标注成本高和部署分布偏移的问题。即使子群体标记不准确，数据收集元数据仍可作为有用线索，帮助训练出跨子群体表现更稳健的模型。

Abstract: In many domains from species identification to cancer diagnosis, labelling data to train predictive models remains costly. In these settings, it is advantageous to intelligently select data points to label to achieve the maximum information gain, this is the motivation behind the study of active learning. In addition to the high costs of acquiring data in such domains, there is the added complexity of data distribution shifts that can occur between training and deployment. For example, the features of cancerous skin cells taken in one country may be different in another region of the world. These two challenges - efficient labelling of data, and learning models robust to distribution shifts have been studied separately but not together, even though these challenges often co-occur in real-life settings. We identify failure modes of standard active learning approaches when the distribution of data at deployment is different from the distribution in which the data is sampled for labelling. We propose a new strategy for efficiently learning classifiers that generalise under specific forms of distribution shift, namely subpopulation shift. Drawing on work in learning from heterogeneous data, we aim to train models that perform well across subpopulations of the data, through active learning. To handle this challenging setting, we exploit the fact that many datasets include metadata about data collection that can serve as a noisy proxy for each sample's underlying subpopulation. We demonstrate how to leverage these noisy labels during active learning selection, and demonstrate empirically that our approach improves classifier accuracy across subpopulations.

</details>


### [140] [Marginal-Nonuniform Multiclass Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09543)
*Nataly Brukhim,Steve Hanneke,Amirreza Shaeiri,Maximilian Thiessen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文刻画了多类分类在边缘分布非一致学习框架下的最优学习速率，并证明速率呈现指数、线性或任意缓慢三种情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典概率近似正确学习的速率常数不能依赖边缘分布或目标概念；普适学习则允许常数同时依赖两者。论文研究两者之间的边缘分布非一致学习：速率常数可依赖未知的无标签数据边缘分布，但不能依赖目标概念，并希望明确这一框架下可达到的最优速率及其决定因素。

Method: 提出一种新的组合维数——非中心化判别集可消解维数，用它刻画学习问题的结构，并据此建立最优速率的完整判定。证明上界时，进一步构造分布耗尽引理，利用该维数的序贯性质控制学习过程。

Result: 研究给出了边缘分布非一致学习最优速率的完整刻画：不同问题分别对应指数速率、线性速率或任意缓慢速率。非中心化判别集可消解维数是体现这一三分结构的核心工具；新的分布耗尽引理则支撑了相应的上界证明。

Conclusion: 多类分类的边缘分布非一致学习存在由组合结构决定的基本速率三分现象。论文不仅建立了相应的刻画框架，也提供了可用于其他序贯维数分析的新型分布耗尽方法。

Abstract: We study the problem of multiclass classification within the framework of marginal-nonuniform learning, where the constants in the learning rate may depend on the unknown marginal distribution generating the unlabeled data, but not on the target concept. This notion naturally lies between classical PAC learning, where the constants are independent of both the marginal distribution and the target concept, and more recent universal learning, where the constants may depend on both. We give a complete characterization of the optimal marginal-nonuniform learning rates, establishing a fundamental trichotomy: exponential, linear, and arbitrarily slow rates. This characterization is witnessed by a new combinatorial dimension, termed the uncentered DS-eluder dimension. A key ingredient in the upper-bound proofs is a set of new distributional exhaustion lemmas that exploit the sequential nature of this dimension and may be of independent interest.

</details>


### [141] [Solver-Free Decision-Focused Learning via Neural Optimization Layers for Edge Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09560)
*Xinda Zheng,Frits de Nijs,Edward Lam*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需求解器和最优解标签的决策导向学习框架，通过神经优化层与预测模型协同训练，在旅行商和定向越野问题上提升决策质量并降低训练与部署成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 决策导向学习旨在让预测模型直接服务于下游决策，但现有方法往往依赖高成本的求解器调用或最优解标签。对于目标函数、约束或两者都含有不确定参数的困难边选择问题，需要一种更高效且不依赖这些资源的训练与部署方案。

Method: 框架分两阶段训练。首先，利用问题目标函数和约束惩罚项训练神经优化层，无需解标签；通过图结构信息引导的边评分和迭代优化，使连续决策更集中且符合结构要求。随后冻结该层，让预测模型通过其连续输出进行训练，并使用观测参数下的问题专用损失评估决策效果。部署时，预测模型、神经优化层和轻量解码器共同生成决策，无需求解原始困难问题。

Result: 在非对称旅行商问题和无端点限制的定向越野问题上，该方法的决策质量持续优于以预测精度为目标的学习方法，并超过多数对比的决策导向学习基线。即使计入神经优化层的预训练，训练耗时仍低于所评估的决策导向基线；神经网络部署也比基于求解器的部署更快。此外，将该层用作近似优化预言机，还能降低现有决策导向方法的训练成本。
