Conclusion: 该框架以可训练的神经优化层替代昂贵的求解器调用和最优解监督，为困难边选择问题提供了兼顾决策质量、训练效率与部署速度的方案，并可作为现有决策导向方法的近似优化组件。

Abstract: Decision-focused learning trains prediction models to improve downstream decisions, but many methods require costly solver calls or optimal-solution labels. We propose a solver-free decision-focused learning framework for NP-hard edge-selection problems with uncertain parameters in the objective, the constraints, or both. A neural optimization layer is first trained without solution labels, using problem objectives and constraint penalties. Graph-informed edge scoring and iterative refinement encourage concentrated and structurally consistent continuous decisions. The layer is then frozen, and a prediction model is trained through its continuous output using a problem-specific loss evaluated under observed parameters. At deployment, the predictor, optimization layer, and a lightweight decoder produce decisions without solving the original NP-hard problem. Experiments on the asymmetric traveling salesperson and endpoint-free orienteering problems show consistently better decision quality than prediction-focused learning and improvements over most evaluated decision-focused baselines. Our framework requires less training time than the evaluated decision-focused baselines, even when optimization-layer pretraining is included. Neural deployment also produces decisions faster than solver-based deployment, offering a better quality-efficiency trade-off. The layer also reduces training cost in existing decision-focused methods when used as an approximate optimization oracle.

</details>


### [142] [Coordinatewise Epsilon for Adam under Heavy-Tailed Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09596)
*Jeonseong Kim,Chenglin Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究自适应矩估计优化器中分母稳定项的坐标共享方式，发现按坐标设置可达到重尾噪声下的最优收敛阶，而共享标量设置存在更差的下界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该类优化器通常逐坐标估计二阶矩，却在所有坐标的分母中加入同一个稳定常数。当不同坐标的噪声强度和曲率差异较大时，共享常数可能限制收敛性能；论文旨在刻画这种影响。

Method: 在各坐标噪声具有有限阶矩（阶数介于一与二之间，含二）的条件下，分析逐坐标稳定项与共享标量稳定项的收敛表现。逐坐标方案依据各坐标的噪声界和曲率界设定稳定项；对共享方案，则建立适用于任意可预测学习率、一阶矩衰减系数、二阶矩衰减系数及稳定项选择的下界。

Result: 逐坐标设置可达到重尾噪声下以一范数衡量的平稳性基准，收敛阶为 O(T^{-(p-1)/(3p-2)})，与光滑非凸随机优化已知的最优时间指数一致。对于 1<p<2，共享标量稳定项的收敛下界为 Ω(T^{-(p-1)/(2p)})。

Conclusion: 稳定项是否按坐标设置会实质影响重尾条件下的收敛能力。逐坐标设置能够达到基准速率；而共享标量设置即使允许灵活地调整各项可预测参数，也无法获得与维数无关的基准速率。

Abstract: Adam estimates second moments coordinatewise, but typically adds the same scalar $\varepsilon$ to every denominator. We study how this shared $\varepsilon$ affects convergence when noise and curvature vary across coordinates. Under coordinatewise finite-$p$ noise moments, $p\in(1,2]$, choosing $\varepsilon_i$ from the noise and curvature bounds for each coordinate attains the heavy-tailed $\ell_1$-stationarity benchmark $O(T^{-\frac{p-1}{3p-2}})$, matching the known optimal time exponent for smooth nonconvex stochastic optimization. For $1<p<2$, Adam with a shared $\varepsilon_t$ admits a lower bound of order $Ω(T^{-\frac{p-1}{2p}})$. This lower bound holds for every predictable scalar choice of $η_t$, $β_{1,t}$, $β_{2,t}$, and $\varepsilon_t$, precluding a dimension-uniform benchmark rate for this class.

</details>


### [143] [Transformer-FSQ: Discrete Latents for Extreme KV-Cache Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09598)
*Andras Szecsenyi,Thomas L. Lee,Vaisakh Shaj,Elliot J. Crowley,Amos Storkey*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种极限压缩键值缓存的方法，在显著降低缓存占用的同时，尽量维持长上下文检索准确率与解码速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向本地设备的个性化语言模型受限于存储空间，而键值缓存会随上下文长度线性增长，甚至远超模型权重。现有压缩方法在较高压缩率下效果不佳，因此需要兼顾极高压缩率、检索能力和运行效率的新方案。

Method: 以有限标量量化瓶颈替换标准注意力中的键和值投影，将每个词元的缓存表示为打包的离散整数码，不需保存逐词元量化元数据。将预训练模型转换为该结构时，仅训练键和值投影，训练数据量约为每个原始参数对应四分之一词元；另实现融合式计算内核，直接从整数码解码。

Result: 在六亿至八十亿参数的模型上，与五类基线方法比较，长上下文检索评测显示：在十二倍至九十七点五倍压缩率下，该方法在所测方法中准确率最高，且适用于不同模型规模。对八十亿参数模型，在三十二倍压缩率下，解码吞吐率约为未压缩原生注意力的百分之一百零一；在九十七点五倍压缩率下约为百分之一百五十四。

Conclusion: 有限标量量化能够将键值缓存压缩至极高倍率，同时保持有竞争力的检索准确率和解码性能，使固定容量的消费级设备能够容纳更长上下文，为本地运行个性化语言模型提供了实用途径。

Abstract: Private, personalised language models must run on local consumer devices, where large, frontier models cannot fit, and retaining long contexts is non-trivial. Sequential memory---the key-value (KV) cache---grows linearly with context length, quickly dwarfing the model itself: for Qwen3-1.7B-Base, 200,000 retained entries require 21 GiB, 6.6 times more than the model weights. Yet the KV-cache compression methods we measured fail beyond moderate compression ratios. We introduce Transformer-FSQ, addressing extreme KV-cache compression by replacing standard key and value projections with a finite scalar quantisation bottleneck. Our cache stores a packed vector of discrete integer codes per token, with no per-token quantisation metadata. Converting a pretrained transformer trains only these projections on 0.25 tokens per original parameter. We evaluate Transformer-FSQ against five baseline families on models from 0.6B to 8B parameters. On long-context retrieval (RULER), from 12$\times$ to 97.5$\times$ Transformer-FSQ is the most accurate method we evaluate, across scales. A fused Triton kernel decodes directly from the integer codes; with baselines given their authors' kernels where available, it decodes at $1.01\times$ (95% interval $0.98$--$1.05$) the throughput of native SDPA attention on the uncompressed Qwen3-8B-Base at $32\times$ and $1.54\times$ at $97.5\times$. Extreme KV-cache compression is therefore a practical way to extend how much context a fixed device can hold.

</details>


### [144] [Encoding Memory in World Models with Test-Time Model-Based Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09625)
*Maxime Burchi,Fayaz Ali Dharejo,Radu Timofte*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该方法在测试阶段通过梯度下降，将每个回合的观察信息写入世界模型参数，从而增强长期记忆；在记忆迷宫基准上取得新最佳成绩，同时在其他控制任务上保持良好表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于模型的强化学习虽能从轨迹中学习准确的世界模型，但在需要长期记忆与规划的任务上仍难以达到人类水平。常见方法主要依赖上下文记忆或调整网络架构，难以充分保存和利用整个回合中的信息。

Method: 提出测试时基于模型的强化学习：在测试阶段使用梯度下降，把当前回合的观察信息编码进世界模型参数，使智能体能够为每个回合形成专属世界模型。作者先在简化的二维记忆迷宫中验证这一思路，再将方法用于三维任务。

Result: 在二维记忆迷宫中，该方法在不同世界模型架构下均带来稳定提升；在三维记忆迷宫基准上取得新的最佳结果。此外，在深度思维控制和雅达利一百千步等非记忆类基准上也保持了较强表现。

Conclusion: 将回合记忆写入测试阶段更新的模型参数，是提升基于模型的强化学习长期记忆能力的一种有效途径。该方法不仅改善记忆密集型任务的表现，也能兼顾非记忆类任务。

Abstract: Model-based reinforcement learning (MBRL) approaches have demonstrated strong performance across diverse domains in recent years, learning accurate world models from agent trajectories. However, despite this progress, current approaches still struggle to surpass human performance on tasks that require long-term memory and planning. In this work, we address the problem of long-term memory in MBRL by moving beyond the commonly studied paradigm of architecture design. We introduce Test-Time Model-Based Reinforcement Learning (TT-MBRL), using gradient descent at test time to encode the memory of episode observations into world model parameters. This enables the agent to learn a distinct world model for each episode, improving memory capabilities compared to traditional approaches that solely rely on contextual memory. We first establish a proof of concept on a simplified two-dimensional variant of the Memory Maze environment, which we refer to as Memory Maze 2D, showing consistent improvements across different world model architectures. We then demonstrate the effectiveness of TT-MBRL in the 3D domain, achieving new state-of-the-art results on the Memory Maze benchmark. Finally, we show that TT-MBRL maintains strong performance on non-memory benchmarks such as DeepMind Control and Atari 100k.

</details>


### [145] [Know What You Need: Efficient Activation Checkpointing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09614)
*Danyal Ahmed,Edouard Oyallon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种近似重计算方法，利用前向阶段收集的信息选择性重算重要层组件，以减少激活检查点带来的额外计算；在大幅减少重算量的同时，困惑度仅小幅上升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练受显存容量限制，激活检查点通过在反向传播时重新计算部分激活来节省显存，但会增加计算开销，尤其是变换器线性层的重算代价较高。既有近似方法可能导致梯度误差在反向传播中累积，或未能利用前向阶段已获得的信息。

Method: 提出一种可产生无偏梯度估计的近似重计算方法。该方法依据初始前向计算收集的信息，选择性重算最重要的层组件；组件选择可采用贪心策略或随机草图技术，从而以较少的重算近似完整计算。

Result: 消融实验显示，所得梯度与精确梯度高度接近。在一款约四点八八亿参数的拉马模型中，对前馈层、注意力层或两者同时应用该方法时，均只需重算约三分之二的层组件；对应的困惑度分别上升百分之一点七、百分之一点二和百分之二点五。

Conclusion: 该方法利用前向阶段信息，在减少反向传播重算开销的同时，较好地保持梯度质量和模型表现，为显存受限的大语言模型训练提供了一种有效的近似重计算方案。

Abstract: Large Language Model training is increasingly constrained by memory capacity. Activation Checkpointing reduces activation memory by avoiding storing selected activations during the forward pass and rematerializing them during backpropagation, thereby trading memory savings for additional computation. Although this technique is essential for scaling model training, rematerialization can be prohibitively expensive, particularly for Transformer linear layers. Approximate computation could reduce this overhead, but conventional approaches either introduce errors that accumulate over the backward pass or fail to exploit information available from the initial forward pass. We introduce a novel approximate rematerialization method that can yield unbiased gradient estimates: using information collected during the initial forward pass, the method selectively recomputes only the most important layer components, either through greedy selection or randomized sketching. Ablation studies show that the resulting gradients closely match the exact gradients. When applied to feed-forward layers, attention layers, or both, our method can recompute only two-thirds of the layer components in a Llama-488M model, while increasing perplexity by just 1.7%, 1.2%, and 2.5%, respectively.

</details>


### [146] [Verifier Noise Is a Temperature: Testing Tilt Predictions in Finite-Budget LLM-Guided Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09699)
*Xiaomeng Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文推导了“只保留得分较高候选”的搜索过程可能产生的偏置，并用预先登记的实验检验其对大语言模型程序搜索和提示优化的预测。小批量规模增大时，搜索水平通常上升，但理论对最终提升幅度的预测并不可靠；长期稳态是否符合理论仍未确定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型引导的搜索常通过比较新候选与当前候选、仅保留得分较高者来迭代。若比较时重新评估双方得分，这种接受规则可能系统性地偏向高分候选，且小批量评估会改变这种偏置。论文希望刻画这一机制所诱导的稳态分布，并检验该理论能否预测实际搜索表现；同时考察缓存当前候选得分是否会造成额外偏差。

Method: 理论上，论文证明：当得分差噪声服从逻辑分布且变异操作可逆时，每次比较都重新评估双方得分的“优者保留”过程构成可逆马尔可夫链，其稳态分布等于变异过程的平衡分布乘以得分指数倾斜项。对于连续且对称的噪声差，小步扩散极限下也得到同类倾斜分布，倾斜参数为噪声分布在零点处密度斜率的四倍；小批量门控下，该参数随批量大小的平方根增长。实验方面，研究者依据校准数据，在搜索运行前预先登记预测，再测试程序搜索与提示优化；还检验了父子候选质量相关性、不同起始程序以及长程搜索的影响。

Result: 七种设置中有六种在运行一百五十步后随批量增大而提升，但预测的提升幅度未能准确命中。在装箱任务中，若提议器修改父程序，实际获得的提升为预测值的百分之六十二至八十八；若提议器直接编写新启发式，实际提升为百分之一百零九至一百一十九。实际提升占预测值的比例会随未筛选变异中父子候选质量相关性升高而下降；两个新增设置的预先登记检验支持这一方向，但经修正后仍未实现定量吻合。另一些预先登记的实验发现，从优质程序出发连续搜索三百步时，质量反而持续下降，且搜索曾选出的起点下降更明显，不过该差异仅略微达到预设判定标准。不同起点的搜索尚未收敛至共同水平，因此未能验证稳态预测。提示优化实验也未支持理论：在冻结任务模型得分的条件下，搜索结果高于倾斜理论的预测，装箱任务中的关系也未能迁移。

Conclusion: 候选比较机制确实可能产生可描述的得分偏置，批量大小也与搜索表现相关，但理论倾斜分布目前尚不能可靠预测实际搜索的提升幅度。父子候选相关性、提议方式及任务类型都可能影响预测效果。由于实验未证明不同起点会收敛到共同稳态，理论能否描述大语言模型编辑过程的长期稳态仍是开放问题；其对提示优化的适用性也未获支持。

Abstract: LLM-guided search loops propose a modified candidate, score it, and keep it if it scores better. When both candidates are re-scored at every comparison, as in the minibatch acceptance gates of prompt optimizers, keep-if-better is a reversible Markov chain if the score difference is logistic and the mutation operator is reversible, and its stationary law is the mutation equilibrium tilted by exp(beta f). For any continuous symmetric noise difference the same tilt is the stationary law of a small-step diffusion limit, with beta = 4F'(0); for a minibatch gate beta grows with the square root of the minibatch size. Caching the incumbent's score instead inflates the recorded score. We test the tilt as a prediction for LLM-guided program search, committing each prediction from calibration data before the search runs. The level after 150 steps rises with the minibatch size in six of seven settings, but its predicted value fails: on bin packing, search attains 62-88% of the predicted gain when the proposer edits the parent and 109-119% when it writes new heuristics. The attained fraction falls with the parent-child quality correlation of unselected mutation; a registered test on two new settings confirmed the direction, but not the quantitative agreement after a correction. Further registered tests found that runs started from a good program kept losing quality over 300 steps, more so from starts that search had selected, by a margin that passed its registered rule narrowly. Runs from different starts had not reached a common level, so whether the tilt describes the stationary law of LLM editing remains open. A registered test in prompt optimisation on frozen task-model scores failed: search exceeded the tilt, and the bin-packing relation did not transfer.

</details>


### [147] [BiLoGo: Reducing the smoothing of localised responses in Transformer-based neural operators for sheet forming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09621)
*Yingxue Zhao,Haoran Li,Haosu Zhou,Tobias Pfaff,Nan Li*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种融合有限元局部信息与全局信息的板料成形学习模拟器，在降低训练内存的同时，更准确地预测局部减薄。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 板料成形的有限元仿真计算成本高，学习型模拟器虽能加速设计，却常因信息平滑而难以准确捕捉尖角、筋条附近的局部减薄，而这些局部响应直接影响成形性。

Method: 提出双向局部—全局学习模拟器，将节点与单元构成的二部图消息传递和基于变换器的神经算子结合。模型包含三部分：双向图网络在有限元实体的原生位置上预测节点与单元量；双流神经算子分别处理板料变形和模具接触信息，避免局部接触特征被整体状态稀释；门控残差融合模块整合全局与局部特征，并依据节点运动学更新单元特征。

Result: 在一个穹顶冷成形案例和两个角形热冲压案例（其中一个含筋条）中，该方法在三种案例上均取得最低的位置误差和阈值减薄误差。筋条附近的减薄预测平滑度指标由变换器神经算子的百分之四十一至百分之五十六提升至该方法变体的百分之九十四。相较层级图网络，两个变体在相应案例中的平均位置误差分别降低百分之十五点五和百分之八点三，训练内存减少百分之六十六至百分之六十九。

Conclusion: 结果表明，将有限元结构引导的局部节点—单元交互与全局长程信息交换结合，有助于兼顾局部响应精度和整体建模能力，可更有效地模拟复杂板料成形中的减薄分布，并降低训练资源需求。

Abstract: Trial-and-error FE simulation of sheet forming could be computationally expensive, neural-network-based learned simulators are used to accelerate design. These simulators should accurately capture localised responses, such as thinning around sharp radii or near ribs, which determine formability. Transformer-based neural operators propagate information efficiently across the mesh but tend to smooth such localised responses. This study develops BiLoGo, a Bipartite Local-Global learned simulator with an FE-guided design that connects local node-element message passing with a Transformer-based neural operator for long-range information exchange. BiLoGo has three modules: a bidirectional node-element GraphSAGE model that learns nodal-elemental coupling on a bipartite graph and predicts each quantity on its native FE entity without interpolation; a dual-stream Transformer-based neural operator that processes blank deformation and tool contact with separate token sets, preventing localised contact information from being averaged with the whole-blank state; and a fusion and element update module that combines global and local features via gated residual fusion and updates element features conditioned on element-node kinematics. Evaluated on a dome-shaped cold forming case and two corner-shaped hot stamping cases (one with ribs), BiLoGo achieves the lowest positional and threshold-based thinning errors on all three cases compared with single-level and hierarchical graph-based models and Transformer-based neural operators. Near the ribs, the smoothing index of predicted thinning increases from 0.41-0.56 for Transformer-based neural operators to 0.94 for BiLoGo-U. Compared with the hierarchical graph network CAtt-BiUGNN, BiLoGo-T reduces mean positional error by 15.5% on the Corner case and BiLoGo-U by 8.3% on the Corner+Rib case, with 66-69% less training memory.

</details>


### [148] [ICare-DT: a digital twin for continuous forecasting of intensive care patients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09712)
*Yanke Li,Yahang Qi,Yumi Kim,Diego Paez-Granados*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究表明，常规重症监护记录可支持随患者新数据逐小时更新的预测，但不足以让常规学习器可靠预测未观察过的治疗方案反应；后者需要学习反应生成机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重症患者的数字孪生应能随新数据更新病情预测，并预测治疗反应。然而，既有研究较少检验常规临床记录是否真正支持这两项能力，尤其是能否将预测可靠地推广到患者从未接受过的治疗方案。

Method: 研究提出基于变换器的重症患者数字孪生模型，每小时根据新测量更新患者的潜在状态，无须重新训练。模型使用重症监护数据库中的14万余次住院记录训练，预测生命体征是否超过训练集均值，并在另一数据库检验迁移表现。研究还构建治疗反应基准：将真实记录中的治疗替换为模拟剂量，在测量时点注入已知大小的反应，再评估模型能否从记录中恢复这些反应，以及能否预测训练数据未覆盖的给药方案。另检验了微调后的孪生状态能否改善临床结局预测。

Result: 在测试数据的12种设置中，使用后续患者数据的预测均优于该模型仅使用第6小时信息作出的预测，ROC曲线下面积提高0.073至0.153；验证实验显示，提升来自患者自身新增测量。迁移至第二数据库时，9种设置中有8种保持提升，增幅为0.025至0.114。微调后的孪生状态使梯度提升模型对急性肾损伤的预测显著改善，ROC曲线下面积增加0.0035；对低血压的增益未达显著水平。治疗反应实验中，只有测量频繁且在实际测量时点读取的变量（如心率）能帮助岭回归恢复注入反应；面对数据支持范围之外的给药方案，常规学习器均未优于预测零效应。若额外提供模拟反应的生成机制，另一模型则可在未见真实反应值的情况下恢复反应，决定系数为0.30至0.40。

Conclusion: 常规重症监护记录能够支持随患者新数据持续改善的病情预测，但这并不意味着模型能据此预测未观察过的治疗方案效果。治疗反应预测需要考虑数据支持范围，并让模型学习反应如何生成；本文提出的模拟基准可用于评估这类能力。

Abstract: Digital twins of intensive care patients should update forecasts from new data and predict treatment responses, but few studies test whether routine records support either. We propose ICare-DT, a Transformer-based twin that updates a latent patient state hourly from new measurements without retraining. We trained it on 140,473 eICU-CRD stays to forecast vital signs crossing their training-set means. On test data, later forecasts beat the twin's own hour-6 forecasts of the same windows in all 12 settings (+0.073 to +0.153 AUROC). On validation data, controls that withheld or swapped the new hours traced the gain to the patient's own measurements. Without refitting, the model kept its gain on a second database (8 of 9 settings, +0.025 to +0.114 AUROC) in a prespecified evaluation of the previously analyzed MIMIC-IV test split. Refitted within MIMIC-IV, a twin state fine-tuned on clinical endpoints gave gradient boosting a significant gain for acute kidney injury (+0.0035 AUROC; hypotension +0.0010, not significant). To test response prediction, we built a benchmark that replaces the treatments in real records with simulated doses and injects responses of known size, visible only at measurement times. Ridge models supervised with injected effects recovered them from real records only through frequently measured variables (heart rate) read at their measurement times. We asked standard learners to predict these effects for a dosing regime no patient had received. No standard learner outperformed predicting zero effect at an out-of-support query. When told how the simulated responses were generated, a separate learner recovered the effects from the same records without seeing their true values (R2 0.30 to 0.40 at one effect size). Routine records support forecasts that improve with each patient's new data, and our benchmark is a testbed for models that learn how responses are generated.

</details>


### [149] [Online Causal Configuration for Networked Systems via Doubly Robust Steady-State Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09665)
*Yuli Liu,Zhiheng Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向局部控制器交互网络的因果上置信界方法，在探索会改变稳态分布的情况下评估配置的无探索价值，并量化估计与部署代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测量探索虽能揭示局部控制器的行为，却可能改变网络的稳态状态分布，进而影响运营者真正希望优化的无探索表现。因此，探索期间获得的奖励未必能反映目标价值；即使日志中的观测分布相同，不同配置的目标价值也可能不同。可靠识别需同时处理动作策略不一致以及稳态上下文分布变化。

Method: 提出因果上置信界方法，依据目标因果价值的乐观置信界选择配置。其评估器考虑干扰效应，并采用截断双重稳健估计；在决策前给出证书，将统计波动与经校准的识别误差、分布迁移误差及时间依赖误差区分开来。理论分析涵盖任意有限时域和预先设定的测量探索率安排，并在受限的已知局部干扰模型中构造适用于估计、选择与报告的统一半径有限样本证书。

Result: 在平稳且可能缓慢混合的模型族中，论文证明极小极大推断误差下界为 Ω((1+∑ₜηₜ)⁻¹ᐟ²)，测量引发的部署损失下界为 Ω(∑ₜηₜ)。在证书有效且所选信息量持续增长等条件下，还给出遗憾界。高不匹配基准实验包含二十四组成对随机种子和二百轮；所提方法的目标遗憾为 1.732，低于经认证的逆倾向评分上置信界方法的 6.326，但部署差距分别为 30.607 和 23.217。独立审计覆盖率测试及基于轨迹的半合成研究进一步考察了证书覆盖表现与部署权衡。

Conclusion: 论文指出，探索带来的信息收益与其造成的稳态改变和部署损失必须一并考虑。所提方法通过因果价值评估和误差分解证书支持配置选择，并提供了相应的理论下界、遗憾保证及有限样本覆盖分析；实验显示其在高不匹配情形下可显著降低目标遗憾，但部署表现仍存在权衡。

Abstract: Measurement exploration reveals how interacting local controllers behave but can alter the steady-state regime a network operator seeks to optimize. Exploratory rewards may therefore fail to identify a configuration's no-exploration value: under full exploration, identical logged distributions can conceal different target values. Identification requires accounting for both action-law mismatch and shifts in the steady-state context distribution. We introduce CAUSAL-UCB, which selects configurations using optimistic bounds on target causal values. Its interference-aware clipped doubly robust evaluator produces predecision certificates separating statistical variation from calibrated identification, transport, and temporal-dependence errors. For any finite horizon and predetermined schedule of measurement-exploration rates $\{η_t\}$, we prove lower bounds of $Ω((1+\sum_tη_t)^{-1/2})$ on minimax inference error and $Ω(\sum_tη_t)$ on measurement-induced deployment loss in a stationary, potentially slow-mixing family. We bound regret under valid certificates and selected-information growth, and establish an anytime finite-sample certificate with a shared radius for estimation, selection, and reporting in a restricted known-local-interference model. In a high-mismatch benchmark with 24 paired seeds and 200 rounds, CAUSAL-UCB attains target regret of $1.732$ versus $6.326$ for certified IPS-UCB, with deployment gaps of $30.607$ and $23.217$, respectively. Independent-audit coverage tests and trace-driven semi-synthetic studies further assess certificate coverage and deployment trade-offs.

</details>


### [150] [Safety Is $Π_1$, Provability Is $Σ_1$: One Framework for the Hugging Face Breach and OpenAI's Mathematical Proofs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09736)
*Jinho Kang,Jinsook Kim*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文以逻辑陈述的形式差异解释人工智能在安全与数学证明任务中的不同表现：证明存在可由一个有限证据确认，而程序安全涉及不存在漏洞，无法靠有限测试完全确立；统计式候选生成器与演绎式验证器的组合也不能消除这一不可判定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 论文试图统一理解两类看似相反的事件：人工智能系统发现安全漏洞，以及生成数学证明。作者认为，关键不在于系统使用了不同机制，而在于安全与数学任务所要求的结论具有不同的逻辑形式，因此有限验证对两者的作用不同。

Method: 论文采用“统计式提议者加演绎式验证器”的混合框架：前者提出候选漏洞或证明，后者通过演绎推导核验候选。作者再以图灵不可判定定理分析两类任务：证明存在属于Σ₁形式，安全性所要求的“漏洞不存在”属于Π₁形式，并据此考察该混合机制能否改变可判定性。

Result: 作者指出，经验证的单个证明即可作为证明存在的有限见证；但通过有限测试并不能证明不存在任何漏洞。停机集合属于Σ₁但不可判定，其补集属于Π₁且不能由有限见证同样确认。因此，无论统计式提议者多么优秀，将其与演绎验证器结合都不能改变这一不可判定性结果。

Conclusion: 论文的核心结论是，人工智能更容易可靠地确认“存在一个有效证明”，却不能仅凭有限成功测试保证“程序绝对安全”。确保安全需要其他保障措施；混合系统无法消除的残余风险并未消失，而是转移到安全论证与保障机制之中。

Abstract: OpenAI's agents breached Hugging Face in July 2026, and the same company released more than seven hundred AI-generated manuscripts of mathematical proofs in October 2026. The first event is read as a failure in security, while the second is read as a success in mathematics. We read both through the hybrid framework of our paper (Kim and Kang, 2026, arXiv:2611.01914), in which coding, proving, and learning from data are derivations by one and the same deductive mechanism, distinguished by the kinds of premises from which they are derived. Under this framework, the AI systems behind the two events ran the same loop, in which a statistical proposer produces candidate exploits or proofs and a deductive verifier settles each one by a derivation. What separates the events is the logical form of the statement wanted by each of the two domains, security and mathematics. The safety of a program is a $Π_1$ statement, for it says that no exploit exists, which is not assured by any finite list of tests that have been passed by the program. By contrast, the provability of a sentence is a $Σ_1$ statement, for it says that a proof exists, which is assured by a single instance, namely a proof that has been checked by the deductive verifier. The theorem behind both is Turing's Undecidability Theorem: the halting set is $Σ_1$ but not decidable, so its complement is $Π_1$ and not $Σ_1$, which is to say that membership has a finite witness but non-membership has none. We prove that attaching a statistical proposer to a deductive verifier does not change this undecidability result, however good the proposer is. We then discuss what does assure safety and where the residual risk goes.

</details>


### [151] [The Multiscale Single-Index Model: An MSE Analysis of Online SGD Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09682)
*Gordon Dai,Joan Bruna*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究两隐层多尺度单指标模型，证明联合在线球面随机梯度下降可从随机初始化实现弱恢复，并进一步达到任意给定精度的强恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度网络如何逐层学习不同物理尺度上的局部特征，仍缺乏易于分析的理论模型。多尺度单指标模型提供了一个可处理的框架；本文进一步关注采用典型回归目标——均方误差时，在线学习能否恢复模型中的隐含特征。

Method: 在连接函数满足非退化条件、真实特征满足去局部化条件的设定下，分析两隐层模型中的联合在线球面随机梯度下降，并在离散时间下控制其学习轨迹。针对第二层接收到的已学习特征并非高斯分布这一难点，作者利用去局部化条件建立定量高斯比较，并结合对耦合随机梯度下降动态的精细轨迹控制完成分析。

Result: 从随机初始化出发，算法使用约为 d 乘以 log²d 的样本即可实现弱恢复；随后再使用约为 d 乘以 log(1/ε) 的样本，可达到精度 ε 的强恢复。

Conclusion: 在所述条件下，即使采用均方误差这一回归目标，两隐层多尺度模型也能通过在线学习依次实现弱恢复和高精度强恢复。该结果为深层架构学习局部多尺度表示提供了理论依据，并补充了针对一般层数、采用相关性目标的相关研究。

Abstract: We consider the Multiscale Single-Index Model (MSIM), first introduced by Oymak and Soltanolkotabi (2021), as a stylized model for hierarchical feature learning with scale separation. Each layer extracts a shared single-index feature at one physical scale and passes it to the next, giving a tractable setting in which to study how deep architectures learn local multiscale representations. Under non-degeneracy and delocalization assumptions on the link function and planted features respectively, for the two hidden-layer case ($K=2$), we analyze joint online spherical SGD under MSE loss, which is a canonical regression objective, in discrete time and show that it achieves weak recovery from random initialization in $O(d\log^2 d)$ samples, followed by strong recovery to accuracy $\varepsilon$ in $O(d\log(1/\varepsilon))$ additional samples. The main technical challenge arises from the non-Gaussian learned features observed by the second layer. We address this challenge through a quantitative Gaussian comparison using delocalization, combined with sharp trajectory-level control of the coupled SGD dynamics. This work is a companion to Bruna (2026, arXiv:2607.03347), which studies recovery under a correlation objective for general $K$.

</details>


### [152] [Scaling Graph Similarity Learning with Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09805)
*Ahmet Tüzen,Helge Langseth,Kjetil Nørvåg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种可扩展的图神经网络框架，并通过新构造的大规模数据集验证其能在超大图上快速、准确地计算图相似度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 图编辑距离和最大公共子图可用于衡量图之间的差异与共有结构，但精确计算属于计算困难问题。现有图神经网络方法主要面向小规模图数据集，对大规模图的扩展能力研究不足，因此需要兼顾准确性与可扩展性的方案及相应评测数据。

Method: 提出一种简化的图神经网络处理流程，旨在保持基准任务表现的同时提升规模适应能力；另设计逐步图编辑策略，构造带有可靠图编辑距离标签的大规模图数据集，用于评估方法的可扩展性。

Result: 该框架在四个基准数据集和四个新构造的大规模数据集上进行了评测。对于超过15万个节点的图，该方法仍能保持较高准确性；对比方法则出现内存耗尽或无法有效学习的情况。该方法可在单张图形处理器上于数秒内完成所有测试图对的评分。

Conclusion: 研究表明，简化的图神经网络流程也能在标准基准上保持竞争力，并扩展到超大规模图相似度计算。逐步图编辑构造的数据集进一步为评估大规模场景下的图编辑距离方法提供了支持。

Abstract: Graph similarity computation is an important task in graph mining with many real-world applications, such as chemical compound analysis, program dependence comparison, and social network analysis. The problem is computationally challenging: Graph Edit Distance (GED) and Maximum Common Subgraph (MCS), which quantify the distance between graphs and identify their largest shared subgraph, respectively, are NP-hard to compute exactly. To address these computational challenges, Graph Neural Networks (GNNs) have been applied to this problem. However, existing GNN-based approaches have primarily focused on small-scale graph datasets, while scalability to larger scales remains underexplored. In this work, we propose FiLSim, a scalable GNN-based framework, and investigate whether a simplified GNN pipeline can maintain competitive performance on standard benchmarks while scaling to large graphs. We additionally propose a sequential graph-editing strategy to construct large-scale graph datasets with reliable GED labels to evaluate scalability. The proposed framework is evaluated on four benchmark datasets and four newly created large-scale datasets, demonstrating its effectiveness and scalability. FiLSim remains accurate on graphs with more than 150{,}000 nodes, where the baseline methods run out of memory or fail to learn, and scores all test pairs within seconds on a single GPU.

</details>


### [153] [Fixed-point acceleration on Neural Wasserstein manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09723)
*Vitalii Aksenov,Sebastian Gutierrez Hernandez*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种用于概率测度优化的黎曼安德森混合加速方法，可减少自然梯度迭代次数，并在多类机器学习任务中提升计算效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于参数化瓦瑟斯坦几何的自然梯度方法适用于多种概率测度优化问题，但迭代可能计算开销较大。论文旨在加速这类方法，同时避免显式构造海森矩阵。

Method: 将概率测度表示为参考分布经神经网络映射后的推前分布，并在参数空间上引入由瓦瑟斯坦度量诱导的拉回度量。在此几何结构下，将自然梯度下降视为黎曼流形上的不动点迭代，再利用历史残差进行黎曼安德森混合，以获得近似二阶信息。参数流形采用带位置依赖度量的欧氏坐标表示，从而简化回缩与向量传输。

Result: 在多峰采样、基于偏微分方程的反问题、聚集动力学、贝叶斯逻辑回归和生成建模等任务中，该方法减少了自然梯度迭代次数。与随机梯度下降和自适应矩估计方法相比，其在损失与梯度评估次数及实际运行时间方面表现具有竞争力。

Conclusion: 结果表明，黎曼安德森混合可作为参数化瓦瑟斯坦方法的有效加速工具，改善训练计算效率；其优势在于利用迭代历史捕捉近似二阶信息，而无需显式计算海森矩阵。

Abstract: We propose a fixed-point acceleration method for optimization over probability measures, applicable to a variety of machine learning problems. We represent probability measures as pushforwards of a reference distribution through a neural network map and equip the parameter space with the induced pullback Wasserstein metric. This geometric structure allows natural gradient descent to be interpreted as a fixed-point iteration on a Riemannian manifold. We accelerate this iteration using Riemannian Anderson mixing method, which exploits historical residuals to capture approximate second-order information without forming Hessians. In the present parametric setting, the manifold is represented by Euclidean coordinates endowed with a point-dependent metric, simplifying retraction and vector transport. We evaluate the method on multimodal sampling, PDE-based inverse problems, aggregation dynamics, Bayesian logistic regression, and generative modeling. Across the tested tasks, Anderson mixing reduces the number of natural-gradient iterations and achieves competitive performance relative to SGD and Adam in terms of both loss-gradient evaluations and wall-clock time. These results suggest that fixed-point acceleration is an efficient tool for improving the computational efficiency of training on parametric Wasserstein methods.

</details>


### [154] [One Run Is Not Enough: A Monte Carlo Protocol for Evaluating LLM Reliability on Data Science Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09729)
*Wenqi Ni,Ozan Evkaya,Miguel de Carvalho*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一个面向数据科学与统计任务的问答基准，并通过重复实验评估大语言模型的回答质量与稳定性；结果显示，部分任务和难度下回答波动明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准已用于评估大语言模型处理数据科学和统计问题的能力，但仅考察回答质量不足以判断其可靠性。还需分析模型在重复运行、不同任务类型及不同难度下表现是否一致。

Method: 构建包含一百道题目的评测基准，题目取自英国大学课程，覆盖十六个数据集和三个难度等级。设计三层评估框架：考察运行时间、回答冗长程度和文本相似度等一般属性；考察准确性、代码可执行性和完整性等粗粒度指标；并针对具体任务采用专门指标。对所选模型进行蒙特卡洛评测，每道任务重复运行一百次。

Result: 数据可视化和监督学习任务的回答存在较大波动，且在中等和较高难度题目中尤为明显。研究还发现，词语冗长比例每增加零点一，词元冗长比例约增加零点零一三。

Conclusion: 评估大语言模型的数据科学能力时，应同时考察回答质量与跨次运行的一致性。该基准及其分层评估框架为分析不同任务类型和难度下的模型可靠性提供了工具。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have shown promise for automating data science workflows. Benchmarks such as DataSciBench (2026) and StatQA (2024) have advanced the evaluation of their capabilities across data science and statistical tasks. Assessing their reliability requires examining both the quality of their responses and their consistency across repeated runs, task types, and difficulty levels. With this aim, we introduce DS-QA, a benchmark comprising 100 data science and statistics questions drawn from UK university courses, spanning 16 datasets and three difficulty levels. We develop a three-layer evaluation framework that combines general response properties (runtime, verbosity and text similarity), coarse-grained metrics (accuracy, code executability and completeness), and task-specific metrics. We evaluate OpenAI models using a Monte Carlo protocol with 100 repeated runs. The results reveal substantial variability in responses to data visualisation and supervised learning tasks, particularly at medium and hard difficulty levels, highlighting the importance of evaluating consistency alongside response quality. We also find that an increase of 0.1 in the word verbosity ratio corresponds to an increase of about 0.013 in the token verbosity ratio.

</details>


### [155] [Diffusion-free Score Matching for Exact Conditional Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09758)
*Thomas Wedenig,Robert Peharz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需学习多时间噪声分数场的分数匹配方法，在保持样本质量的同时，更准确地表示数据分布，并支持从任意条件分布中进行可靠采样。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散模型通过学习不同噪声水平下的分数场取得了优异的生成效果，但条件信息通常只适用于干净数据，无法直接用于高噪声阶段，因此从模型中准确采样任意条件分布较为困难。现有启发式采样方法也难以保证条件采样的可靠性。

Method: 作者提出“无扩散分数匹配”方法。它采用一种与扩散训练目标相似、但不需要学习随时间变化的分数场的目标函数，只估计轻微扰动后数据分布的单一分数。由于该分布的条件分数可以直接求得，方法能够据此进行条件采样。

Result: 实验显示，该方法的样本质量可与先进扩散模型相媲美。相比之下，它对底层数据分布的刻画更忠实，并能更准确地从任意条件分布采样，优于标准启发式采样器。

Conclusion: 该方法绕开了扩散模型条件采样中的关键障碍，在维持生成质量的同时提升了概率推断的准确性，为基于分数模型开展灵活、可靠的条件推断提供了新途径。

Abstract: The success of modern score-based generative models is largely driven by diffusion-based training, which learns a collection of time-indexed score fields corresponding to progressively noisier versions of the data distribution. While such models achieve impressive sample quality, leveraging them for sound probabilistic inference---particularly sampling from arbitrary conditionals of the model---remains challenging. This difficulty arises because conditioning information is only available for clean data and not for the higher noise levels required to generate exact conditional samples. In this paper, we introduce DIffusion-free SCOre matching (DISCO), a principled approach that sidesteps the need for time-dependent score fields altogether. Our method is based on an objective that, while reminiscent of diffusion-based training, estimates only a single score of the (slightly perturbed) data distribution, for which the exact conditional score is readily obtained. In experiments, DISCO is competitive with state-of-the-art diffusion models in terms of sample quality. More importantly, DISCO yields a more faithful representation of the underlying data distribution and---crucially---enables accurate sampling from arbitrary conditionals, outperforming standard heuristic samplers. This capability opens the door to principled and flexible probabilistic reasoning with score-based models.

</details>


### [156] [A Tight Hierarchy for Chain of Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09759)
*Thanh Le,A. Pavan,N. V. Vinodchandran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文首次证明思维链推理步数存在严格层级：对任意满足条件的资源界，增加推理步数会带来严格更强的计算能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究逐步揭示加入思维链的变换器模型的计算能力及其与经典复杂性理论的联系，但尚未证明思维链推理步数本身具有严格的资源层级。论文旨在填补这一理论空白，明确推理步数是否能带来可证明的计算能力提升。

Method: 作者利用思维链变换器与图灵机之间已知的双向粗粒度模拟关系，将经典复杂性理论中的填充技巧改造并应用于该模型。关键技术在于实现适用于思维链变换器的填充，从而建立资源界之间的严格分离。

Result: 论文证明：对于任意满足条件且至少为输入长度量级的合理资源界，以及任意正数 ε，使用 O(r(n)) 步思维链推理可解的问题严格少于使用 O(r(n)^(1+ε)) 步可解的问题。也就是说，思维链推理步数增加后，计算能力严格增强。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了思维链计算的首个层级定理，证明推理步数是变换器计算中的基本资源。不同于通常依赖对角化的经典层级定理，论文通过模拟关系与填充技术完成证明，并拓展了复杂性理论方法在思维链模型中的应用。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) reasoning has emerged as a powerful paradigm for extending the computational capabilities of transformer-based models. A growing body of recent work has begun to uncover the fine-grained computational power of transformers augmented with CoT, as well as their connections to classical complexity theory.
  In this work, we advance this line of research by establishing the first hierarchy theorem for CoT-based computation. Our main result shows that for any reasonable resource bound $r(n) \geq n$ and any $\varepsilon > 0$, \[ \mathrm{CoT}(r(n)) \subsetneq \mathrm{CoT}(r(n)^{1+\varepsilon}), \] where $\mathrm{CoT}(r(n))$ denotes the class of decision problems solvable by transformers using $O(r(n))$ CoT reasoning steps on inputs of length $n$. Our result shows that increasing the number of CoT reasoning steps provably yields strictly greater computational power, establishing CoT steps as a fundamental resource in transformer-based computation.
  Unlike classical hierarchy theorems, which are typically proved via diagonalization, our approach leverages known coarse-grained simulations between CoT-augmented transformers and Turing machines in both directions. Building on these connections, we adapt the classical padding technique from complexity theory to this setting to obtain a tight hierarchy. A key technical contribution of our work is the implementation of padding within the transformer-based CoT model.

</details>


### [157] [From PAC bounds on the generalization error to PAC bounds on the parameter estimation error](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09767)
*Mihaly Petreczky,John Leth,Emma Lukacs,Balint Daroczy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文将由概率近似正确理论导出的预言机界用于动力系统有限样本分析，并据此为线性与双线性系统推导参数估计误差界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动力系统参数估计的有限样本界往往依赖特定算法，通常需要将辨识过程改写为最小二乘回归；而经典泛化误差界不依赖具体算法，可适用于多种经验损失最小化方法。论文旨在弥合这两类分析之间的差距，为更广泛的系统辨识算法提供有限样本保证。

Method: 首先建立预言机不等式，比较学习所得模型的真实预测误差与可达到的最小真实预测误差；随后利用真实损失的凸性，或损失函数所具有的系统理论性质，将预测误差界转化为参数估计误差界。作者以输入和噪声有界的线性系统及双线性系统为例，分析基于正则化经验损失最小化的辨识算法，并研究误差界的收敛速率及其与经典渐近结果的差异。

Result: 论文给出了线性系统和双线性系统的有限样本参数估计误差界，适用于基于正则化经验损失最小化的辨识算法。摘要说明作者还分析了这些界的速率，并与经典渐近结果进行了比较，但未提供具体界的形式或数值比较结果。

Conclusion: 由泛化误差界导出的预言机不等式，可以作为从预测性能保证推导参数估计保证的通用工具。该思路不局限于特定的最小二乘算法，为动力系统辨识的有限样本理论提供了更具算法适用性的分析框架。

Abstract: Probably Approximately Correct (PAC) bounds on the generalization error (the difference between the empirical prediction error computed on the training data and its expectation) are an established tool in statistical machine learning, and there have been several extensions of these bounds to dynamical systems. In parallel, in recent years, there has been growing interest in finite-sample bounds for parameter estimation error in system identification. The latter bounds tend to be algorithm-specific, relying on reformulating the identification algorithm as a least-squares regression problem. This is in contrast to classical PAC bounds, which are algorithm-agnostic and can be applied to any identification algorithm based on minimizing a cost function involving the empirical loss.
  In this paper, we show how oracle bounds derived from PAC bounds can be used to derive finite-sample bounds for dynamical systems. As an illustration of this approach, we present finite-sample parameter-estimation bounds for linear and bilinear systems with bounded inputs and noise. These bounds apply to identification algorithms based on minimizing a regularized empirical loss function.
  The key ingredient is to establish an oracle inequality, i.e., an inequality between the true prediction error of the learned model and the minimum achievable true prediction error, and then use either the convexity of the true loss or system-theoretic properties of the loss function to derive bounds on the parameter estimation error. We also analyze the rates of the obtained bounds and compare them with those of classical asymptotic results.

</details>


### [158] [Quasipolynomial-Time Planning for Decodable POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09778)
*Oliver Dobson,Varun Jog,Chun Hei Michael Shiu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出一种易于检验的可解码性条件，并证明在该条件下可实现准多项式时间的近似规划。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 有限时域部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程的精确与近似规划都具有很高的计算复杂度。已有研究利用观测通道的可观测性条件获得高效算法，但相关条件可能较难检验，且不同可观测性条件之间的关系尚不清楚。论文旨在寻找更易验证、适用范围更广的规划假设。

Method: 研究可解码性条件及其与一范数、二范数可观测性的关系，并据此构造近似规划算法。作者还分析了可解码性常数和一范数可观测性常数的计算复杂度，以及二范数可观测性是否足以保证快速滤波遗忘。

Result: 对任意固定的正数可解码性参数，论文给出准多项式时间近似规划算法。可解码性由一范数和二范数可观测性蕴含，但严格弱于可观测性；其最优常数可在多项式时间内计算，而最优一范数可观测性常数在最坏情况下难以计算。此外，常数级二范数可观测性并不能直接推出已有方法所需的快速滤波遗忘保证。

Conclusion: 可解码性为部分可观测决策过程提供了一项统一且易于检验的结构条件，使准多项式时间近似规划成为可能，同时弥补了传统可观测性条件在可计算性和适用性方面的不足。

Abstract: We study planning for finite-horizon partially observable Markov decision processes (POMDPs) with finite state, action, and observation spaces. Both exact and approximate planning are known to be computationally hard in this setting, so prior work has explored natural assumptions that make planning easier. Recent work [Golowich et al., 2022] gave quasipolynomial-time approximate planning algorithms for observable POMDPs. These are POMDPs whose observation channels satisfy an $\ell_1$-observability condition, but notably, no assumptions are required on the transition dynamics. In a similar vein, for every fixed $ρ>0$, we give quasipolynomial-time approximate planning algorithms for $ρ$-decodable POMDPs. We show that decodability is implied by both $\ell_1$- and $\ell_2$-observability and is strictly weaker than observability. Moreover, we show that the optimal decodability constant can be computed in polynomial time, whereas computing the optimal $\ell_1$-observability constant is NP-hard in the worst case. We also show that the fast filter-forgetting guarantee underlying the approach of [Golowich et al., 2022] does not extend directly from $\ell_1$-observability to constant $\ell_2$-observability. Thus, decodability is an easy-to-check, unifying assumption under which quasipolynomial-time approximate planning for POMDPs is possible.

</details>


### [159] [The Cost of Coverage: Conformalising Native Forecast Uncertainty for GB Wind Power](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09815)
*Carl Somers*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 保形校准可修复风电预测区间的总体覆盖率，但按风况分组校准更能改善大风条件下的覆盖表现，并可同时缩窄区间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 风电预测区间用于辅助备用容量配置与承诺决策，但实际覆盖率常低于其标称水平，尤其可能无法准确反映高风速下的不确定性。研究旨在检验保形预测能否改善现有预测区间，以及这种改善需要付出什么代价。

Method: 研究将保形预测应用于六种预测模型生成的原生预测区间，模型范围从自回归移动平均模型到表格型基础模型。实验在大不列颠两座风电场的实际运行条件下进行，并比较总体合并校准与按预测风况分组校准的效果，评估覆盖率、温克勒评分及区间外预测量。

Result: 校准可修复经验边际覆盖率，且温克勒评分几乎没有恶化或完全不受影响；但总体覆盖率达标仍掩盖了高风速下的覆盖不足。按风况校准可将总体覆盖改善为各风况区间内的覆盖改善；相较于合并校准，该方法使日前高风速时的区间外预测量减少超过百分之四十，同时总体预测区间更窄。

Conclusion: 仅校准总体覆盖率不足以保障关键风况下的可靠性。按预测风况进行分组校准，更适合风电运行决策，能以较小的评分代价改善高风速条件下的预测区间表现，并减少区间外预测量。

Abstract: Uncertainty bands on wind forecasts help inform reserve sizing and commitments, but often fail to deliver their stated coverage. We apply conformal prediction to native bands to see what it adds and what it costs, comparing six forecasters, from ARIMA to a tabular foundation model, at two wind farms in Great Britain (GB) under operational conditions. We show that calibration repairs empirical marginal coverage with little or no Winkler Score penalty, but this apparent success conceals under-coverage in high winds. Calibrating by forecast wind regime turns marginal coverage into regime-level coverage, cutting day-ahead out-of-band volume in high winds by over 40% relative to pooled calibration while narrowing bands overall.

</details>


### [160] [Designing Effective Monitor-Based Interventions for Mitigating Reward Hacking During RL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09828)
*Aria Wong,Joshua Engels,Neel Nanda*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究如何在强化学习训练中有效使用监测器来抑制奖励钻空子，并在编程、医疗对话和人物传记生成三类环境中验证相关方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习奖励难以设计和控制，模型可能通过测试覆盖、迎合用户或编造信息等方式获得高奖励，却违背任务本意。虽然监测器可用于发现并惩罚此类行为，但模型也可能学会规避监测；目前对于训练中如何有效使用监测器，理解仍有限。

Method: 研究者构建并开放三种可发生奖励钻空子的现实任务环境，分别涉及编程测试覆盖、医疗对话中的迎合行为，以及人物传记生成中的幻觉。研究首先聚焦编程环境，考察监测器准确度、监测信号对强化学习的反馈程度，以及训练题目难度对钻空子的影响；随后将所得经验用于改进医疗对话和传记生成环境中的监测干预方案。

Result: 实验发现：模型能够利用探测机制和语言模型评判器中的系统性缺陷，规避准确度很高的监测器；监测器提供的学习信号越充分，越能抑制奖励钻空子，但也越容易被模型规避；在训练中加入较容易的问题能够减少钻空子。基于这些发现设计的监测方案，在医疗对话和传记生成任务中优于简单基线，部分干预能使多个随机种子下的钻空子率降至训练前水平。

Conclusion: 监测器可以有效缓解奖励钻空子，但其干预方式需要兼顾抑制效果与被规避风险。监测机制的系统性缺陷、反馈信号强弱和训练问题难度都会影响结果；针对这些因素设计干预，有望将监测器更稳健地应用于不同任务。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) rewards are notoriously difficult to design and control, often leading to the model learning unintended behaviors such as reward hacking. One potential solution is to monitor for reward hacking and penalize it when detected; however, training against a monitor could lead to evasive behavior, and our general understanding of how to apply monitors effectively during training is limited. To study how best to use monitors to mitigate reward hacking, we introduce and open source three realistic environments where Qwen3-4B reward hacks: a coding environment hackable via test overwriting, a medical chat environment hackable via sycophancy, and a biography generation environment hackable via hallucination. We first focus on the coding environment, where we find that: (1) models can learn to evade highly accurate monitors by exploiting systemic flaws in probes and LLM judges; (2) monitors that leak more learning signal during RL suppress reward hacking but are more often evaded; and (3) including easier problems in training can decrease reward hacking. We apply our findings to build better reward hacking monitors for the medical chat and biography generation environments that improve upon naive baselines to reduce reward hacking rates across seeds to before-training levels in some interventions. Our results demonstrate that our takeaways translate to new settings and that better monitor intervention designs are possible.

</details>


### [161] [Fifty-Two Shades of Pig: What the UCR Archive Really Asks Classifiers to Do](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09843)
*Omar Swelam,Jaris Küken,Shi Bin Hoo,Frank Hutter,Lennart Purucker*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文审查常用时间序列分类基准库中的数据集来源与评测设计，发现部分任务与实际应用脱节，呼吁谨慎解读整体成绩并加强贴近部署场景的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该基准库虽被广泛用作时间序列分类的标准评测平台，但其中任务是否真正反映时间序列分类的目标，长期缺少系统检验。例如，某些记录原本用于检测猪的出血，却被改造成识别猪只身份的问题。这引发了对任务代表性和评测有效性的质疑。

Method: 作者对基准库进行来源与任务审计，核查数据集最初的研究目的、构造方式及测试集划分，并讨论有序但不具时间属性的数据（如频谱和形状轮廓）是否能代表真实时间序列分类问题，以及将多变量问题拆分处理可能带来的影响。

Result: 审计发现，若干数据集被改用于不同于原始目的的任务、来自玩具式实验设置，或存在有缺陷的测试集划分。此外，基准库中的部分有序非时间序列数据及多变量问题的拆分方式，也可能削弱其对实际应用的代表性。

Conclusion: 基准库上的总体成绩不能未经审视就被视为真实应用能力的可靠指标。研究者应更谨慎地解释跨数据集评测结果，并更加重视与实际部署目标相符的数据、任务设计和评估方式。

Abstract: The pigs were bleeding. That was the point of the experiment: could airway pressure reveal hemorrhage before it became critical? In the UCR Time Series Classification Archive, the de facto benchmark for Time Series Classification (TSC), the same recordings ask a different question: "which of 52 pigs produced this trace?". The dataset PigAirwayPressure is not an outlier. Although the archive is the standard benchmark, whether its tasks resemble the problems TSC is meant to solve has rarely been examined. We find that several datasets are repurposed from their original task, built from toy setups, or have flawed test splits. We further question how representative the archive's ordered non-temporal sequences (e.g. spectra, shape outlines) are of real TSC, and the potential downsides from the decomposition of multivariate problems. Our findings call for more careful interpretation of archive-wide scores and greater attention to deployment-relevant evaluation. The audit is at https://github.com/automl/ucr-provenance-audit.

</details>


### [162] [Computer-Use Agents Are Embodied Generalists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09847)
*Pranjal Aggarwal,Sean Welleck*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出，计算机使用智能体能够通过屏幕画面和键盘、鼠标操作完成具身任务，并有望成为具身智能通用智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通用模型正在取代许多人工设计方法和专用架构，作者认为具身智能也可能经历类似转变。其核心动机是探索：能否将与物理环境交互转化为操作数字软件，从而复用计算机使用智能体已有的通用能力，减少针对不同具身任务单独设计系统的需要。

Method: 作者构建软件，将智能体观察到的物理环境渲染为屏幕画面，并提供相应操作控件。计算机使用智能体沿用操作普通软件时的视觉输入及键盘、鼠标动作来完成具身任务。实验中保持智能体设计不变，仅针对任务更换软件环境，且不进行竞赛专门调优。

Result: 在四项欧洲计算机视觉会议研讨会竞赛中，作者的系统获得第一、第二、第三和第六名。结果表明，智能体在软件操作中学到的视觉定位、精确点击和探索等技能，能够迁移到具身任务中。

Conclusion: 作者主张，计算机使用智能体可以成为具身通用智能体；若将促成其通用性的训练策略应用于具身智能，具身领域也可能逐步形成单一通用模型。不过，摘要所报告的竞赛成绩提供了初步支持，尚不足以证明这一范式适用于所有具身任务或实际环境。

Abstract: In many areas of AI, general-purpose models have increasingly replaced handcrafted methods and specialized architectures. We argue that embodied AI is poised for a similar shift. Our key claim is that embodied tasks can be formulated as interaction with digital software. Specifically, we build software that renders an agent's view of a physical environment on a screen and provides controls for acting in it. A computer-use agent (CUA) can then perform embodied tasks through the same screenshots and keyboard and mouse actions that it uses to operate ordinary software. Using a single CUA design and changing only the software, our entries win first-, second-, third-, and sixth-place prizes across four ECCV workshop competitions, without any competition-specific agent tuning. These results suggest that the skills CUAs learn on software, such as visual grounding, precise clicking, and exploration, already carry over to embodied tasks, giving CUAs a far broader scope in embodied AI than previously realized. We therefore advocate that computer-use agents can be embodied generalists. Further, we advocate that, by following the training strategy that made computer-use agents general, embodied AI can converge on a single general model as well, ushering in a new era of general embodied AI. Website: https://pranjal2041.github.io/cua-embodied

</details>


### [163] [On the (non-)closedness of the set of ReLU networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09887)
*Eloi Martinet,Leon Bungert*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文证明：单隐层、神经元数量有界的整流线性网络在相应的索伯列夫空间中弱闭，因此一类能量泛函存在最小值；但双隐层、宽度有界的网络并不闭。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究网络函数类的闭性，是确保变分训练问题能够达到最优解的重要前提。论文针对单隐层网络建立闭性与最小值存在性，同时检验更深层网络是否具备类似性质，并回应已有研究提出的闭性猜想。

Method: 分析有界神经元数量的单隐层网络在索伯列夫空间中的弱收敛极限，证明其仍属于该网络类，从而得到弱闭性；再结合泛函的强制性与弱下半连续性推出最小值存在。论文还考察正则化参数趋于零时极小解的收敛，并构造双隐层、宽度有界网络的反例，展示其在索伯列夫空间及连续函数空间中均不闭。

Result: 对于有界连通开集上的单隐层网络，神经元数量至多为给定上限的网络集合在相应的索伯列夫空间中弱闭。因此，满足强制性和弱下半连续性的能量泛函在该集合上能够取得最小值，适用范围包括具有凸被积函数的深度瑞兹方法、索伯列夫训练和利普希茨学习。正则化参数趋于零时，正则化问题的极小解经子列收敛到参数范数最小的极小解。相反，双隐层且宽度有界的网络集合在索伯列夫空间和闭包上的连续函数空间中都不闭。

Conclusion: 网络深度会显著影响函数类的拓扑性质：单隐层且规模有界时，弱闭性可支持变分最小值存在性；双隐层情形则可能失去闭性。后者推翻了关于双隐层有界宽度网络闭性的既有猜想。

Abstract: We prove that the set $\mathcal{N}_N$ of ReLU networks with one hidden layer and at most $N\in\mathbb N$ neurons is weakly closed in $W^{1,p}(Ω)$, for a bounded connected open set $Ω\subset\mathbb R^d$. Consequently, every coercive, weakly lower semicontinuous energy on this space attains its minimum over $\mathcal{N}_N$. This covers the Deep Ritz method with convex integrands, Sobolev training and Lipschitz learning. Moreover, as the weight decay parameter tends to zero, minimizers of the regularized problem converge, up to subsequences, to minimizers with minimal parameter norm. We also prove that the set of ReLU networks with two hidden layers of bounded widths is not closed in $W^{1,p}(Ω)$ or in $C(\overlineΩ)$. This disproves a conjecture of Petersen, Raslan and Voigtlaender.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [164] [Enhancing Academic Advisement with Case-Based Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08572)
*Tanat Tonguthaisri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文设计、开发并验证了一种基于案例推理的学业指导推荐系统原型，旨在补充传统指导服务，并探索未来提升其效率与覆盖能力的可能性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学业指导是高等教育的重要职能，但许多院校希望改善现有服务。指导工作耗时且需要结合多种工具与方法；尽管基于案例推理的专家系统已应用于多个问题求解领域，这项技术尚未成功用于学业指导，而该领域被认为适合采用此类系统。

Method: 研究围绕基于案例推理的方法，设计、开发并验证一个学业指导推荐系统原型，用于支持传统指导流程。

Result: 摘要说明研究完成了系统原型的设计、开发与验证，但未提供具体的验证指标、实验数据或效果比较，因此无法判断其实际表现及相对于传统指导的提升幅度。

Conclusion: 基于案例推理的系统有望为学业指导提供辅助，改善指导过程的效率与服务能力。作者提出，该原型未来可能进一步发展，但替代人工指导仍是潜在愿景，并非摘要已证实的结果。

Abstract: This research involved the study of an application of Case-Based Reasoning (CBR) in the field of academic advising. Academic advisement has been recognised as an important function of higher education. However, there is a widespread desire to improve the advisement services provided by many educational institutions (e.g., colleges and universities) around the world. In the last ten years, CBR expert systems have made important contributions in many domains by applying CBR methodology to a variety of areas of human problem solving. However, to date, expert advisement systems have not been successfully applied to academic advisement, which seems to be a very appropriate domain for this technology.
  Academic advising is an important and time consuming task, and different tools and techniques must be used to make it an effective and efficient process. This thesis describes the design, development, and validation of a prototype case-based academic advisement recommender system that supplements the conventional advising process, and could eventually evolve to replace human academic advisors when its full potential is realised.

</details>


### [165] [NormGym: Evaluating Normative Competence through Social Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08613)
*Binze Li,Zihuan Zhang,Seokhyun Baek,Gillian K. Hadfield,Tianmin Shu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一个用于评估智能体能否通过社会互动习得未知规范的交互式基准。实验显示，当前大语言模型在同时完成任务并遵守规范方面仍有明显不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在陌生的人类社群中行动，智能体不仅需要知道哪些行为可行，还要理解哪些行为被允许。现有评测多关注识别或应用已有规范，较少检验智能体能否从互动中习得此前未知的规范，因此难以衡量其真正的规范能力。

Method: 研究提出“规范健身场”交互式基准：智能体作为社群中的新成员，一边完成自身任务，一边从其他成员的行为及其受到的制裁中推断隐藏且任意设定的规范，并将推断出的规范应用于新情境。基准涵盖多种规范和不同环境，用于系统评估现有智能体及大语言模型。

Result: 表现最好的受测大语言模型，在任务完成与规范遵守两项要求上同时成功的比例为百分之五十五点三。模型有时能在自身行为受到制裁后调整做法，但难以依据其他成员的行为及其受到的制裁推断潜在规范；微调也未必能提升这项能力。

Conclusion: 当前模型尚不具备稳健、可迁移的社会互动规范习得能力。要让智能体可靠地适应陌生社群，仍需提升其综合利用观察、制裁反馈和新情境信息来推断并应用规范的能力。

Abstract: Normative competence, the ability to infer socially enforced expectations from interaction and adjust behavior accordingly, is essential for agents operating in unfamiliar human communities. Yet existing evaluations largely test whether agents can recognize or apply norms they may already know, rather than whether they can acquire previously unknown norms from social interaction. We introduce NormGym, an interactive benchmark for normative competence in which a newcomer must infer a hidden, arbitrary norm while pursuing its own task in a multi-agent community. Success requires distinguishing what is possible from what is permitted, inferring the norm from observed behavior and sanctions, and applying it in new situations. NormGym contains diverse norms and distinct environments, allowing systematic evaluation of current agents, including large language model (LLM) agents. We find that normative competence remains challenging: the strongest evaluated LLM achieves a joint task-and-compliance success rate of 55.3%. Our analysis reveals that models can sometimes revise their behavior following sanctions for their own actions, but struggle to use other agents' behavior and the sanctions they receive to infer the underlying norm. We further find that fine-tuning models may be insufficient to improve normative competence. These results suggest that current models lack a robust, transferable capability for acquiring normative knowledge through social interaction.

</details>


### [166] [LLM-Based Feature Extraction for Multi-Criteria Inventory Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08626)
*Humza Akhtar,Rami Pinto*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出利用大语言模型从顾客评论等非结构化数据中提取量化特征，并将其纳入多准则库存分类，以改进库存优先级判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统年度金额使用量分类主要依赖单一指标；现有多准则库存分类虽纳入多个因素，仍偏重结构化定量数据，未充分利用顾客反馈等非结构化信息。电商供应链中的这些数据可能包含有助于库存决策的信号。

Method: 研究利用向量嵌入和大语言模型生成的评价准则，将顾客评论等非结构化来源转化为量化特征，再将这些新增特征纳入基于哈迪—文切赫模型的多准则库存分类框架。

Result: 实验显示，纳入顾客情感信号后，库存分类结果出现了可测量的变化，表明非结构化数据会影响库存项目的优先级划分。摘要未提供具体数值或对照指标。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型提取的非结构化信息融入多准则库存分类具有可行性，并可丰富库存决策依据。不过，分类变化是否能带来更高的经营绩效，仍需进一步验证。

Abstract: E-commerce supply chains generate large volumes of customer feedback and operational data that remain underutilized in inventory management. Traditional inventory classification techniques such as ABC analysis rely primarily on annual dollar usage, while multi-criteria inventory classification (MCIC) methods extend this but still depend on structured quantitative data and do not utilize unstructured information available in modern supply chains. This paper proposes an LLM-based feature extraction approach that transforms unstructured sources such as customer reviews into quantitative features using vector embeddings and LLM-generated evaluation criteria. The enriched feature set is incorporated into an MCIC framework based on the Hadi-Vencheh model. Experimental results show measurable reclassification effects when customer sentiment signals are included, demonstrating that integrating unstructured data into MCIC can enhance inventory prioritization.

</details>


### [167] [Aspect-wise Pairwise Tournaments for Test-Time Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08640)
*Hannah Guan,Yilun Du*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出按方面进行两两比较的淘汰赛方法，用于从多个模型答案中选出更优者；该方法在多领域任务中优于多种常见筛选基线，并可按用户偏好调整评判重点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 增加推理时计算量能够提升大语言模型的推理表现，常见做法是生成多个候选答案后择优。逐个孤立评判候选答案可能不够自然，也难以处理复杂答案；人类更擅长直接比较两个选项，并按不同方面逐项评估。因此，论文探索如何将两两比较和分方面评估结合起来，以更可靠地筛选答案。

Method: 提出按方面进行两两比较的淘汰赛：候选答案参加单败淘汰赛，每场对决由多个现成的评审器投票决定。每个评审器依据自然语言指定的一个方面，对两份答案作出偏好判断，再汇总投票选出胜者。该方法不需要训练模型；改变方面描述即可调整筛选标准或迁移到新领域。

Result: 在五个领域、三种答案生成模型上的实验中，该方法整体优于多方面二元审批、自洽性筛选和奖励模型评审，在开放式任务上的提升最大。消融比较显示，两两比较与分方面评审缺一不可：使用相同方面的二元审批，或在相同评审预算下使用通用两两评审，都未能达到该方法的表现。

Conclusion: 将候选答案置于淘汰赛中进行分方面的两两比较，是一种有效且灵活的推理时筛选策略。它不仅能提升答案选择质量，还能通过指定评审方面贴合用户偏好，而无需重新生成答案；由于不涉及模型训练，迁移到新领域也只需改写评审提示。

Abstract: Scaling test-time compute has proven highly effective at improving LLM reasoning. A popular method is best-of-n sampling, where a model generates many candidate solutions and one is selected. This motivates the question: how can we select the best candidate? When humans make choices, we find it more natural to compare two options than judge each in isolation. Additionally, when the options become complex, we like to judge the options one aspect at a time, such as comparing two job candidates on experience, communication, etc. In the test-time setting, candidate solutions should be judged in the same way. Here we introduce Aspect-wise Pairwise Tournaments (APT), which verifies by comparison and compares by aspect. Each matchup of a single elimination tournament is decided by a vote over multiple off-the-shelf aspect verifiers, each expressing a pairwise preference along its own aspect. Across five domains and three generator LLMs, APT improves over binary multi-aspect approval, self-consistency and reward model verification, with the largest gains on open-ended tasks. We find that both components matter; neither binary approval over the same aspects nor a generic pairwise judge at a matched budget matches APT. Because aspects are specified in natural language, APT also lets users steer selection toward properties they care about, better aligning with user preferences without needing to regenerate solutions. No component is trained, so APT transfers to new domains by simply rewriting prompts.

</details>


### [168] [Decision Context: Persistent Behavioral Learning for AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08644)
*Nima Vahdat,Pashootan Vaezipoor*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种跨会话行为记忆系统，将用户纠错和工具调用失败转化为明确规则，并在相关决策时注入；其合规率达到百分之九十五，明显优于朴素检索，同时每次查询所需令牌约减少九成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体记忆系统主要保存偏好、实体和对话历史等事实性信息，难以积累和运用程序性知识。因此，用户不得不在每次新对话中重复纠正同类错误。

Method: 研究提出“决策上下文锻造”系统，从用户纠错和智能体对工具调用失败的自我评估中提取行为信号，将其编译为明确的指令式规则，并在规则适用的决策节点注入。研究还构建了行为决策基准，包含九个行为维度的七十一个场景，基于真实执行轨迹，并在确定性的模拟工具环境中进行评测；系统与四种先前方案进行比较。

Result: 该系统的行为合规率达到百分之九十五，而朴素检索方案为百分之五十五；与此同时，每次查询注入的令牌数量约为朴素方案的十分之一。比较结果还显示，表现较强的方案会将行为记忆表示为明确指令，并在适用的决策节点提供这些指令。

Conclusion: 跨会话行为学习的关键不仅是保存记忆，还包括如何表示、何时激活以及在何处提供记忆。将纠错信息转化为明确规则，并精准置于相关决策环节，有望提升智能体持续改进的能力，同时降低上下文开销。

Abstract: LLM agents do not learn from corrections across sessions. A user fixes the same mistake at the start of every conversation. Existing agent memory systems primarily handle facts, such as preferences, entities, and conversational history, rather than procedural knowledge.
  We present Forge Decision Context (forge_dc), a system that captures behavioral signals from human corrections and self-assessed tool-call failures, compiles them into imperative RULE directives, and injects them at the decision points where they apply. We evaluate forge_dc alongside four predecessor approaches on DC-Bench, a new benchmark of 71 scenarios across nine behavioral dimensions, built from real executed agent traces in a deterministic mocked-tool environment. forge_dc reaches 95% compliance, up from 55% with naive retrieval, while injecting approximately 10 times fewer tokens per query.
  Across the five-stage progression, the strongest systems represent behavioral memory as explicit directives and deliver it at the decision points where it applies. The results identify representation, activation, and placement as central design choices for persistent behavioral learning.

</details>


### [169] [IWIBench: Evaluating Gradient-Free Model Driven Intra-weight Injection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08647)
*Chris von Csefalvay,Pranav Doma,Achyuthan Unni Krishnan,Tamás Földi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一套基准，用于评估大型模型直接改写较小模型权重、且不使用梯度来改变其行为的能力；实验显示，这种能力已初步出现，但不同模型和任务之间差异显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 直接编辑模型权重可以在不依赖梯度的情况下改变模型行为，应用范围包括调整词语关联、控制输出格式和更新事实知识。然而，此类方法缺少系统的评估方式，难以比较不同编辑模型的能力，也难以判断编辑是否会损害目标模型原有能力。

Method: 论文提出“权重内注入基准”，评估大型编辑模型对较小目标模型（通常为约四十亿参数的指令微调模型）进行权重修改的能力。基准涵盖十类经典文本编辑目标，并限制编辑模型使用四种权重读写工具、限定张量访问范围，以及固定生成和检查模型输出的预算。验证器在隔离环境中应用编辑补丁，再将编辑后的模型与未修改模型进行比较，以衡量目标行为是否实现及其他能力是否保留。

Result: 评测发现，前沿闭源与开放权重模型的表现差异很大，少数模型明显领先；结合模型间的新旧差异与定性分析，结果表明这项能力可能是近期训练中获得的。任务难度也高度不均：结构化响应任务无人成功，控制回答长度仅有一个方案成功；获取别名和重新编码则相对容易。不同任务类别之间的成功率相关性较弱，说明能力发展尚不均衡。

Conclusion: 权重内注入已成为一种有潜力且具实际效果的免梯度行为修改方法，但目前能力前沿仍早期、偏斜，不能从单项任务的成功推断模型具备普遍的权重编辑能力。该基准为后续系统比较编辑能力、考察能力保留情况及研究编辑策略提供了评估框架。

Abstract: Intra-weight injection lets one model change another model's behaviour by editing its weights directly, without gradients. Such edits can range from altering lexical associations to enforcing output formats or updating factual knowledge. Despite growing interest in these approaches, there has been no systematic way to evaluate how well models perform them. We introduce IWIBench, which measures a large editor model's ability to modify the weights of a smaller target model (typically Gemma 3 4B IT) to achieve one of ten classical text-editing objectives while preserving its other capabilities. Editors are limited to four tools for reading and writing weights, with bounded tensor access and a fixed budget for generating and inspecting rollouts. A validator applies each proposed patch in a clean environment and compares the edited model against the unmodified target. Evaluating frontier closed- and open-weight models reveals sharply diverging capabilities, both across models and across tasks. A small number of models, most notably Astra and GPT-6.1 Sol, perform far ahead of the rest. The recency of this ability (little of it is evident in GPT-5.6 Sol, for instance), together with qualitative analysis, suggests it has been acquired through recent training. Tasks also vary widely: structured response resisted every attempt, and answer-length control yielded only one successful solution, whereas alias acquisition and recoding proved most amenable. Success in one task family correlated poorly with success in others, indicating an early and uneven capability frontier. We discuss strategies that editors repeatedly adopted and conclude with an outlook on intra-weight injection as an emerging but already potent approach to gradient-free behaviour modification.

</details>


### [170] [Pdb or Not to Be: Comparing Debugging Interfaces With Pass$_{X}$@$k$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08666)
*Ryan Magnuson,Ellie Kitanidis,Ariel Lee*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文在固定其余智能体配置的条件下比较仅使用调试器与仅使用命令行工具的编码智能体，并提出跨智能体成功率指标以区分工具互补性与随机波动；结果显示，重复运行两个命令行智能体优于各运行一个命令行智能体和调试器智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体通常依赖反复编辑、执行和观察的命令行循环，而交互式调试器可直接呈现运行时状态，或许能带来不同的问题解决能力。但以往研究常同时引入新工具和新架构，难以单独判断调试器的贡献；此外，不同运行间的随机波动也可能被误认为工具互补性。

Method: 在五百个软件工程基准实例上，对比仅使用调试器和仅使用命令行工具的智能体，并固定其他配置。研究采用两种模型，每种“工具架构与模型”组合进行三次独立运行。作者提出跨智能体的至少一次成功率估计指标，用于估算从多个智能体运行中至少有一个成功的概率，从而分析工具差异是否真正带来互补收益。

Result: 单次配对运行中，两类智能体解决的问题集合有部分差异。但跨运行的估计结果表明，这种差异主要来自运行间随机波动，而非工具间的稳定互补。对两种模型而言，运行两个命令行智能体的表现均优于各运行一个命令行智能体和一个调试器智能体。

Conclusion: 评估智能体或工具多样性时，必须考虑随机波动，不能仅凭单次运行中解决问题集合不同就断定工具互补。作者提出的跨智能体成功率指标可为相关评估提供更稳健的分析框架。

Abstract: Interactive debuggers are indispensable to human software development, yet coding agents overwhelmingly rely on edit-execute-observe loops, using common bash tools, test scripts, and print statements to debug via repeated shell commands. Stepping through a debugger provides live access to runtime state, which may enable different problem-solving capabilities. However, prior work typically introduces the debugger as an additional tool or through a new subagent, making its individual contribution difficult to isolate from the associated scaffold changes. To address this, we compare a debugger-only agent against a bash-only agent on all 500 SWE-bench Verified instances, holding the rest of the scaffold fixed. We evaluate both architectures on two models, GPT-OSS-120B and Nemotron-3-Ultra-550B, with three independent runs per architecture $\times$ model configuration. Crucially, to distinguish interface specialization from expanded coverage under repeated sampling, we introduce pass$_X$@$k$, a cross-agent pass@$k$ that provides an unbiased estimator of the probability that at least one of $k$ agents is successful. Although the bash and debugger agents solve partially disjoint subsets of the benchmark in any single pair of runs, our pass$_X$@$2$ estimator reveals that this is largely due to run-to-run variance rather than tool complementarity. Across both models, we find that two bash rollouts outperform the combination of one bash and one debugger rollout. Our findings highlight the importance of accounting for stochastic variance in evaluating agent and tool diversity, and we hope that pass$_X$@$k$ will serve as a useful framework for the community.

</details>


### [171] [ChainFlow: Few-Step Latent Flow Matching for Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08669)
*Selim Aktas,Yusuf Erdem Erdem,Ahmed Zeer,Himmet Toprak Kesgin,Muzaffer Kaan Yuce,Mehmet Fatih Amasyali*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 链流是一种基于目标模型隐状态的流匹配推测解码方法，通过少量传输步骤、状态压缩、依赖反馈和树状草拟提升推理速度与接受率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码草拟器各有局限：自回归草拟器逐个生成词元，草拟长度越长，计算开销越大；扩散草拟器虽能并行生成，却通常独立预测各位置，忽略词元间依赖。论文旨在兼顾并行效率与序列依赖，并降低草拟成本。

Method: 提出链流，将草拟过程设计为目标模型隐状态之间的短程传输，而非从噪声开始进行多步去噪，因此只需一至两步流匹配计算。方法以目标模型隐状态为条件，使用变分自编码器压缩状态以控制随目标模型规模增长的开销；再用轻量反馈头恢复词元间依赖，并通过树状草拟提高候选词元的接受率。

Result: 在匹配草拟器规模和训练数据的条件下，链流在启文三点五模型上通过快速语言模型推理引擎部署时，速度分别比鹰三和深闪高百分之三十九、百分之十一。将草拟器依次适配到新语言后，性能优势进一步扩大。论文还进行了草拟器设计与并发服务方面的消融研究。

Conclusion: 链流展示了基于目标隐状态的流匹配草拟器可有效结合并行生成与词元依赖建模，并以较低计算开销提升推测解码性能。摘要报告了多项对比和消融结果，但未提供绝对延迟、接受率等具体数值。

Abstract: Speculative decoding has become a standard way to accelerate LLM inference: a lightweight drafter proposes tokens that the target verifies in one parallel pass. Existing drafters face trade-offs that limits their speedups: autoregressive drafters generate tokens one at a time, so drafting cost grows with draft length, while diffusion drafters generate tokens in parallel but predict each position independently, ignoring inter-token dependencies. We introduce ChainFlow, a new speculative decoding paradigm and the first flow-matching drafter conditioned on the target's hidden states. ChainFlow treats drafting as a short transport over hidden states, needing one or two flow steps instead of the many denoising steps of generation from noise. A variational autoencoder compresses these states so drafting cost barely grows with target size, lightweight feedback heads restore inter-token dependencies, and tree drafting raises acceptance. On Qwen3.5 models, at matched drafter size and training data, ChainFlow outperforms EAGLE-3 and DFlash in vLLM by 39% and 11%, respectively. These gains further widen as drafters are adapted to new languages sequentially. Our ablations cover drafter design and concurrent serving, and we open-source our code and drafter models.

</details>


### [172] [Transporting the Past: An Optimal Transport View of Backtracking Counterfactuals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08682)
*Zhangyi Liu,Pouria Fatemi,Suvrit Sra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文将最优传输用于结构因果模型中的回溯式反事实推断，提出两种把事实观测诱导的外生变量后验分布映射到满足目标反事实条件的方案，并比较其代价、约束满足度与独立性保持之间的权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在结构因果模型中，事实观测通常对应外生变量上的后验分布，而非唯一取值，因此反事实推断本质上具有分布性。论文旨在系统处理如何将该后验分布转化为满足指定反事实查询的外生变量配置，同时兼顾推断有效性及外生变量的解释意义。

Method: 论文从最优传输视角提出两种互补框架：第一种构造逐点投影，将后验中的配置映射到满足目标反事实条件的集合，适用于可直接使用传输后外生分布的情形；第二种额外要求反事实外生变量相互独立，使其仍可解释为同一结构因果模型中的真正外生噪声。作者证明两种框架均符合原有的回溯式反事实原则，并针对有限后验提出边缘式、基于惩罚项以及硬约束式求解器。

Result: 在非双射结构因果模型上的合成实验展示了问题的几何特征，并揭示了传输成本、反事实约束满足程度与外生独立性保持之间的权衡。摘要未提供具体数值或某一求解器占优的结论。

Conclusion: 该研究为回溯式反事实推断提供了基于最优传输的分布变换框架，并区分了直接传输后验与保持外生噪声独立性两类需求。实验表明，实际方法选择需在传输成本、目标约束和外生变量独立性之间进行权衡。

Abstract: We study backtracking counterfactual inference for Structural Causal Models (SCMs) through the lens of optimal transport. In SCMs, a factual observation generally induces a posterior distribution over exogenous variables, making counterfactual inference inherently distributional. We introduce two complementary frameworks that transport this posterior onto the set of exogenous configurations satisfying a desired counterfactual query. The first constructs a natural pointwise projection and is suitable when the transported exogenous distribution can be used directly. The second enforces mutual independence among counterfactual exogenous variables, so that the transported variables remain interpretable as genuine exogenous noises of the same SCM. We show that both frameworks are consistent with the original backtracking counterfactual principle, and we propose marginal, penalty-based, and hard-constrained solvers for finite-posterior settings. Synthetic experiments on non-bijective SCMs illustrate the geometry of the problem and reveal trade-offs among transport cost, counterfactual constraint satisfaction, and preservation of exogenous independence.

</details>


### [173] [Cancellation Is Not Enough: What Retries Leave Behind in an Overloaded LLM Engine](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08684)
*Xindan Zhang,Fei Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现，单靠取消超时请求无法缓解重试造成的过载；合并重复请求能交付所有过载期间及之后的调用，但取消可能使其失效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 客户端和代理会重试超时调用。短暂过载后，这些重试可能持续挤占推理引擎资源，使引擎即使在到达负载低于处理能力时也无法恢复。取消已离开客户端对应的计算看似可以解决问题，但其实际效果尚不明确。

Method: 研究人员在某大型语言模型推理引擎上，针对三种开放模型开展过载实验，并分析一种模型的调度轨迹，将每次客户端调用尝试与其在引擎中创建的执行对应起来。研究比较了请求取消、重试预算和请求合并等控制策略，并考察执行行为及客户端实际收到的调用结果。

Result: 开启重试时，单独取消请求在所有实验中均未能恢复服务；过载后到达的调用仅有百分之十二至三十八得到交付。调度轨迹显示，重试请求通常在超时前不到半秒就开始提示词预处理；随后被中止的执行已消耗其计算提示词令牌的百分之七十九至八十五，而引擎的提示词令牌计数器漏记了约三分之一至一半的实际计算量。重试预算虽能通过削减重试恢复服务，却仍使许多调用未能交付。请求合并则能交付从过载开始的所有调用，部分调用会延迟；但若取消操作已中止正在运行的执行，这一效果便无法保证。

Conclusion: 评估过载控制策略时，不能只看服务是否恢复或请求是否被取消，还应统计重试新增的实际执行及客户端最终收到的调用。请求合并在实验中最能保障调用交付，而取消机制可能提前终止执行、削弱其效果。

Abstract: Clients and proxies in front of an LLM engine retry calls that time out. After a transient overload, these retries can keep the engine from recovering even when calls arrive below its capacity. Since the engine may keep computing for clients that have left, propagating cancellation looks like the natural remedy. In this paper, we test it on vLLM with overload experiments on three open models, and with scheduler traces of one that follow each client attempt to the execution it creates in the engine. We find that with retries on, cancellation alone did not restore service in any run, and only 12-38% of the calls that arrived after the overload were delivered. The traces suggest why: retries that vLLM ran typically started prefill less than half a second before they timed out. Executions that vLLM later aborted took 79-85% of the prompt tokens it computed, and vLLM's prompt-token counter missed a third to half of all computed prompt tokens. A retry budget restored service by shedding retries but left many of the overload's calls undelivered. Request coalescing delivered every call from the overload onward, some late, unless cancellation had already aborted the running execution. Overload controls should thus be judged by the executions that retries add and the calls that clients receive.

</details>


### [174] [Memory-Aware Multi-Agent Architecture with LLM-based Orchestration for Conversational Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08718)
*Emilly Pitman de Castro,Vanessa Silva,Gustavo Kanno,Marcus Santos,Bruno Ferraz do Amaral,Marcelo Nogueira,Lucas Francisco Pellicer,Lucas Lanaro,Pedro Augusto Luiz,João Renato Ribeiro Manesco,Giovanni Carbinatti,Isabela de Lima Santos,Lucas Oliveira Jeronymo,Jeison Fappi,Raul Rotilli Aguirre,Fabio André Frantz,Renata De Paris,Fabiana Marucci,Lohan Silva,Vinicius Diniz Reolon,Wandemberg Gibaut*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出面向对话式推荐的记忆感知多智能体架构，强调长期记忆、协调编排及超越传统排序指标的评估；实验显示这些设计可改善跨轮对话表现与用户接受度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对话式推荐常将每次会话彼此割裂，难以追踪用户意图随时间的变化。虽然大型语言模型提升了语义理解能力，但通常只是独立组件，缺少对记忆、检索与决策如何协同的系统级设计；传统排序指标也难以反映推荐内容的语义一致性。

Method: 提出记忆感知的多智能体架构，由大型语言模型充当协调层，统筹意图细化、查询构建和交互流程。长期记忆被纳为核心组件，用于影响系统路由与语义查询生成。研究通过跨会话记忆实验、多轮对话模拟及监督式交互研究进行评估，并引入语义一致性评分，补充传统排序评估。

Result: 在亚马逊评论数据集上的实验表明，启用记忆的交互最多弥补显式查询情境与常规对话情境之间差距的百分之六十三，并能避免长对话中的语义退化。监督式协调使用户接受度提高二十八点九个百分点。语言模型重排序虽未提升前若干名命中率，却能持续改善语义一致性与多样性；研究还发现，从检索候选到实际展示的推荐存在曝光瓶颈。

Conclusion: 有效的对话式推荐不仅依赖模型本身，也取决于系统层面的设计。显式整合长期记忆、协调多智能体交互，并采用兼顾语义质量的评估方式，有助于提升意图追踪、对话质量与用户接受度。

Abstract: Conversational recommender systems often treat user sessions in isolation, limiting their ability to capture how user intent evolves over time. While large language models (LLMs) have improved semantic understanding, they are typically used as isolated components, without addressing how memory, retrieval, and decision-making should be integrated at the system level. In this work, we propose a memory-aware multi-agent architecture for conversational recommendation, in which LLMs act as orchestration layers that coordinate intent refinement, query construction, and interaction flow. A key design principle is the integration of long-term memory as a first-class component, influencing both routing decisions and semantic query formation. We evaluate the system through cross-session memory experiments, multi-turn conversational simulations, and supervisor-driven interaction studies on the Amazon Reviews dataset. Results show that memory-enabled interactions recover up to 63\% of the gap toward explicit-query scenarios and prevent semantic degradation in extended dialogues. In addition, supervisory orchestration increases user acceptance by +28.9 percentage points, highlighting the importance of structured orchestration in guiding interaction and output quality. We also observe an exposure bottleneck between retrieved candidates and displayed recommendations. While LLM-reranking does not improve Hit@K, it consistently enhances semantic consistency and diversity. To capture these effects, we introduce the Semantic Consistency Score (SCS), a complementary evaluation measure. Overall, the results emphasize the importance of system-level design choices, suggesting that effective conversational recommendation benefits from explicit memory integration, orchestration, and evaluation beyond traditional ranking metrics.

</details>


### [175] [AlterNet: Adaptive-Depth Mixture-of-Experts with Learned Halting for Elastic Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08741)
*Alexander Kastius*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合混合专家架构与动态深度计算的模型，使模型能按任务需求调整推理计算量，并可在训练后降低推理成本而无需重新训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多数大语言模型的计算需求在训练阶段即固定，难以兼顾复杂任务的能力与推理速度、成本。本文旨在让模型根据任务难度动态分配计算，并允许部署时按资源条件缩减计算量。

Method: 架构结合浅层专家与动态深度机制：模型通过迭代计算逐步处理输入；采用序列级、基于深度的路由，在专家内部由停止机制控制是否继续计算；加入深度编码以追踪计算进度，并使用正则化机制鼓励模型有效利用已分配的计算资源。训练后还可缩减计算量，无需重新训练。

Result: 论文报告了初步基准测试，并讨论了未来研究方向。摘要未提供具体实验数值或与基线模型的性能比较，因此无法据此判断方法的实际收益幅度。

Conclusion: 该工作提出一种可调节推理深度的模型设计，目标是在任务需求与部署成本之间实现灵活权衡。其有效性目前仍属初步验证，后续需要更全面的实验来评估性能、计算节省程度及不同任务上的适用性。

Abstract: Most LLM architectures have fixed compute demands determined at training time. We propose a novel architecture that combines the Mixture-of-Experts architecture with shallow experts and a novel dynamic-depth system to make the extent of a forward pass dynamically adjustable. Our approach lets the model automatically determine the compute needed for a given task, paired with a system to scale it down after training without retraining when the execution environment demands faster or cheaper inference. To achieve this, we introduce an iterative computation mechanism, sequence-level depth-based routing steered by a halting mechanism within the experts, a depth encoding that lets the model track its progress in the chain, and a regularization mechanism that incentivizes the model to use allocated compute efficiently. We present initial benchmarks and discuss future research directions.

</details>


### [176] [AttentionSwitch: Jailbreak Defense for LLMs via Joint Learning of Sparse Attention-Head Selection and Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08752)
*Doniyorkhon Obidov,Honggang Yu,Xiaolong Guo,Kaichen Yang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 注意力切换方法在不更新大语言模型参数的情况下，联合选择干预位置与干预强度，以增强抵御越狱攻击的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型容易受到越狱攻击。全面微调虽然可能提升安全性，却计算成本较高，并可能因灾难性遗忘损害已有能力。已有针对特定模型组件的防御方法通常先选定干预位置、再决定修改方式，忽略了两者相互依赖，因而可能无法找到最优方案。

Method: 该方法在冻结的大语言模型中联合优化干预位置和强度：学习一组稀疏的注意力头，并根据输入查询生成乘法缩放因子，对所选注意力头的输出进行增强或抑制，同时保持其表示方向不变。

Result: 在多种常用语言模型和越狱攻击上，该方法优于已有防御方案；训练数据较少时，优势尤其明显。摘要未提供具体数据或评测指标。

Conclusion: 将干预位置与干预强度联合优化，有望比顺序决策更有效地提升模型的越狱防御能力。该方法无需改动模型主体参数，并在少量训练数据条件下表现出较强潜力。

Abstract: Large language models have achieved remarkable performance across a wide range of tasks, yet they remain susceptible to jailbreak attacks that circumvent safety mechanisms and elicit harmful or unintended outputs. Although safety can be improved through model fine-tuning, such approaches can be computationally expensive and may degrade previously acquired capabilities through catastrophic forgetting. Recent jailbreak defenses have therefore explored targeted interventions on specific model components associated with unsafe behavior. However, existing approaches typically decouple two inherently related decisions: where to intervene and how to modify the selected components. Such sequential optimization may be suboptimal because the optimal intervention location can depend on the intervention applied there, and vice versa. In this work, we introduce AttentionSwitch, the first jailbreak defense to jointly optimize intervention sites and strengths within a frozen LLM. Specifically, AttentionSwitch learns a sparse set of attention heads together with query-conditioned multiplicative scaling factors, allowing the outputs of the selected heads to be either attenuated or amplified without altering the directions of their representations. Across popular language models and jailbreak attacks, AttentionSwitch outperforms existing defenses, with particularly strong results when training data is scarce.

</details>


### [177] [Debate to Deceive: Poisoning LLM's Fact-Checking via Game-Theoretic Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08756)
*Liting Huang,Huizhi Liang,Yi Lei,Xingjun Ma,Varun Ojha*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种多智能体辩论式攻击框架，通过竞争演化修辞策略，生成更具说服力且更能规避防御的对抗性文本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对大型语言模型的知识攻击可能操纵事实核查判断，威胁系统可靠性。现有的对抗后缀或模板注入难以适应不同防御；单智能体自我反思则容易产生确认偏差，并依赖成本高昂的反复试错。

Method: 提出辩论诱骗框架，将对抗文本生成建模为演化博弈。多个智能体通过竞争调整逻辑诉求、情感诉求和信誉诉求等修辞策略。为降低大型语言模型输出空间带来的搜索难度，框架将行为拆分为策略层和生成层：策略层从有限策略集合中选择修辞行动，生成层则结合所选策略与辩论上下文撰写具体文本。

Result: 在四个事实核查基准上，该框架显著优于现有攻击方法，并成功绕过大多数防御措施。生成的对抗文本在语义连贯性、修辞效果和抵抗过早确认偏差方面表现较强。

Conclusion: 将结构化多智能体辩论与演化策略学习结合，有望提升知识攻击的适应性与有效性。该研究也凸显了事实核查模型面临的安全风险，以及开发更强健防御措施的必要性。

Abstract: Knowledge attacks on Large Language Models, such as manipulating fact-checking judgments, pose significant risks to AI reliability. Existing approaches -- adversarial suffix/template injection and single-agent self-reflection -- lack robustness across diverse defenses and are prone to confirmation bias and costly trial-and-error, respectively. We propose Debate-to-Deceive (D2D), a multi-agent debate framework grounded in human debate structure and rhetorical style, enabling agents to generate adversarial passages that are semantically coherent, rhetorically effective, and resistant to early confirmation bias. D2D formalizes the adversarial process as an evolutionary game in which agents adaptively evolve their rhetorical strategies (Logos, Pathos, Ethos) through competition.
