In contrast, concerns that automation would reduce personal fulfillment predicted lower automation preference. Open-ended responses further revealed a preference for bounded autonomy, with participants often favoring substantial AI execution while retaining human involvement, oversight, or final authority. We argue that delegation is best understood as a configuration of execution, collaboration, leadership, and authority.

</details>


### [248] [Continuous wavelet-based feature extraction for laser-induced breakdown spectroscopy classification of human plasma samples](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09746)
*Md Mahmudur Rahman,Atqiya Munawara Mahi,Nazim Ahmed Belabbaci,Mohammad Arif Ul Alam*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究利用连续小波变换提取等离子体激光诱导击穿光谱的特征峰，并结合支持向量机区分新冠病毒阳性与阴性样本；初步留一法结果较好，但仍需核实并进行独立验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂生物样本的激光诱导击穿光谱包含大量信息，提取具有区分能力的特征对于样本分类至关重要。本研究旨在评估连续小波变换提取光谱峰值、用于区分新冠病毒阳性和阴性人血浆样本的可行性。

Method: 研究分析了181名个体的血浆样本，其中阳性样本53份、阴性对照128份。每份样本取5微升滴加于硅基底并干燥，使用1064纳米纳秒脉冲钕钇铝石榴石激光采集光谱；每份样本测量100次，经基于强度的异常值剔除后取平均。随后通过连续小波变换检测光谱峰，并使用支持向量机分类，采用留一法评估性能。

Result: 留一法报告的准确率为93.00%，灵敏度为94.67%，特异度为91.61%。特征峰被归属为钙离子、钾原子和氮原子的发射线。不过，研究指出这些性能估计仍属初步结果，需结合样本层面的预测结果进一步核对。

Conclusion: 该流程为研究所分析的两组血浆样本之间可能存在的光谱差异提供了基础，但目前不能据此确认其具有普遍适用性或诊断价值。仍需开展独立验证，并评估样本、基底及光谱采集条件变化带来的影响。

Abstract: Extracting discriminative features from laser-induced breakdown spectroscopy (LIBS) spectra is important for the classification of complex biological samples. This study evaluates continuous wavelet transform (CWT)-based spectral peak extraction for distinguishing human plasma samples labeled as SARS-CoV-2-positive or negative. Plasma specimens from 181 individuals, comprising 53 positive samples and 128 negative controls, were deposited as 5 μL droplets on silicon substrates and dried before analysis. Spectra were acquired using a nanosecond Nd:YAG laser operating at 1064 nm, with 100 single-shot measurements collected per specimen and averaged after intensity-based outlier rejection. CWT-based peak detection was coupled with a support vector machine (SVM) classifier. The reported leave-one-out evaluation yielded an accuracy of 93.00%, sensitivity of 94.67%, and specificity of 91.61%; these estimates remain provisional pending reconciliation with specimen-level predictions. Features identified by the CWT pipeline included emission lines assigned to Ca II, K I, and N I. The workflow provides a basis for examining spectral features associated with the studied plasma groups. Independent validation and assessment of specimen, substrate, and acquisition-related variability are required to establish generalizability and diagnostic relevance.

</details>


### [249] [Assessing the Impact of Retrieval-Augmented Generation on Algorithmic Problem-Solving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09791)
*Gabriel Souza Baggio,Gabriel Machado Lunardi,Guilherme Medeiros Machado,Subhankar Maity,Faiza Belbachir,José Palazzo Moreira de Oliveira*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向竞赛编程的检索增强生成方法：检索相似训练题作为示例，实验显示这能提升大语言模型的解题表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 竞赛编程不仅要求模型生成正确代码，还要求其进行复杂的算法推理。仅依靠模型已有能力可能不足以应对这类任务，因此研究探索如何利用相似题目的示例增强解题能力，同时区分检索带来的泛化收益与模型对训练数据的潜在记忆。

Method: 研究采用基于嵌入表示的相似度检索，从知识库中寻找与待解题目相近的训练题，并动态调整检索阈值以减少无关示例。在竞赛题数据集的测试集相似题目上，对比三种条件：仅提供原题描述、补充随机训练示例、补充检索得到的相似示例。此外，使用最小似然值成员推断分析考察生成代码可能受到训练数据记忆影响的程度，并以通过率和题目竞技等级分评估表现。

Result: 摘要报告称，基于相似度的检索显著提升了大语言模型在竞赛编程场景中的表现。实验比较了原题输入、随机示例增强和相似示例检索增强，但摘要未提供具体数值或各项指标的提升幅度。成员推断分析用于辅助判断提升是否可能来自对训练数据的隐式记忆。

Conclusion: 相似题目检索可作为提升大语言模型算法编程能力的有效途径，并提供一种可扩展的代码生成增强框架。结合成员推断分析有助于进一步理解模型表现来源；不过，摘要所述结果尚未给出量化细节，实际增益及其与数据记忆的关系仍需依据完整实验结果评估。

Abstract: Competitive programming problems pose distinctive challenges for Large Language Models, as they demand not only proficient code generation but also advanced algorithmic reasoning. This study presents a Retrieval-Augmented Generation approach specifically designed to enhance the capacity of Large Language Models to address such problems. Our approach integrates an embedding-based retrieval mechanism and a similarity search algorithm to identify analogous problems within a knowledge database, employing a dynamically adjusted threshold to mitigate retrieval noise. We conducted an evaluation using the CodeContests dataset, focusing on the test split problems that exhibit similarity to instances from the training split. The experimental framework considered three conditions: (i) problems provided solely with their original descriptions, (ii) problems supplemented with randomly selected examples from the training split, and (iii) problems augmented with similar examples retrieved through the proposed method. In addition, we incorporated a Min-K membership inference analysis to examine whether the generated code solutions were influenced by potential memorization of training data within the evaluated Large Language Models. This complementary perspective enables a deeper understanding of model behavior by distinguishing genuine generalization through retrieval from implicit recall of pretraining samples. Performance was assessed using both pass rates and the CodeForces Elo rating of the problems. The results provide empirical evidence that similarity-based retrieval substantially enhances Large Language Models performance in competitive programming scenarios, offering an scalable framework for improving code generation in algorithmic problem-solving contexts.

</details>


### [250] [Knowing When to Intervene: Causal Evaluation of AI Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09792)
*Ayush Sawarni,Wenqian Xing,Tijana Zrnic,Ramesh Johari,Vasilis Syrgkanis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种评估智能体轨迹中随机审计干预因果效果的框架，可据此比较干预策略并辅助安全审计优先级排序。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体评估主要依赖静态基准，难以判断部署过程中何时应核验输出或引导智能体改变行为，也无法衡量这些干预是否改善最终结果。由于干预效果受当前情境、环境及智能体后续行为共同影响，直接估计其因果效应颇具挑战。

Method: 作者提出一个具有因果推断基础的评估框架。利用规模相对较小、通过随机核验或筛查等干预收集的智能体轨迹数据，估计一类丰富的情境化干预策略的动态因果效果；同时估计结构参数，用于构建反映不同智能体行为风险的安全排行榜。

Result: 在零售任务基准、工具沙盒和不可能任务基准上的实验显示，干预效果会随智能体采取的动作及所处状态显著变化，说明干预价值具有明显的情境依赖性。

Conclusion: 评估智能体不能只依赖静态基准，还应研究部署过程中的干预何时有效、对最终结果有何因果影响。该框架为干预策略评估和审计资源排序提供了方法基础，也凸显了因果推断与智能体评估交叉研究对安全可靠部署的重要性。

Abstract: Current evaluations of agentic AI systems primarily rely on benchmarks. However, the static nature of benchmarks makes them uninformative about the value of auditing interventions at deployment time. For reliable deployment, we often need to determine within an agent's trajectory whether or not to intervene, e.g., by verifying the agent's current output or steering it toward different behavior, using either human review or more costly models or time-consuming agentic supervision. The value of such interventions is fundamentally a causal inference question, asking whether the causal effect of such an intervention is beneficial for the final outcome. Estimating this causal effect is particularly challenging due to the dynamic nature of the problem: the impact of an intervention depends on the current context and environment, as well as the agent's future behavior. We develop the first methodologically grounded framework for evaluating the causal effects of randomized interventions within agent trajectories. By leveraging a relatively small dataset of agent trajectories collected under randomized interventions such as verification or screening, we can estimate the dynamic causal effect of a rich class of (contextual) intervention policies. The structural parameters that we estimate can also be used to construct safety leaderboards that capture the risk of various agentic actions, which can be used by practitioners for audit prioritization. Applying our methods to tau-bench Retail, ToolSandbox, and ImpossibleBench, we find that intervention effects vary substantially across agent actions and states. More broadly, our work suggests a rich research agenda at the intersection of causal inference and agentic evaluation, which we believe is essential for the reliable and safe deployment of AI agents in the real world.

</details>


### [251] [Test-Time Compute Scales Better with Parallel Agent Teams](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09798)
*Junlin Wang,Haozhe Chen,Ang Li,Rahul Chalamala,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 并行智能体团队通过并行运行多个小型协作团队并选出最佳结果，兼顾协作带来的高质量与多次采样带来的方差收益，从而更有效地利用推理时计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时增加计算量可以提升固定模型的表现，但现有两种做法各有局限：独立运行大量智能体并择优，效果容易很快饱和；扩大单个协作团队虽可提升表现，却也会逐渐停止增长。研究旨在结合两者优势。

Method: 提出并行智能体团队：同时运行多个规模较小的协作团队，由能够评估每次尝试的验证器为结果打分，再选取表现最佳团队的结果。研究还建立了一个简化统计模型，用于解释不同计算分配方式的表现差异。

Result: 在涵盖图形处理器内核、形式化证明和漏洞利用开发等领域的十二项长程任务上，使用二百七十亿参数模型和一百个智能体组成的预算时，十人团队并行方案在十二项任务中的十项优于独立智能体并行采样；在进行了百人单团队对比的四项任务中，该方案全部胜出。按相同费用比较时，该方案在一半任务上优于前沿模型。

Conclusion: 并行运行多个小型协作团队并择优，比单纯增加独立样本或扩大单一团队更有效地平衡平均表现与采样方差带来的收益，是扩展推理时计算的一种有前景的方法。

Abstract: Test-time scaling improves the performance of a fixed model by spending more computation at inference. There are two common ways to spend it on agents when a verifier can score every attempt. Best-of-N sampling runs many independent agents and keeps the best result but saturates quickly. One large team of collaborating agents can score higher but stops improving as it grows. Can we get the best of both? Our method, parallel agent teams, does so by sampling many small teams in parallel and keeping the best team's result. We test it on 12 long-horizon tasks from GPU kernels and formal proofs to exploit development. With a 27B model and a budget of 100 agents, parallel teams of ten beat parallel agents on 10 of 12 tasks and beat one team of 100 on all four tasks where we ran such large teams. At equal dollar cost the same parallel teams beat a frontier model on half the tasks. A simple statistical model shows that individual agents lose because their many samples have a low mean and that one large team loses because a single sample gets no gain from variance. Sampling many small teams in parallel balances a high mean with the gain from variance. Parallel agent teams thus offer a more effective way to scale test-time compute.

</details>


### [252] [Generative Latent Priors for Interpreting and Steering Protein Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09818)
*Daniel Hinjos,Lucía Urcelay*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究将生成式潜在先验用于蛋白质模型表征：它能有效识别部分蛋白质概念，并改善单轮表征编辑的流形一致性；但多轮解码后的序列结构可信度仍不足。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 主流大模型解释方法往往依赖较强的结构假设。本文探索一种互补思路：直接学习冻结蛋白质模型内部激活的分布，不预设激活结构，并评估这种方法能否同时支持表征理解与表征编辑。

Method: 作者在两种规模的蛋白质语言模型上，以蛋白质序列数据库中的激活为对象训练扩散式生成潜在先验，并考察其随模型深度和计算量变化的生成质量。作为解释工具，比较单个先验生成的元神经元与稀疏自编码器特征对离散概念及连续属性的探测能力，并进行证伪测试。作为编辑先验，将其用于生化属性引导和结构引导编辑，再把解码得到的序列重新嵌入模型，以评估表征与序列的实际质量。

Result: 生成质量随模型深度和计算量平稳提升。作为解释工具，元神经元在两种模型规模上识别离散数据库概念的表现均优于已发布的稀疏自编码器特征；对连续属性的探测在小模型上领先，在大模型上则未领先。证伪测试表明，这一优势主要来自信息集中，而非发现了新信息。作为编辑先验，它能使三种生化属性的引导编辑生效，并在单独评估时恢复表征的流形一致性；但经过多轮解码后，生成序列的结构可信度会受损。额外的稀疏自编码器结构引导实验也呈现类似问题，仅螺旋结构基序取得成功。自然性缺陷主要归因于序列重写覆盖范围和解码器，而非先验本身。

Conclusion: 生成式潜在先验为蛋白质表征解释和编辑提供了有潜力的无结构假设方案，但其解释优势未必代表新增信息，且表征层面的改善不能保证解码序列具备结构可信度。若要同时用于编辑式发现与探索式发现，仍需解决重写覆盖和解码质量等关键问题。

Abstract: Dominant methods for interpreting large models impose strong structural assumptions. Generative Latent Priors (GLP) are a complementary approach: a diffusion model that learns the distribution of a frozen model's activations directly, imposing no assumptions. We port GLP to ESM-2 activations (UniRef50) and evaluate it at two host scales (ESM-2-8M and -650M), both as a lens for protein representations and as a prior for editing them. Generation quality scales smoothly with depth and compute. As a lens, single GLP "meta-neurons" are the strongest probes of discrete Swiss-Prot concepts at both hosts, beating InterPLM's released SAE features, and lead on continuous properties at 8M though not at 650M; falsification tests show this is mostly concentration, not new information. As an editing prior, the on-manifold correction makes DiffMean steering of three biochemical properties effective and restores on-manifoldness, but only in isolation: re-embedding the sequences actually produced after several rounds of decoding, the structural plausibility is compromised. This is mainly reproduced in additional SAE-based clamping structural steering experiments, where only Helix motif succeeds. The naturalness failure is traced to the rewriting coverage and the decoder rather than the prior, highlighting what the method must clear before it can serve for both discovery-by-editing and discovery-by-exploration. Trained GLPs are released on huggingface.co/collections/HPAI-BSC/glprot.

</details>


### [253] [Can Agentic Systems Close the Gap in Radiology with Production AI Tools?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09836)
*Aubin Charley,Julien Khlaut,Charles Corbière,Pierre Manceron,Corentin Dancette,Léo Machado*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究显示，全面的放射学人工智能报告既受限于可调用工具的覆盖范围，也受限于编排器能否正确选择工具；两者缺一不可。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有放射学人工智能要么专注于少数任务、覆盖临床发现有限，要么虽能处理较广任务，却容易产生幻觉或测量不准。代理式系统有望整合专用工具与通用推理，但已有系统通常局限于单一成像模态或身体部位，因此需要评估其能否支持更全面的报告生成。

Method: 研究构建标准化的放射学代理框架，由编排器调用模块化工具生成结构化报告，并在大型多模态报告语料上模拟评估。研究比较两种工具目录：一是整合已获监管批准设备所覆盖的发现类别，并限定其适用模态；二是允许请求任意发现类别。编排器分别依据临床指征或目标报告制定计划。工具输出直接从参考报告模拟，从而排除工具自身误差，重点考察工具可用性与编排能力。

Result: 在受监管批准工具目录且仅依据临床指征规划时，系统找回的临床可行动发现不足三分之一。扩大为不受限目录后，找回率显著提高；再依据目标报告规划，表现进一步改善。漏检原因分析显示，多数漏检是该模态下没有可用工具覆盖的发现类别；约三分之一则是已有类别或工具未被编排器调用。在留出报告上补充最常漏掉的工具可弥合不受限目录带来的大部分差距；但若使用能力较弱的编排器，即使工具目录不受限，收益也会全部丧失。

Conclusion: 全面的人工智能辅助放射学报告同时受工具覆盖与有效编排制约。扩充工具类别能减少覆盖缺口，但必须配合能够识别并调用适当工具的编排器；因此，工具建设与编排能力提升应协同推进。

Abstract: Radiology AI has developed along two paths: specialized models that perform well on narrow tasks but cover few clinical findings, and generalist models that address a broader range of tasks but remain prone to hallucination and inaccurate measurements. Combining the two through agentic systems offers a route towards comprehensive radiology reporting, but published systems are tied to one modality or body part. We describe a radiology agentic framework that standardizes how an orchestrator calls modular tools to produce a structured report, and use it to measure what such a system can do with the tools from FDA-cleared radiology AI devices. Report generation is simulated on a large multi-modality report corpus, with the orchestrator given either a catalog of finding classes consolidated from the cleared devices, each available on the modalities it is cleared for, or an unlimited catalog in which any class can be requested, and planning either from the clinical indication or from the target report. Tool outputs are simulated from the reference report, so the comparison isolates tool availability and orchestration from tool error. With the cleared catalog and the indication alone, the orchestrator recovers under a third of the clinically actionable findings; recovery improves substantially with the unlimited catalog, and further when the plan is written from the target report. Attributing every missed finding to a cause shows that most are classes no cleared device offers for the study's modality, and that a third are classes or tools that were offered and not called. Adding the most frequently missing tools closes most of the gap to the unlimited catalog on held-out reports, whereas a smaller orchestrator with the unlimited catalog gives up the whole gain. Tool availability and effective orchestration are thus complementary constraints on comprehensive AI-assisted radiology reporting.

</details>


### [254] [Ensemble Methodology for Reference-Free Confidence Estimation in LLM-based Code Summarization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09846)
*Marco Colombo,Balaji A Jayaram,Sowmyakumari Selvaraj,Sumit Tokkar,Animesh Kumar,Aravinda Prasad Sistla,Ravishankar Shetty,Rigel Gjomemo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种在缺少人工参考答案时评估大语言模型生成的科博尔程序摘要正确性的方法，利用不同模型间的语义一致性，并通过校准后的证据汇总降低误判。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估程序摘要通常需要人工撰写的参考答案，但这类答案可能难以获得。传统的字符串相似度或向量嵌入指标也难以可靠判断摘要是否在语义上正确，因此需要一种不依赖人工参考、能够直接评估语义的办法。

Method: 首先使用多个不同的大语言模型，以受控方式生成程序摘要；随后由大语言模型评估器对摘要进行成对比较，并输出结构化判断；最后通过经过校准的汇总规则整合支持性证据，同时仅在矛盾得到多方佐证时予以惩罚。

Result: 摘要介绍了该方法及其应用场景，并提出模型间的语义一致性可作为判断正确性的有效信号，成对的大语言模型评估可能比传统文本相似度指标更忠实地反映语义。摘要未提供定量实验结果或具体性能数据。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种无需人工参考答案来估计程序摘要正确性的思路，核心是结合多模型生成、成对语义评估与审慎的证据汇总。其有效性及相对于现有方法的提升幅度，仍需依据完整论文中的实验结果进一步判断。

Abstract: This paper describes an approach and use case to estimate the correctness of LLM-generated COBOL summaries without access to human-authored references. The core intuition is that inter-model agreement on semantics is informative about truth, and that an LLM-as-judge can provide higher-fidelity pairwise assessments than traditional string- or embedding-based metrics. We operationalise this intuition with (i) controlled summary generation from diverse models; (ii) a LLM evaluator that compares pairs of summaries and emits structured judgments; and (iii) a calibrated aggregation rule that combines supportive evidence while penalising only corroborated contradictions.

</details>


### [255] [Flow-Guided Target-Space Alignment via Path Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09863)
*Ruizhi Yuan,Zeqiu Yu,Wei Gao,Wei Chen,Xiwei Tang,Lu Tang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种由流场引导的多模态对齐方法，在保留配对样本对应关系的同时，使源模态表示符合目标模态空间的整体结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多多模态任务需要将源模态映射到固定的目标空间，而该空间会直接用于下游分析。现有方法往往顾此失彼：对比损失侧重相对相似度，分布匹配侧重整体分布，逐点回归则拟合配对样本，却没有明确刻画目标空间中的路径几何。因此，需要同时兼顾配对对应关系与目标空间整体结构的对齐方法。

Method: 提出流引导目标空间对齐方法，将条件流匹配得到的流场用作训练阶段的对齐信号，而非推理阶段的传输模型。该流场引导源侧表示对齐到固定目标空间，使表示既保留源—目标配对关系，也与目标空间的整体结构相协调。论文还从理论上分析其作用机制，指出端点精度控制与局部目标空间几何建模有助于改善配对对应关系。

Result: 在合成数据、配对单细胞多组学基准，以及图像—文本对齐任务上的实验表明，该方法能够提升实例级对应效果，同时保留目标空间结构，并适用于不同模态设置。

Conclusion: 流场可作为有效的训练期几何引导信号，弥补仅依赖相似度、分布匹配或逐点回归的不足。该方法在兼顾配对关系与目标空间结构方面取得了实验支持，但摘要未提供具体量化指标或与各基线的比较幅度。

Abstract: Many multimodal alignment problems require mapping a source modality into a fixed space defined by a target modality, where the target space is not merely an intermediate embedding but is used directly for downstream analysis. In this setting, successful alignment should preserve both the global structure of the target distribution and the instance-level correspondence observed in paired source--target training data. Existing objectives typically address only part of this goal: contrastive losses encourage relative similarity, distribution-matching losses improve marginal agreement, and pointwise regression fits each source sample to its paired target without explicitly modeling target-space path geometry. We propose FlowAlign, a flow-guided target-space alignment method that repurposes conditional flow matching as a training-time alignment signal rather than an inference-time transport model. FlowAlign uses the learned flow field to guide the alignment of source-side representations with the fixed target space, encouraging them to preserve paired correspondence while remaining compatible with the global target-space structure. We further provide theoretical analysis showing how FlowAlign helps improve paired correspondence by controlling endpoint accuracy and capturing local target-space geometry. Experiments on synthetic data, paired single-cell multiome benchmarks, and image--text alignment demonstrate that FlowAlign improves instance level correspondence while preserving target-space structure across different modality settings.

</details>


### [256] [On the Reliability of Majority-Vote Pseudo-Labels in Test-Time Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09872)
*Haoyu Wang,Ru Wang,Yuanyuan Wang,Jiaxian Guo,Mingming Gong*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文研究测试时强化学习中自生成多数投票标签的可靠性，并提出按提示词可靠性调整训练权重的方法，以降低采样波动带来的负面影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试时强化学习可利用目标领域的无标注数据适配预训练语言模型，但训练依赖模型自行生成的伪标签，其可靠性尚不清楚。多数投票监督的误差既来自总体多数答案可能有偏，也来自有限次生成造成的提示词相关波动。总体偏差通常无法在缺少外部监督时识别，因此论文聚焦于缓解有限采样波动对后续训练的影响。

Method: 论文分析在线直接偏好优化中多数投票监督的误差，并提出提示词条件分布鲁棒直接偏好优化：根据观测到的投票可靠性，对同一提示词下的训练样本进行稳健重加权，使对采样结果敏感的提示词产生更保守的参数更新。此外，论文引入基于缓存的机制，以减少在线适配过程中的自监督遗忘。

Result: 在六个数学与知识密集型推理基准、五种模型骨干组成的三十种评测设置中，该方法有二十四种设置达到或超过最强测试时强化学习基线。

Conclusion: 研究表明，有限生成次数导致的提示词级可靠性差异会影响伪标签训练效果；利用投票可靠性进行条件化稳健重加权，并辅以缓存机制，可提升测试时适配的稳定性与竞争力。不过，该方法主要缓解采样波动，无法解决缺乏外部监督时难以识别的总体多数答案偏差。

Abstract: Test-time reinforcement learning (TTRL) adapts pretrained language models to unlabeled target-domain data using self-generated pseudo-labels, whose reliability remains poorly understood. We analyze majority-vote supervision for TTRL with online Direct Preference Optimization (DPO) and decompose its error into population-majority bias and prompt-dependent finite-rollout variation. Because population-majority bias is generally unidentifiable without external supervision, we focus on mitigating the downstream effects of finite-rollout variation. We propose \emph{Prompt-Conditional Distributionally Robust DPO (PC-Dr.DPO)}, which conditions within-prompt robust reweighting on observed vote reliability to make updates from sampling-sensitive prompts more conservative. We further introduce a cache-based mechanism to reduce self-supervised forgetting during online adaptation. Across six mathematical and knowledge-intensive reasoning benchmarks and five model backbones, PC-Dr.DPO matches or outperforms the strongest TTRL baseline in 24 of 30 settings.

</details>


### [257] [CPU-ARC: Agentic Reasoning Caching for on-CPU Reasoning Reuse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09891)
*Peter Belcak,Greg Heinrich,Saurav Muralidharan,Shizhe Diao,Pavlo Molchanov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出一种推理缓存方法，将大型语言模型生成的推理过程转化为可在中央处理器上执行的智能体程序，在多类数学和科学任务中以更低成本达到与先进模型相当的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型往往把解决问题与执行解法混为一谈，因此面对每个新实例都要从头推理，造成重复计算和高昂成本。论文希望将已经获得的解题方法复用于新实例，减少对语言模型的依赖。

Method: 作者提出智能体推理缓存：从大型语言模型的推理轨迹中构建稳健、可在中央处理器上运行的推理缓存。缓存把已推导出的解题方法用于处理新问题实例，使执行阶段无需再次进行大量语言模型推理。

Result: 在多种高难度数学与科学任务上，推理缓存的表现与先进大型语言模型相当；其具备可解释、可持续训练、能抵抗输入扰动等特点，调用成本比生成缓存所用的源模型低十六至二百九十一倍。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，语言模型完成一次复杂推理后，可将所得方法转化为高效、低成本且可解释的可执行缓存，用于解决后续同类问题，从而避免反复从头推理。

Abstract: Large language models reinvent the wheel all the time. They conflate task-solving with the execution of the solutions, which forces them to solve every problem from scratch. We show that once a task has been reasoned through, the application of its solution to a new problem instance can be done very efficiently and with little involvement from language models. We introduce agentic reasoning caching, a method for constructing robust CPU-executable agentic reasoning caches (ARCs) from LLM reasoning traces. ARCs show on-par performance with frontier LLMs across a range of difficult mathematical and scientific tasks, are fully explainable, continually trainable, robust to input perturbations, and 16-291x cheaper to invoke than the source model.

</details>


<div id='cs.IR'></div>

# cs.IR [[Back]](#toc)

### [258] [Improving Contextual Retrieval for Long Documents using Semantic Chunking and Hybrid Retrieval Strategies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08498)
*Sakshi Garg,William B. Andreopoulos*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种结合知识图谱与稠密向量检索的混合检索增强生成框架，用于提升跨文档、多跳问题的检索与回答能力；在多跳检索数据集上优于单独使用向量或图谱的基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇且分散的文档中，答案往往需要整合多个来源的证据。传统检索增强生成系统在多跳推理和保持上下文连贯性方面存在不足，容易出现上下文碎片化。研究旨在结合知识图谱的结构化关系推理能力与向量检索的广泛语义覆盖能力，以更可靠地处理复杂问题。

Method: 框架采用多阶段流程，先通过基于变换器的摘要生成和语义分块改善上下文保留，再同时构建用于表达实体间显式关系的动态图谱和稠密向量索引。推理时采用分层检索机制，优先通过图谱遍历进行精确检索；若图谱检索不足，则回退到向量检索。

Result: 在多跳检索数据集上的评估显示，该混合方法优于单独使用向量检索或知识图谱的基线，在精确匹配得分和复杂查询的检索准确率方面均有所提升。摘要未提供具体数值或统计显著性信息。

Conclusion: 将结构化关系推理与语义向量检索结合，可增强系统处理跨来源、多跳知识密集型问题的能力。该结果支持混合检索作为缓解传统检索增强生成系统上下文碎片化和推理不足的一种方案。

Abstract: The vast amount of information available in lengthy, distributed documents makes finding relevant answers difficult, especially when the answer requires connecting evidence from multiple sources. Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) systems often struggle with this type of "multi-hop" reasoning and frequently face issues with context fragmentation. In this paper, we propose a hybrid RAG framework that integrates the structured reasoning of knowledge graphs with the broad semantic coverage of dense vector retrieval. Our approach employs a multi-stage pipeline using transformer-based summarization and semantic chunking to improve context retention. We construct a dynamic knowledge graph to capture explicit relationships between entities while simultaneously maintaining a dense vector index. During inference, the system uses a hierarchical mechanism that prioritizes graph traversal for precision and falls back to vector retrieval when necessary. We evaluated our framework on the MultiHop-RAG dataset. The results show that this hybrid approach outperforms standalone vector and graph-based baselines, achieving higher Exact Match scores and improved retrieval accuracy for complex queries. This confirms that merging structured reasoning with standard vector retrieval creates a more robust system for handling complex knowledge-intensive questions.

</details>


### [259] [RedMall: Generative Recommendation for Large-Scale E-commerce](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08519)
*Kunyang Zhou,Xin Jia,Yi Yang,Yongjun Yang,Manjie Xu,Honglei Zhang,Yan Zhu,Kai Zuo,Xu Tang,Zheng Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出面向大规模电商的生成式推荐方法，通过层次化语义标识、会话建模及结合用户反馈与排序目标的优化，提升推荐效果；离线和线上实验均显示其优于对比方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式推荐存在三方面不足：将语义标识视为一维序列，忽略不同层级间的依赖；仅用下一词预测，难以直接优化整个会话的推荐效果；强化学习虽能对齐用户偏好，却较少明确优化会话中正向物品的排序。

Method: 该方法基于多模态表示和残差量化构建层次化语义标识，并设计轻量编码器建模其层间依赖。模型结合稀疏专家混合结构、双门控注意力和多目标预测，以扩大模型规模、增强用户兴趣表示并优化会话推荐；同时通过曝光感知损失，将已曝光但未点击的物品作为负反馈。优化阶段将组相对策略优化与辅助排序损失结合，利用真实用户反馈和物品排序共同引导推荐。

Result: 在亚马逊美妆、玩具、食品及电影镜头一百万数据集上，该方法在所有报告的召回率和归一化折损累计增益指标上均优于对比基线。线上测试中，电商广告的广告主价值提升百分之二点二四、点击率提升百分之零点五九；电商推荐的商品交易总额提升百分之一点零八、日活跃购买者提升百分之零点九五。

Conclusion: 研究将层次化语义建模、会话级推荐优化与排序导向的策略学习整合到生成式推荐框架中，并在多个公开数据集及线上业务测试中验证了其有效性，表明该方案有助于提升电商推荐质量与业务指标。

Abstract: Generative recommendation has shown considerable potential in industrial recommendation systems. However, the performance of existing generative methods is limited by several challenges. 1) Semantic IDs (SIDs) are treated as one-dimensional sequences, overlooking the hierarchical dependencies among different layers. 2) Next-token prediction used is unable to explicitly optimize session-wise recommendation. 3) Although reinforcement learning has been applied to align generated recommendations with user preferences, few existing methods explicitly optimize the ranking of positive items in session-wise recommendation. To address the above issues, we propose RedMall, a generative recommender designed for large-scale e-commerce. Firstly, RedMall constructs hierarchical SIDs through residual quantization over multimodal representations. Secondly, RedMall introduces a lightweight SID encoder to explicitly model layer-wise dependencies in SIDs. Moreover, RedMall incorporates sparse mixture-of-experts, dual gated attention, and multi-target prediction to scale model size, enhance user interest representation, and optimize session-wise recommendation. We further design an exposure-aware loss that treats exposed but unclicked items as negative feedback. To better align user preferences and list-wise ranking objectives, we combine Group Relative Policy Optimization with an auxiliary LambdaRank loss, using both real user feedback and item ranking to guide optimization. Experiments on four public datasets-Amazon Beauty, Amazon Toys, Amazon Food, and MovieLens-1M---show that RedMall outperforms the evaluated baselines across all reported Recall and NDCG metrics. Online A/B tests show improvements of 2.24% in advertiser value (ADVV) and 0.59% in click-through rate (CTR) for e-commerce advertising, as well as gains of 1.08% in GMV and 0.95% in daily active buyers (DAB) for e-commerce recommendations.

</details>


### [260] [CascadeRec: Deployed Adaptive LLM Compute Allocation for Multi-Tenant Content Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08566)
*Kai Zhao,Guilin Zhang,Xiang Cheng,Xiquan Cui,Henry Heng,Xu Chu,Alet Blanken*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: CascadeRec采用两级大语言模型推荐重排方案，仅将预计能从额外推理中获益的请求升级处理；线上实验显示，在未观察到质量下降的情况下，推理成本约降低百分之十。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对每次推荐请求都使用昂贵的大语言模型会带来较高成本；此外，交互历史稀疏的用户未必能从复杂重排中获益，甚至可能导致推荐质量下降。论文旨在以按需升级的方式，在控制成本的同时避免对特定用户群体造成损害。

Method: 系统采用两级级联架构：轻量模型先重排所有请求，路由器再判断哪些请求可能受益于额外计算，并将其升级至更昂贵的处理流程，例如自一致性采样、多路径推理或更大规模的模型。升级策略依据用户分群进行门控。作者在企业生产环境中开展线上测试，并使用公开推荐数据集进行分群评估，以配对自助法计算置信区间；同时介绍部署架构、租户负载不均衡处理及模型输出解析失败的服务端应对方式。

Result: 生产环境持续一个多月的对照实验表明，相较于统一使用昂贵模型，级联方案将推理成本降低约百分之十，延迟影响较小，迄今未观察到推荐质量回退。公开数据集的分群结果显示，统一使用大语言模型重排会使交互历史稀疏用户的前十名归一化折损累计增益最多下降百分之三十；显著收益仅出现在历史最长的用户群体。升级冷启动用户甚至可能扩大质量损失，而适合升级的用户群体随领域而异。

Conclusion: 大语言模型重排不宜对所有请求一视同仁。基于用户群体和领域特征进行选择性升级，有助于控制推理成本并降低质量风险；在实际部署中，还需考虑租户负载差异及输出解析故障。论文报告的线上质量结论仍受限于实验时长和无法公开租户数据等因素。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are attractive rerankers for recommendation. Running an expensive model on every request is costly, and for users with sparse interaction histories it often lowers quality. We present CascadeRec, a two-tier LLM reranking cascade deployed in production at a large financial planning and analysis (FP&A) SaaS provider. It serves content recommendations to enterprise tenants on AWS EKS and GKE. A lightweight Tier-1 model reranks every request. A router escalates only the requests where extra inference-time compute is likely to help, and sends them to a more expensive Tier-2 procedure (self-consistency sampling, multi-path reasoning, or a larger model). The system has run in a production A/B experiment for over a month. Compared with uniform use of the expensive tier, CascadeRec has reduced inference cost by about 10%, with minimal latency impact and no quality regression observed to date. Tenant data cannot be released, so we also evaluate the pipeline on public benchmarks (MovieLens-1M and Amazon-Electronics). Segment-level analysis with paired-bootstrap confidence intervals shows that uniform LLM reranking lowers NDCG@10 for sparse-history users by up to 30%, and its only statistically significant gain appears on the segment with the longest histories. Escalating cold users can make the loss larger instead of repairing it. Which segments benefit from escalation depends on the domain, and this motivates the segment-gated rollout we use in production. We describe the deployment architecture, the measured benefits, and lessons learned, including inter-tenant load imbalance as a driver for cascading and the handling of LLM output-parsing failures at the serving layer.

</details>


### [261] [SteerGR: Steering Generative Recommendation via Sparse Autoencoders for Controllable Game Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08790)
*Sunkyung Lee,Xiaokai Wei,Da Long,Hyunsik Jeon,Jiajun Wu,Rithwik Seth,Jongwuk Lee,Michelle Gong,Gungor Polatkan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于稀疏自编码器的可控生成式推荐框架，可通过调节内部特征，使游戏推荐更符合或避开指定概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐能够依据用户交互历史预测物品标识，但难以直接控制推荐物品的属性。核心挑战是将人类可理解的概念与模型内部特征对应起来，并找到能够有效改变推荐结果的特征。

Method: 框架分为三个阶段：首先借助大语言模型定义并落地游戏玩法概念；随后根据特征与概念的关联发现候选特征，并依据其对推荐结果的引导效果选出目标特征；最后放大或抑制该特征，在不重新训练推荐模型的情况下调整推荐结果。

Result: 在真实游戏数据集上的实验使用了两种工业级生成式推荐模型。结果表明，选出的内部特征能够使推荐结果朝目标概念靠近，或远离该概念。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了利用稀疏自编码器实现推荐属性控制的可行性，并将基于稀疏自编码器的引导方法从大语言模型拓展至工业级生成式推荐，为可控推荐研究提供了初步探索。

Abstract: Generative recommendation aims to predict item identifiers from user interaction histories, but explicit control over the properties of recommended items remains underexplored. A key challenge is to connect human-readable concepts to internal features whose interventions effectively change the recommended items. To address this, we propose Steerable Generative Recommender (SteerGR), a framework that uses sparse autoencoders for concept-level control in game discovery. SteerGR proceeds through three stages: (i) Concept Definition and Grounding for defining play concepts with an LLM; (ii) Feature Discovery and Selection for discovering candidate features by their concept associations and selecting one by its steering effect; and (iii) Sparse Feature Intervention for amplifying or suppressing selected features to steer recommendations without retraining the recommender. Extensive experiments with two industrial generative recommendation models on a real-world game dataset show that the selected features successfully steer recommendations toward or away from target concepts. We believe this work takes a first step to extend SAE-based steering from LLMs to industrial generative recommendation.

</details>


### [262] [Generative Search and Recommendation at Shopify](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08804)
*Juhana Kangaspunta,Khalil Hajji,Darshan Fofadiya,Kyumin Park,Michael Garner,Anthony Maltsev,Ryan Synk,Jose Sepulveda,Joshua Crisologo,Shuying Sun,Akshay Soni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种以语义标识符生成产品的统一检索方法，在提升搜索与推荐效果的同时满足大规模线上服务的时延要求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电商搜索和推荐需要从不断变化、规模达数十亿商品的目录中快速找到相关产品。传统方案依赖多个任务专用模型，难以兼顾统一表示、检索质量与服务效率，因此需要更可扩展的产品发现方法。

Method: 该方法将搜索与推荐都建模为生成由商品内容衍生的短语义标识符，并联合利用商品目录数据和用户行为数据进行预训练，使两个任务共享表示和训练目标。论文进一步通过强化学习提升搜索相关性，通过任务专家蒸馏改善推荐效果，并结合多词元预测与轻量级排序器提高检索质量、降低时延。

Result: 作为独立搜索系统，该方法的效果可与现有多模型搜索系统相当，并满足生产环境的时延要求。接入线上搜索和推荐系统后，离线评估与在线对照实验均显示显著收益。专家蒸馏提升了推荐质量，且未检测到搜索质量的统计显著损失；多词元预测与轻量级排序器也优于自回归基线，同时降低时延。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于语义标识符生成的统一方法能够在大规模商品目录中兼顾搜索与推荐质量及服务效率，具有实际部署价值。联合训练、任务专家蒸馏和高效解码等设计可进一步增强其生产应用效果。

Abstract: Search and recommendation at Shopify require retrieving relevant products from a changing catalog of billions of products under strict latency constraints. We present SID-LLM, which casts both tasks as generating semantic IDs: short sequences of content-derived tokens representing products. We jointly pre-train SID-LLM on catalog and behavioral data, giving search and recommendation a shared representation and training objective. We show that reinforcement learning further improves judged search relevance. Furthermore, distillation from task-specific experts improves recommendation quality over joint supervised fine-tuning without a statistically detected loss in search quality. Finally, for efficient serving, we pair multi-token prediction with a lightweight ranker, improving retrieval quality over our autoregressive baseline while reducing latency. We evaluate SID-LLM both as a standalone retriever and as a component of Shopify's production search and recommendation systems. As a standalone search system, SID-LLM achieves quality comparable to Shopify's existing multi-model search stack while meeting production latency requirements. Integrating SID-LLM into the search and recommendation stacks provides significant gains in both offline evaluations and online A/B tests. Together, these results demonstrate the practical value of semantic-ID generation as a unified approach to product discovery at scale.

</details>


### [263] [SkinMind: Equipping Agentic RAG with Human-Inspired Graph Probing for Reliable Dermatology AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08935)
*Jing Hao,Weinan Zhou,Zhihong Lu,Yaling Shen,Yiming Xu,Yefeng Liu,Siyuan Yan,Shuaibo Li,Ming Dai,Junjun He,Zuyi Zhu,Jinjie Gu,Lijun Wu,Jian Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向皮肤病学的多模态、智能体式检索增强框架，通过逐步探查知识图谱来获取文本、关系和图像证据，提升视觉问答表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可靠的皮肤病学人工智能需要能够查证分散于指南、教材及图像中的证据。传统检索增强生成通常只进行一次检索，难以灵活调整检索范围、追踪多跳关系，也不善于整合多模态临床证据。

Method: 研究构建了一个由协调器与图环境迭代交互的检索框架，并设计八种分层图探查工具，使模型能够逐步识别相关实体、查看节点信息、遍历关系与路径，并获取原文及视觉证据。模型据此自主选择检索模态、探索方向和证据粒度，形成与问题相关的证据子图。配套知识图谱依据皮肤科专家参与制定的模式构建，并采用冲突解决机制，整合皮肤病学指南和教材文本以及大量图像。

Result: 知识图谱包含超过一百万个节点和两百万条边，融合文本、关系与视觉证据。在六个皮肤病学视觉问答基准及两种策略模型设置中，相较于直接作答，该方法分别使平均得分提高八点七零分和八点八九分，并持续优于其他检索增强方法。

Conclusion: 该研究表明，借助分层图探查进行自主、渐进式证据检索，有助于提升皮肤病学多模态推理的证据支撑能力。所提出的框架及配套知识图谱为构建可核验的皮肤病学人工智能提供了有效基础。

Abstract: Reliable dermatology AI requires access to verifiable evidence distributed across heterogeneous textual and visual sources. However, conventional retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) typically relies on one-shot retrieval from flat corpora, limiting its ability to adapt retrieval granularity, explore multi-hop relations, and integrate multimodal clinical evidence. We introduce SkinMind, a multimodal agentic RAG framework that equips policy models with human-inspired graph probing for autonomous evidence seeking. SkinMind organizes retrieval as an iterative interaction between an orchestrator and a graph environment. Through eight hierarchical graph-probing tools, the orchestrator progressively identifies relevant entities, inspects node-level information, traverses relations and paths, and retrieves source-text and visual evidence. This progressive-disclosure design allows the model to adaptively determine the retrieval modality, exploration direction, and evidence granularity while constructing a query-specific evidence subgraph. To support autonomous graph probing, we construct SkinMind-KG from a 62M-token corpus of dermatology guidelines and textbooks and 423K images, guided by a dermatologist-informed schema and a conflict-resolution mechanism. The resulting graph contains over 1.0M nodes and 2.0M edges, unifying textual, relational, and visual evidence for retrieval. Across six dermatology VQA benchmarks and two policy-model settings, SkinMind improves the average score over direct answering by 8.70 and 8.89 points with GPT-5.4-mini and Gemini-2.5-Flash, respectively, while consistently outperforming competing RAG methods. Together, SkinMind and SkinMind-KG provide an effective foundation for evidence-grounded multimodal reasoning in dermatology.

</details>


### [264] [Rethinking Latent Reasoning for Generative Recommendation: Beyond Teacher-Generated CoT and Recurrent Hidden-State Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09006)
*Yiwen Chen,Fuwei Zhang,Zehao Chen,Deqing Wang,Hehan Li,Peizhi Xu,Hanmeng Liu,Shuanglong Li,Xin Pei,Fuzhen Zhuang,Zhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种无需教师链式推理标注或循环反馈的新方法：从用户已观察到的物品历史中构造语义监督，并在单次预填充计算中输入思维令牌；该方法在四个推荐基准上优于对比基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐中的潜在推理面临两项实际障碍：构造教师生成的链式推理监督成本高，循环式隐藏状态反馈则需要专门的执行流程。作者进一步分析推理轨迹后发现，只有历史分析步骤平均而言同时有助于下一物品预测和推理轨迹连贯性，后续偏好与意图步骤对预测答案的平均贡献为负，因此有必要重新审视监督来源与计算方式。

Method: 提出一种预填充、历史信息驱动的思维令牌方法。它聚合缓存的稀疏物品编码，从用户观察到的物品历史中直接提取语义目标，并以此监督不同词表思维令牌对应的上下文状态。所有思维令牌作为普通输入标识一次性输入因果预填充过程，不再依赖循环式的最终状态反馈。训练仅使用监督微调。

Result: 在四个推荐基准上，该方法均优于所比较的基线。受控消融实验支持输入匹配的语义监督具有价值；成本测量显示，目标构造速度快，训练时间与仅使用思维令牌的推荐基线相近。

Conclusion: 研究表明，推荐潜在推理不必依赖昂贵的教师推理轨迹或复杂的循环执行机制。利用物品历史构造与输入相匹配的语义监督，并通过单次预填充处理思维令牌，能够以相近训练成本提升推荐表现。

Abstract: Latent reasoning in generative recommendation faces two practical barriers: constructing teacher-generated chain-of-thought (CoT) supervision is costly, and recurrent hidden-state feedback requires a specialized execution loop. We revisit both the supervision source and the computation interface. A step-level diagnosis measures how recommendation CoT supports next-item prediction and trace coherence. In the analyzed traces, history analysis is the only step with positive mean contributions on both dimensions, while later preference and intent steps have negative mean answer contributions. Guided by this finding, we propose PHiT (Prefilled, History-Informed Thought Tokens), which learns intermediate states from semantic targets extracted directly from observed item histories. PHiT aggregates cached sparse item encodings to construct these targets and supervises the contextual states of distinct vocabulary thought tokens. All thought tokens are supplied together as ordinary input IDs during a single causal prefill, removing the need for recurrent final-state feedback. With supervised fine-tuning alone, PHiT outperforms the compared baselines across four recommendation benchmarks. Controlled ablations support the value of input-matched semantic supervision, while cost measurements show fast target construction with training time comparable to the recommendation-only thought-token baseline.

</details>


### [265] [MV-IVF: Multi-Vector Retrieval via Multi-Vector Clustering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09040)
*Kishen N Gowda,Laxman Dhulipala,Lars Gottesbüren,Rajesh Jayaram*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种直接面向多向量数据构建的倒排文件索引，通过点云聚类树与优先束搜索，加快多向量检索，并在延迟、吞吐量和索引开销方面取得良好表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多向量检索以多个嵌入表示查询和文档，并通过最大相似度等细粒度集合交互衡量相关性，通常比单向量检索更准确，但计算开销更高。既有方案多依赖代理表示或先筛选再重排，将候选生成简化为单向量搜索。本文探索互补方向：直接为多向量数据建立原生倒排文件索引。

Method: 论文提出多向量倒排文件索引，并设计一种基于切弗距离目标的点云聚类算法。其关键在于：点云分配到聚类后，中心更新可转化为对该簇内展平嵌入进行标准单向量聚类，从而高效构建层次化索引树，且内部节点存储点云中心。查询时采用优先束搜索选择可能相关的叶簇，再对其中的文档打分；同时结合低比特量化、查询压缩和缓存友好的批量搜索加速。

Result: 在多个文本检索基准上，该方法展现出较强的延迟—召回率权衡，并取得最佳批量吞吐量前沿，同时具有较有利的建索引时间和索引体积。在召回率十指标达到百分之九十时，相比基线，单次查询延迟最高降低八点零三倍，查询吞吐量最高提升一百七十三点五倍。

Conclusion: 研究表明，无需将多向量检索退化为单向量候选生成，也能通过原生倒排文件索引实现高效搜索。该方法兼顾检索质量与系统效率，说明基于点云聚类的层次索引是扩展多向量检索的一条实用路径。

Abstract: Multi-vector retrieval represents each query and document as a set of embeddings and scores relevance via fine-grained set-to-set interactions such as MaxSim (Chamfer similarity). This expressiveness improves retrieval quality over single-vector representations, but makes search substantially more expensive. Existing systems address this challenge primarily through proxy representations or filter-and-rerank pipelines that reduce candidate generation to single-vector search. We study the complementary direction of building a native IVF-style index directly over multi-vector data.
  We introduce MV-IVF, an inverted-file index for multi-vector retrieval built on MV-Lloyd, a new Lloyd-style algorithm for clustering point clouds under a Chamfer objective. Our key observation is that once point clouds are assigned to clusters, the center-update step reduces to standard single-vector clustering over the flattened embeddings in each cluster. This enables efficient construction of a hierarchical IVF tree with center point clouds at internal nodes.
  At query time, MV-IVF performs best-first beam search over the tree to identify promising leaf clusters, then scores the documents in those leaves. We further accelerate search using low-bit quantization, query compression, and cache-efficient batched search. We implement MV-IVF in MVSIC, an optimized C++ library and unified evaluation framework for multi-vector retrieval that includes several major approaches. Across BEIR and LoTTE text retrieval benchmarks, MV-IVF achieves strong latency--recall tradeoffs, the best batched throughput frontiers, and favorable build-time and index-size profiles. At $90\%$ Recall-$10$@$10$, MV-IVF achieves up to $8.03\times$ lower single-query latency and up to $173.5\times$ higher QPS than the baselines, demonstrating that native IVF-style indexing is practical and effective for scalable multi-vector retrieval.

</details>


### [266] [PAGSL: Path-Augmenting Graph Structure Learning for explainable scholar recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09126)
*Jiale Xu,Mingshu Ai,Junjie Huang,Tao Jia*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出路径增强图结构学习模型，在提升学者推荐准确性的同时，生成简洁且有信息量的路径解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释学者推荐方法容易受到优化噪声和枢纽节点偏差影响，产生浅显、缺乏层次性与个性化的解释。论文旨在兼顾推荐效果与解释质量。

Method: 模型通过图增强激活学者及相关实体之间潜在的学术路径，并利用基于路径的神经网络编码路径信息以进行推荐；同时设计结合路径评分优化的掩码机制，筛选出简洁且有意义的解释。模型还采用异质卷积和路径表示学习。

Result: 在学者网、学术网和研究之门三个数据集上进行实验，数据覆盖约四万九千名作者及三十三万余条交互记录。与先进基线相比，模型的调和平均值、准确率和曲线下面积分别提升百分之六点三一、百分之六点二九和百分之五点九五。消融实验也验证了异质卷积与路径表示学习的有效性；论文还通过定量结果和可视化展示了解释效果。

Conclusion: 路径增强图结构学习能够结合学术语义路径与图表示学习，在提高推荐准确性的同时提供可解释结果，展现出兼顾推荐性能与解释性的潜力。

Abstract: Explainable scholar recommendation aims to identify potential scholars of interest and provide reasonable explanations for the recommendations. However, existing frameworks struggle with optimization-induced noise and "hub node bias", yielding trivial explanations that lack hierarchical depth and personalization. To this end, we propose a novel explainable scholar recommendation model, Path-Augmenting Graph Structure Learning (named PAGSL). PAGSL combines representation learning and semantic path knowledge to ensure recommendation performance while providing interpretable results from two perspectives. Specifically, it activates latent academic paths among entities through graph augmentation and employs a path-based neural network to encode these paths for recommendation. A masking mechanism with path score optimization is designed to generate concise and informative explanations. We evaluate PAGSL on three academic datasets (Scholat, AMiner, and ResearchGate), comprising approximately 49,000 authors and over 330,000 interaction records. Experimental evaluations validate that PAGSL outperforms state-of-the-art baselines, achieving significant improvements of 6.31\% in F1-score, 6.29\% in Accuracy and 5.95\% in AUC for recommendation. Crucially, in terms of explainability, we provide quantitative results and visualization effects. Furthermore, ablation studies validate the effectiveness of our heterogeneous convolution and path representation learning. PAGSL achieves strong performance in both recommendation accuracy and explainability.

</details>


### [267] [Structure and Lineage Evidence for Vietnamese Statutory Article Retrieval on DRiLL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09200)
*Duc-Vu Nguyen,Ngan Luu-Thuy Nguyen*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 研究改进了越南法定条文检索：官方Ｆ₂从百分之七十一点七七升至百分之七十二点九一，新增特征对决策树训练目标下降的贡献为百分之十八点二；结果属于描述性发现，不能据此确认单项特征的独立效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 法条检索需要识别条文的具体身份与局部形式，并利用同一法律文件、引用关系及修订沿革等证据。研究旨在探索这些信息能否提升越南法定条文检索表现，尤其关注涉及两条法条的问题。

Method: 研究在越南法条检索基准上比较不同候选共享方式、列表式通义千问证据和学习器设置。所用特征涵盖条文身份与局部形式、同法支持、引用及沿革证据；另以决策树训练目标的下降量衡量新增特征对分裂收益的贡献，并在五个随机种子下评估。

Result: 五个随机种子的实验中，官方Ｆ₂由百分之七十一点七七提升至百分之七十二点九一；最大的描述性增益出现在需要检索两条法条的问题。新增特征占所提选择器总决策树分裂收益的百分之十八点二，主要来自同法和引用证据。案例分析展示了基础条文与修订条文、配套条文找回、干扰项移除等情形，也揭示了部分失败案例。

Conclusion: 结果支持进一步研究同法、引用和沿革等证据在法条检索中的作用，并为扩展到其他数据集、表征方式和学习器提供动机。但研究未能确立单项特征的独立因果效果，也不意味着必须采用特定学习器、手工评分或显式图结构。

Abstract: We study Vietnamese statutory Article retrieval on DRiLL using features for provision identity and local form, same-law support, and citation and lineage evidence. Across five random seeds, selectors sharing candidates, listwise Qwen evidence, and learner settings improve official $F_2$ from 71.77% to 72.91%, with the largest descriptive gain on two-Article questions. Added features account for 18.20% of the proposed selector's total decision-tree split gain, measured as training-objective reduction, mainly from same-law and citation evidence. Cases reveal base provisions and amendments, companion recovery, distractor removal, and failures. These findings motivate research on other datasets, representations, and learners without establishing individual feature effects or requiring LightGBM, hand-crafted scores, or explicit graphs.

</details>


### [268] [Orthogonal Norm Growth in Two-Tower Retrieval: A Popularity Mechanism for Unified Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09294)
*Andrei Atamaniuk*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文研究共享物品表示在搜索与推荐训练中反复更新时，向量范数增长如何影响未归一化内积检索，并通过理论分析和受控实验考察这一现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一搜索与推荐系统可能利用两类交互数据共同更新物品向量。若服务阶段采用未归一化内积，向量范数增长可能使高频物品获得更高排名与更多曝光。论文希望厘清余弦损失、参数更新与实际物品向量移动之间的关系，并审慎评估这种影响。

Method: 首先分析余弦损失产生的输出梯度：它与当前物品向量垂直，但由于训练更新的是编码器参数，实际向量移动不必然保持垂直。论文推导了实际移动保持切向的充分条件；在这些条件下，每次非零移动都会使范数平方增加，增量等于移动长度的平方。作者强调，这只是有条件的结论，并非联合随机梯度下降的一般定理。随后，在电影评分三千二百万条数据集和亚马逊图书数据集上进行受控消融实验；还研究了结合物品标识向量与共享内容编码的非线性塔式模型，分解范数变化来源，并通过匹配训练干预去除标识向量更新造成的即时范数增长。

Result: 实验显示，累积的近切向移动伴随物品频率与向量范数的关联；采用未归一化内积排序时，高频头部物品获得更多曝光，而对物品向量归一化后，这种额外曝光有所不同。在非线性塔式模型中，活跃物品的移动仍近似切向。去除标识向量更新导致的即时范数增长后，范数相关曝光下降，但推荐效用也随之降低。

Conclusion: 共享物品表示在多任务或多轮更新下可能累积范数增长，并经未归一化内积检索转化为头部物品曝光偏差。论文提供了有条件的理论解释与共享表示审计方法；其干预实验是诊断工具，而非已验证的缓解方案。由于实验日志仅来自推荐场景，这一现象在搜索与推荐联合流量中的普遍程度仍有待研究。

Abstract: Unified search and recommendation systems may update the same item vectors from both interaction streams. We study how repeated updates can increase an item vector's norm and affect retrieval under an unnormalized dot product. A cosine-only loss makes the output gradient perpendicular to the current item vector - tangent to its constant-norm sphere. Training changes encoder parameters, however, so the realized item-vector movement need not remain tangent. We derive sufficient conditions under which it does; every nonzero update then increases squared norm by its squared length. When the tasks share an item encoder, sequential updates from both can accumulate in the same norm, which raw-inner-product serving can expose for either task. This conditional result is not a theorem about joint SGD. Controlled ablations test these conditions. On MovieLens 32M and Amazon Books, accumulated tangent movement accompanies frequency-norm association and additional head-item exposure under raw-dot rather than normalized-item ranking. In a nonlinear tower combining a per-item ID vector with a shared content encoding, active item movement remains near-tangent. We separate its norm change into the ID-update contribution and other output changes, then use a matched training intervention that removes immediate norm growth caused by the ID-row update. The intervention reduces norm-related exposure but also recommendation utility, so it is diagnostic rather than a proposed mitigation. Our logs are recommendation-only: the formulation supplies a shared-representation audit, while prevalence in joint traffic remains open.

</details>


### [269] [SPARSEUP: Towards Better Recipes for Training Modern Learned Sparse Retrievers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09680)
*Thibault Formal,Pierre Erbacher,Cosimo Rulli*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一套改进稀疏词汇编码器训练效果与效率的方案，并训练出在公开词汇型稀疏检索模型中表现领先的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式稀疏嵌入是稠密检索的替代方案，但其表现落后于单向量与迟交互模型，且训练过程难以稳定收敛，限制了实际应用。论文旨在改善训练稳定性、模型稀疏度及检索效果之间的权衡。

Method: 作者分析对数几率分布对训练稳定性和最终稀疏度的影响，并提出逐位置扇出硬上限，限制每个位置可激活的词汇维度数量。此外，针对区分大小写的字节对编码骨干网络，采用词汇合并策略，去除大小写或词边界变体等冗余词项；再将整套方案应用于相应骨干网络与训练数据。

Result: 据摘要报告，所得模型在“深度评测基准十三项”上的平均前十名归一化折损累计增益为56.4；在参数量低于1.5亿的公开词汇型稀疏编码器中，达到作者所知的最高成绩。

Conclusion: 通过调控对数几率分布、限制每个位置的词汇输出数量并合并冗余词项，可以提升类似稀疏词汇编码器的训练稳定性与效率，并取得有竞争力的检索效果。

Abstract: Learned sparse embeddings are a powerful alternative to dense retrieval, yet they have fallen behind single-vector and late interaction models. One reason for their limited adoption is that they are notoriously difficult to train, generally requiring very tight conditions to converge. We present a recipe for training Splade-like encoders on modern backbones and data that achieves a better efficiency-effectiveness trade-off. We discuss the role of logit distribution and its impact on the training stability and the final sparsity of the encoder. We also introduce a new per-position fan-out control - a hard cap on how many vocabulary dimensions each position may emit. We further apply a simple vocabulary merging strategy that removes redundant entries - such as case and word-boundary variants of the same word - which are common in cased BPE-based backbones. We apply our recipe to the LateOn backbone and training data, obtaining SPARSEUP, a sparse encoder that reaches 56.4 average nDCG@10 on BEIR-13, the highest we know of among public vocabulary-based sparse encoders below 150M parameters.

</details>


### [270] [SAGE-PAIR: Source-Pretrained Routed Residual Adaptation for Sparse-Feedback Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.09728)
*Weiqing Zeng,Zida Yang,Guquan You,Jiexin Zheng,Cong Wang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出一种面向极少目标反馈的推荐模型适配方法：先从相邻时间段学习低维残差修正空间，再用少量目标数据校准，并通过用户画像路由不同残差专家。实验显示该方法提升了排序效果，且适配与推理成本较低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推荐系统进入新的服务周期时，往往尚未积累足够反馈来重新训练模型。目标反馈仅占百分之零点零五至百分之一时，常规微调和元学习仍需从极少样本中估计更新方向，导致更新难以可靠识别。论文据此提出，关键不只是标签数量不足，更在于待估计的更新空间是否能被少量反馈识别。

Method: 该方法采用分阶段适配：首先利用相邻来源时间段的数据，预训练一个低维、全局性的残差修正；随后加入以来源用户画像为条件的软路由器，在多个残差专家之间分配权重。目标反馈只用于校准这一已有来源支持的残差状态，而不从头重学推荐模型。

Result: 在快手搜索推荐数据集第二版的锁定测试集上，完整方法使前十位归一化折损累计增益相对提升百分之一点七七。按核心实验协议比较，用户路由专家相较于仅使用来源数据预训练的残差方法又带来百分之零点二五的提升。相同来源预训练条件下的低秩适配和适配器微调基线表现接近来源模型。论文还报告，适配与推理开销处于毫秒级。

Conclusion: 少量反馈下的有效适配，取决于更新方向是否可识别。来源数据预先学习的修正方向能为稀疏目标标签提供可估计的适配空间；基于用户画像的路由则在全局修正之上，带来稳定且因用户而异的额外修正，从而改善排序质量并控制计算成本。

Abstract: Recommendation systems often enter a new serving window before enough outcomes are available to retrain the deployed model. We study this regime with 0.05-1.0% target feedback, a range that spans our stress tests while keeping supervision far below the degrees of freedom of the source recommender. At this scale, existing fine-tuning and meta-learning methods still estimate target-specific updates from extremely sparse feedback, leaving the update direction weakly identified. SAGE-PAIR instead transfers a source-supported residual correction space and only calibrates this space using target outcomes. We introduce SAGE-PAIR, a staged adapter that pretrains a low-dimensional global residual on an adjacent source period and adds a soft router over residual experts conditioned on source user profiles. Target feedback then calibrates this residual state rather than relearning the recommender. On a locked test split of KuaiSAR-v2, SAGE-PAIR (Full) achieves a 1.77% relative improvement in NDCG@10, while a core-protocol comparison shows a further 0.25% gain from routed experts beyond source-pretrained PAIR. Matched source-pretrained LoRA and Adapter-Tuning baselines remain near the source model, supporting the need for a different adaptation space. Thus, SAGE-PAIR improves ranking quality with millisecond-scale adaptation and inference costs. Sparse-feedback adaptation is fundamentally governed by the identifiability of the update space rather than by label count alone. Source pretraining supplies estimable correction directions for scarce labels, while user-conditioned routing contributes a consistent, differentiated cross-user correction on top of the global correction. Our code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/SAGE-PAIR-610E/.

</details>
